Together, EgoAfford and EgoLens provide a foundation for jointly studying perception and planning in multi-step tabletop tasks. Our project page is available at: https://egoafford.github.io

</details>


### [86] [VoxStruct3D: Structure-Leading Flow Matching for Voxel-Space 3D MRI Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04557)
*Fang Li,Yang Gao,Shihao Zou,Weixin Si,Hongyu Wu,Qing Xia,Shuai Li,Aimin Hao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VoxStruct3D是一个直接在体素空间进行流匹配的3D MRI生成框架，通过干净数据预测目标实现全分辨率体素建模，并结合"结构优先、图像跟随"（SFIF）策略，利用冻结的3D医学编码器和StructVAE提取结构令牌作为解剖先验引导图像生成，在病理和健康T1加权脑MRI数据集上实现了最优的整体性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的3D MRI生成方法多依赖潜在扩散模型，但图像自编码器在体积数据上引入重建瓶颈，限制了最终体素级别的细粒度细节恢复。因此需要一种能够同时保证全局解剖一致性和亚体素级细节的高保真3D MRI合成方法。

Method: 1) 提出VoxStruct3D，一种体素空间的流匹配框架，采用干净数据预测目标直接建模全分辨率MRI体积；2) 设计Volumetric Voxel Generator (VVG)，包含分解式3D patch嵌入、重叠上采样、时间调制残差细化与跳跃融合，使相邻token协同重建共享体素区域并抑制patch边界伪影；3) 引入Structure-First, Image-Follows (SFIF)策略，利用冻结的预训练3D医学编码器和StructVAE提取紧凑结构令牌，通过结构领先调度（structure-leading schedule）保持结构轨迹领先于图像轨迹；4) 采用Patch-Aligned RoPE对齐不等token网格，非对称注意力实现结构到图像的单向引导。

Result: 在病理和健康T1加权脑MRI数据集上的实验表明，VoxStruct3D在特征分布对齐、样本多样性和感知质量方面均取得最强整体性能，能够生成解剖结构一致且视觉真实的体积。

Conclusion: 直接在体素空间进行流匹配并显式注入解剖结构先验，可以有效规避潜在空间重建瓶颈，实现高保真、解剖协调的3D MRI体积生成，为医学图像合成提供了新的范式。

Abstract: High-fidelity 3D MRI synthesis requires both globally coherent anatomy and fine-grained voxel-level detail. Although latent diffusion makes volumetric generation tractable, its image autoencoder introduces a reconstruction bottleneck that can limit the fine detail recoverable in the final volume. We present VoxStruct3D, a voxel-space flow-matching framework that directly models full-resolution MRI volumes using a clean-data prediction objective. Its Volumetric Voxel Generator (VVG) combines factorized 3D patch embedding with overlapping upsampling, time-modulated residual refinement, and skip fusion, enabling neighboring tokens to jointly reconstruct shared voxel regions and suppress patch-boundary artifacts. To complement direct voxel-space modeling with an explicit anatomical prior, we further introduce a Structure-First, Image-Follows (SFIF) strategy. A frozen pretrained 3D medical encoder and a StructVAE extract compact structure tokens that preserve dominant anatomy, while a structure-leading schedule keeps their trajectory ahead of the image trajectory. Patch-Aligned RoPE spatially aligns the unequal token grids, and asymmetric attention enforces one-way guidance from structure to image. Experiments on pathological and healthy T1-weighted brain MRI datasets show that VoxStruct3D achieves the strongest overall performance across feature-distribution alignment, sample diversity, and perceptual quality, producing anatomically coherent and visually realistic volumes.

</details>


### [87] [ColorFD: A Finite-Difference Guided Black-Box Physical Adversarial Attack for Remote Sensing Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04559)
*Tiannuo Guo,Guhang Qiu,Yuzhen Xie,Rui Feng,Ligang Li,Deliang Xiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ColorFD，一种基于多个纯色斑块的黑盒物理攻击方法，利用差分进化联合优化斑块位置和颜色参数，并引入目标感知适应度与两种引导策略（关键区域定位和共性特征提取）来约束搜索空间，在YOLOv3u、YOLOv5u和Faster R-CNN上超越了现有黑盒斑块方法，且可与白盒基线竞争，并被成功迁移到物理世界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络遥感目标检测器对对抗扰动仍然脆弱，但现有研究主要集中于数字域或白盒攻击，黑盒物理攻击探索不足。现有黑盒物理攻击受限于物理可行性差以及高维搜索空间中优化效率低的问题。

Method: ColorFD包含三个核心组件：(1)基于差分进化(DE)联合优化多个纯色斑块的位置和颜色参数；(2)目标感知适应度与选择机制，评估每个目标的攻击状态并在进化过程中保留目标特定改进；(3)两种引导策略——关键区域定位通过有限差分颜色探测识别敏感区域，共性特征提取提供类别级空间先验以避免重复定位。攻击在数码域优化后迁移至物理场景。

Result: 在YOLOv3u、YOLOv5u和Faster R-CNN三种检测器上，ColorFD在所有评估的检测器中均优于所测试的黑盒斑块方法，并且与强白盒基线相比具有竞争力。物理世界实验表明优化后的纯色斑块能够成功从数字域迁移到真实成像条件。

Conclusion: ColorFD有效地解决了黑盒物理攻击中物理可行性受限和优化效率低的问题，通过多纯色斑块联合优化和引导策略实现了高效的黑盒攻击，且该方法的公式化并不固有地限于飞机这一类别，具有良好的泛化潜力。

Abstract: Although deep neural network-based remote sensing object detectors have achieved strong performance, they remain vulnerable to adversarial perturbations. Existing studies mainly focus on digital or white-box settings, whereas black-box physical attacks remain underexplored. These attacks are often constrained by limited physical feasibility and inefficient optimization in high-dimensional search spaces. To address these challenges, this paper proposes ColorFD, a black-box physical attack based on multiple pure-color patches. The patch positions and color parameters are jointly optimized using Differential Evolution (DE). A target-wise fitness and selection mechanism evaluates the attack state of each target and preserves target-specific improvements during evolution. Two guidance strategies further constrain the patch search space. Key-region localization identifies sensitive regions through finite-difference color probing. Common-feature extraction provides category-level spatial priors and avoids repeated localization. Although evaluated on aircraft, the formulation is not inherently restricted to this category. Experiments on YOLOv3u, YOLOv5u, and Faster R-CNN show that ColorFD outperforms the tested black-box patch method across all evaluated detectors and remains competitive with strong white-box baselines. Physical-world experiments further demonstrate that the optimized pure-color patches can be transferred from the digital domain to real imaging conditions.

</details>


### [88] [OutLangSplat: 3D Language Gaussian Splatting for UAV Outdoor Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04560)
*Xia Yan,He Wu,Yanghui Xu,Zizhao Wu,Jiazhou Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出OutLangSplat，将开放词汇语言高斯表示适配到无人机户外场景，通过2D-3D双分支特征表示和训练无关的一致性感知特征聚合策略，解决了现有方法在UAV户外场景中因严重遮挡和远距离视角导致的语义激活错误和目标响应缺失问题，并提供了首个UAV户外场景的开放词汇3D场景理解数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D语言高斯溅射（3D Language Gaussian Splatting）方法将开放词汇语言特征嵌入3D高斯表示中，但局限于室内或小规模场景。在无人机（UAV）户外场景中，严重遮挡和远距离视角会导致错误的语义激活和缺失的目标响应，现有方法难以适用，因此需要专门适配UAV户外场景的语言高斯表示方法。

Method: OutLangSplat通过两个关键设计提升语言高斯表示在UAV户外场景中的表现：1）特征表示方面，设计2D-3D双分支表示，结合区域级对齐与融合，提升空间一致性，减少目标响应不完整和背景误激活；2）特征聚合方面，提出无需训练的贡献度与一致性感知的高斯特征聚合策略，利用像素贡献可靠性和跨视角语义一致性来抑制来自噪声视角的不可靠响应。此外构建了基于四个真实世界公共UAV户外数据集的人工标注新数据集。

Result: 在开放词汇语义分割和实例定位任务上的定量评估与消融实验表明，OutLangSplat在两项任务上均优于现有最先进方法（SOTA）。该工作提供了首个UAV户外场景的开放词汇3D场景理解数据集，并将开源数据集和代码。

Conclusion: OutLangSplat成功将语言高斯表示从室内小场景扩展到UAV户外场景，验证了通过改进特征表示和聚合可靠性可以解决户外场景中的遮挡和远距离带来的语义理解问题，为该领域提供了可复现的数据集和基准。

Abstract: 3D Language Gaussian Splatting embeds open-vocabulary language features into 3D Gaussian Splatting, providing an efficient explicit representation for text-driven 3D scene understanding. However, existing methods are limited to indoor or small-scale scenes, and tend to fail in Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) outdoor scenes, where severe occlusions and long distance viewpoints often lead to incorrect semantic activations and missing target responses. In this paper, we present OutLangSplat which adapts language Gaussian representations to UAV outdoor scenes by improving feature representation and aggregation reliability. For the feature representation, a 2D-3D dual-branch representation with region-based alignment and fusion is designed to improve spatial consistency, reducing incomplete target responses and background misactivations. For the feature aggregation, we introduce a training-free contribution and consistency-aware Gaussian feature aggregation strategy that leverages pixel contribution reliability and cross-view semantic consistency to suppress unreliable responses from noisy viewpoints. A new dataset is provided by manually annotating various objects on four real-world public UAV outdoor scene datasets. To the best of our knowledge, it is the first accessible dataset of open-vocabulary 3D scene understanding for UAV outdoor scenes. Quantitative evaluations and ablation studies demonstrate that OutLangSplat outperforms SOTA methods on both open-vocabulary semantic segmentation and instance localization tasks. The datasets and codes will be open-sourced.

</details>


### [89] [Talk2Sensors: 3D Visual Grounding in Autonomous Driving via Sensor-Adaptive Physical Cue Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04568)
*Runwei Guan,Di Tian,Ningwei Ouyang,Ruixiao Zhang,Shaofeng Liang,Haocheng Zhao,Lianqing Zheng,Xiaokai Bai,Guotao Wang,Daizong Liu,Henghui Ding,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Talk2Sensors，首个基于相机、LiDAR和4D雷达的多传感器3D视觉定位数据集，以及TSFormer，一个统一的基于Transformer的框架，通过粗到细的属性感知融合策略（Language-Routed Property Sampler和Sparse-Preserving Modality Arbiter）实现自动驾驶场景下的语言引导3D视觉定位，在多个基准上达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D视觉定位研究主要集中在室内场景（RGB-D或点云输入），而室外扩展大多仅依赖单目图像。真实世界的室外感知需要异构传感器捕捉互补且异质的物理属性（视觉纹理、3D几何、物体运动学），这些对灵活鲁棒的查询自适应定位至关重要但尚未被充分利用。

Method: TSFormer采用粗到细的属性感知融合策略：(1) Language-Routed Property Sampler通过查询级语言线索调制传感器采样权重，进行粗粒度的文本条件特征检索；(2) Sparse-Preserving Modality Arbiter进行细粒度的模态仲裁和文本引导精炼，确定精确的被指空间位置。此设计可根据每个提示的语义需求动态路由外观、几何和运动线索，防止密集模态淹没稀疏但关键的传感器信号。

Result: TSFormer在Talk2Sensors上比最强基线提升8.05 mAP，并在单目Mono3DRefer基准上达到53.05%的Acc@0.5，实现跨基准的最先进性能。

Conclusion: 多传感器融合结合语言引导的动态模态路由策略可有效提升自动驾驶场景下的3D视觉定位性能，异构传感器互补属性的充分挖掘是突破现有方法局限的关键。

Abstract: As a key capability for embodied intelligence, 3D visual grounding (3DVG) has been predominantly studied in indoor scenes with RGB-D or point-cloud inputs, while existing outdoor extensions largely rely on monocular images alone. Both settings fall short of real-world outdoor perception, where heterogeneous sensors capture complementary yet distinct physical properties, such as visual texture, 3D geometry, and object kinematics, that are indispensable for flexible and robust query-adaptive grounding but remain under-exploited. To bridge this gap, we introduce Talk2Sensors, the first multi-sensor 3D visual grounding dataset built upon camera, LiDAR, and 4D radar. It contains 8,682 language instructions and 20,558 referred objects, with diverse prompts explicitly aligned with sensor-specific physical cues. Furthermore, we propose TSFormer, a unified Transformer-based framework for language-guided 3D visual grounding in autonomous driving. TSFormer adopts a coarse-to-fine property-aware fusion strategy: the Language-Routed Property Sampler first performs coarse text-conditioned feature retrieval by modulating sensor sampling weights with query-level linguistic cues, while the subsequent Sparse-Preserving Modality Arbiter module conducts fine-grained modality arbitration and text-guided refinement to determine the precise referred spatial location. This design enables dynamic routing of appearance, geometry, and motion cues according to the semantic requirements of each prompt, preventing dense modalities from overwhelming sparse but critical sensor signals. Extensive experiments demonstrate that TSFormer achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple benchmarks: it improves over the strongest baseline by 8.05 mAP on Talk2Sensors, and transfers to the monocular Mono3DRefer benchmark with 53.05\% Acc@0.5.

</details>


### [90] [PhysMind: From Video to Executable Worlds for Training-Free Physical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04575)
*Chen Yang,Shenxiang Zeng,Haoyang Zhao,Zhouyuan Xu,Youquan He,Haoyu Li,Mingyi Deng,Jiansheng Fan,Chen Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhysMind是一个无需训练的智能体框架，通过为每个视频构建可复用、与问题无关的可执行世界模型，实现可靠的物理推理。它整合了物体分割、网格重建和6D姿态跟踪，拟合连续时间动力系统，在CLEVRER和Physion++上分别比直接思维链推理提升38.23和8.08个百分点，在反事实问题推理上超过最强VLM基线GPT-5.5达19.25个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉语言模型（VLMs）在理解视频中的物体运动、交互及干预响应方面存在困难，难以可靠地推理未来结果和反事实场景，因此需要一个更结构化的物理推理框架来弥补这一不足。

Method: PhysMind采用无需训练的智能体架构，核心流程包括：(1) 通过物体分割、网格重建和6D姿态跟踪恢复时间一致的动态场景；(2) 在不展开时间步进仿真的情况下，拟合解析的连续时间动力学模型和潜在物理参数；(3) 针对给定问题，检查、延续或编辑所构建的世界模型，并从生成的轨迹和交互中得出答案。

Result: PhysMind在CLEVRER数据集上比同VLM的直接思维链推理提升38.23个百分点，在Physion++上提升8.08个百分点；在反事实问题上比最强VLM基线GPT-5.5高出19.25个百分点。

Conclusion: PhysMind证明了通过构建可执行、可编辑的显式世界模型，能够在无需额外训练的情况下显著提升VLM在物理推理任务上的表现，尤其在反事实推理方面展现出明显优势。

Abstract: Reliable physical reasoning from video requires understanding how objects move, interact, and respond to interventions. Existing vision-language models (VLMs) often struggle to interpret these dynamics and reason reliably about future and counterfactual outcomes. We introduce PhysMind, a training-free agentic framework that constructs one reusable, question-agnostic executable world per video. PhysMind recovers a temporally consistent dynamic scene through object segmentation, mesh reconstruction, and 6D pose tracking, then fits analytic continuous-time dynamics and latent physical parameters without unrolling a time-stepped simulator. Given a question, it inspects, continues, or edits the world and answers from the resulting trajectories and interactions. Relative to direct chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning with the same VLM, PhysMind improves accuracy by 38.23 points on CLEVRER and 8.08 points on Physion++. On counterfactual questions, it exceeds the strongest evaluated VLM baseline, GPT-5.5, by 19.25 points.

</details>


### [91] [ACA-GS: Adaptive-Capacity Anchored Gaussian Splatting for Compact Dynamic Radiance Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04581)
*Seunghyeon Song,Joo Chan Lee,Chanung Park,Jun Young Jeong,Minseo Lee,Eunbyung Park,Jong Hwan Ko*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自适应容量的锚点（anchor-based）4D高斯泼溅框架，通过动态分配每个锚点的Neural Gaussian数量和特征通道数，打破传统锚点方法中固定参数化的刚性限制，在保持渲染质量的同时实现更高的存储压缩率（最高比SOTA方法压缩1.5倍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有4D高斯泼溅（4DGS）方法在运动表达力与存储效率之间存在根本性权衡。基于锚点的方法虽然通过锚点级参数共享实现紧凑性，但其刚性均匀参数化强制每个锚点固定Neural Gaussian数量和特征预算，导致当保真度不足时只能通过过度增加锚点密度来解决，而非轻量级、针对性地增加Neural Gaussian数量或特征容量，造成内存浪费。

Method: 1. Adaptive Anchor Cardinality：根据局部空间-时间复杂度动态调整每个锚点的Neural Gaussian数量，在高几何或运动复杂度区域集中原始体（primitives），在简单区域抑制冗余。2. Adaptive Anchor Feature Masking：动态调制锚点级特征通道，为复杂区域分配丰富特征，为简单区域分配轻量级表示。

Result: 在MPEG、Panoptic Sports和N3DV数据集上，该方法在保持视觉质量的同时实现了显著的存储压缩。尤其在运动复杂的MPEG序列上，相比最先进的锚点方法实现最高1.5倍的压缩率提升，且质量相当。

Conclusion: 通过打破锚点参数的刚性均匀分配，实现按需分配表示容量，能够在压缩率与渲染质量之间取得更优的平衡，验证了自适应容量分配策略在4DGS框架中的有效性与通用性。

Abstract: Recent advances in 4D Gaussian Splatting (4DGS) enable high-fidelity, real-time spatiotemporal rendering, but expose a fundamental trade-off between motion expressiveness and storage efficiency. While anchor-based designs achieve compactness through anchor-level parameter sharing, their rigid uniform parametrization enforces fixed Neural Gaussian counts and feature budgets per anchor. Consequently, insufficient fidelity is addressed by excessive anchor density, rather than lightweight, targeted increases in Neural Gaussian count or feature capacity, resulting in memory waste. To overcome this rigidity, we introduce an adaptive-capacity anchor-based framework that dynamically allocates the representational capacity based on local spatiotemporal demands. Adaptive Anchor Cardinality varies the number of Neural Gaussians per anchor, concentrating primitives in regions of high geometric or motion complexity while suppressing redundancy. In parallel, Adaptive Anchor Feature Masking modulates anchor-level feature channels, assigning rich features to complex regions and lightweight representations to simpler ones. Experiments on MPEG, Panoptic Sports, and N3DV datasets demonstrate substantial storage reduction without degrading visual quality. Notably, on challenging MPEG sequences with complex motion, our method achieves up to 1.5x higher compression than state-of-the-art anchor-based methods while preserving comparable quality.

</details>


### [92] [MetaVideoAgent: Automated Video-Agent Evolution for Long-Form Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04587)
*Benlei Cui,Ruize Wang,Junjie Li,Jinhao Chen,Longtao Huang,Yinghao Chen,Yuwen Zhai,Jingqun Tang,Ruijian Jia,Weiwei Wu,Pengfei Sun,Haiwen Hong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MetaVideoAgent框架，自动为特定视频分布演化视频智能体，以及VA-EvoBench基准，涵盖八种视频分布。通过四轮演化迭代，将宏平均准确率从38.44%提升到51.47%，平均演化成本为每分布354万token，相比最强固定设计视频智能体提升6.39个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频理解需要在冗长多模态视频中定位稀疏且与问题相关的证据。真实世界的视频分布在模态信息密度、内容结构和证据模式上各不相同，导致固定设计的视频智能体在分布不匹配时会产生冗余处理或失效。将自动智能体演化从文本扩展到视频面临三大挑战：全长视频执行导致候选验证成本高昂、失败在耦合的证据处理阶段间传播、复杂的预处理/感知工具/定位策略使代码级更新难以可靠实现。

Method: MetaVideoAgent包含以下核心组件：(1) 从稀疏采样帧和相关查询中分析信息密度与证据需求以指导初始设计；(2) 将局部失败压缩为可独立执行的最小验证任务；(3) 构建基于证据的Gold Paths，审计Student轨迹，跨样本聚合反复出现的失败并归因到负责模块；(4) 采用模块化智能体表示，将每次更新限制在主要责任模块及其必要依赖上。同时引入VA-EvoBench基准，覆盖八种视频分布，包含独立的演化集和保留测试集。

Result: 在每种分布上进行四轮演化迭代后，MetaVideoAgent使每个初始智能体均获得改进，宏平均准确率从38.44%提升到51.47%，平均演化成本为每分布354万token。演化后的智能体相比最强先前的固定设计视频智能体提升6.39个百分点，同时在对比的视频智能体中使用的token和每问题的视频帧数最少。

Conclusion: MetaVideoAgent证明了自动视频智能体演化的可行性，通过模块化归因和最小化验证任务有效解决了长视频环境下智能体演化的核心挑战，显著优于固定设计方法，并将公开代码和数据以支持可复现研究。

Abstract: Long-form video understanding requires locating sparse, question-relevant evidence in long, multimodal videos. Real-world video distributions differ in modality-specific information density, content structure, and evidence patterns, causing fixed video-agent designs to incur redundant processing or fail when mismatched. Extending automated agent evolution from text to video is challenging because full long-video execution makes candidate validation expensive, failures propagate across coupled evidence-processing stages, and complex preprocessing, perception tools, and localization strategies make code-level updates difficult to implement reliably.
  We introduce MetaVideoAgent, a framework that automatically evolves a video agent for a target distribution. It profiles information density and evidence requirements from sparsely sampled frames and associated queries to guide initial design, then compresses localized failures into independently executable minimal validation tasks. It constructs evidence-grounded Gold Paths, audits Student trajectories, aggregates recurring failures across samples, and attributes them to responsible modules. A modular agent representation constrains each update to the primary responsible module and its necessary dependencies.
  We further introduce VA-EvoBench, covering eight video distributions with separate evolution and held-out splits. With four evolution iterations per distribution, MetaVideoAgent improves every initial agent and raises macro-average accuracy from 38.44% to 51.47%, at an average evolution cost of 3.54M tokens per distribution. The evolved agents outperform the strongest prior fixed-design video agent by 6.39 percentage points while using the fewest tokens and video frames per question among the compared video agents. We will release all code and data to support reproducible research.

</details>


### [93] [The First EgoCross Challenge at EgoVis 2026: Cross-Domain Egocentric Video Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04589)
*Yuqian Fu,Tianwen Qian,Yanjun Li,Yu Li,Kunyu Peng,Xu Zheng,Yongqin Xian,Alessio Tonioni,Yanwei Fu,Xiaoling Wang,Danda Paudel,Federico Tombari,Luc Van Gool,Leyi Wu,Yifan Zhao,Jinjie Zhang,Yinchuan Li,Yingcong Chen,Zixu Li,Zhiwei Chen,Zhiheng Fu,Wenbo Wang,Yupeng Hu,Weili Guan,Liqiang Nie,Takuya Murakawa,Toru Tamaki,Yi Wen,Zhenglin Du,Zhengyang Li,Lingling Li,Licheng Jiao,Wenping Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EgoCross是一个跨域自我中心视频问答基准测试，用于评估多模态大语言模型在日常生活场景之外的泛化能力。首届挑战赛于CVPR 2026的第三届EgoVis工作室举办，涵盖手术、工业装配、极限运动和动物视角四个目标域，收到超过1500份提交，最终发布了官方排行榜和获胜方案总结。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自我中心视频问答模型主要针对日常生活场景进行训练和评估，尚不清楚多模态大语言模型能否泛化到手术、工业装配、极限运动等专业或非典型领域。作者希望通过构建跨域基准来推动这一研究方向。

Method: 构建了包含四个目标域（手术、工业装配、极限运动、动物视角）的跨域自我中心视频问答基准。每个测试样本包含一个第一人称视频片段、一个问题及四个候选答案。设置了两个官方竞赛轨道：Source-Limited Track（限制使用官方基线模型和少量支持集）和Open-Source Track（允许更广泛的模型和数据选择，但禁止手动构建目标域训练数据）。

Result: 挑战赛吸引了超过130名参与者的1500多份提交，其中19支队伍参加Open-Source Track，38支队伍参加Source-Limited Track。论文呈现了官方排行榜结果并总结了两个轨道的优胜方案。

Conclusion: 该报告作为推进跨域自我中心视频理解的实用技术参考，所有资源（挑战数据、基线实现、获胜团队代码）均已公开，期望促进该领域的进一步发展。

Abstract: EgoCross is a cross-domain egocentric video question answering benchmark designed to evaluate whether multimodal large language models can generalize beyond common daily-life scenarios. The first EgoCross Challenge was hosted at the Third EgoVis Workshop at CVPR 2026 and evaluated models on first-person videos from four target domains: surgery, industrial assembly, extreme sports, and animal perspectives. Each test example consists of an egocentric video clip, a question, and four candidate answers, from which the model must select the correct option. This technical report introduces the challenge task, benchmark resources, and two official Codabench tracks. The Source-Limited Track restricts participants to the official baseline model and a small support set, whereas the Open-Source Track permits broader choices of models and training data under rules that prohibit the manual construction of target-domain training data. In total, the challenge received more than 1,500 submissions from over 130 participants, with 19 teams participating in the Open-Source Track and 38 teams in the Source-Limited Track. We further present the official leaderboard results and summarize the winning solutions from both tracks. We hope that this report will serve as a useful technical reference for advancing cross-domain egocentric video understanding. All resources, including the challenge data, baseline implementation, and code released by the winning teams, are made publicly available.

</details>


### [94] [COSMO: Consensus-Driven Shift Modulation for Source-Free Domain Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04604)
*Bo Li,Junjie Peng,Xiaohua Xie,Jianhuang Lai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出COSMO方法，将VLM引导的源域自由域适应（SFDA）建模为样本级别的可靠性分配问题，通过锚定的共享共识实现源模型与VLM的协同适应，在四个基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SFDA方法在显著域偏移下自生成监督会强化源偏差；VLM提供互补语义知识但源模型与VLM的相对可靠性因样本而异，而现有多模型引导方法未显式建模这种变化，在冲突时可能覆盖有效源证据，即"源证据遗忘"问题。

Method: 将VLM引导的SFDA形式化为样本级可靠性分配问题，提出Consensus-Driven Shift Modulation (COSMO)。方法包含两步：(1) 构建样本特定的初始共识，偏向预测更集中的分支；(2) 适应过程中重新聚合两个分支的演进证据，并基于共识不确定性和训练进度调节共识相对初始锚点的移动幅度，使共享监督既锚定又自适应。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上，COSMO在匹配VLM骨干网络的条件下达到当前最优性能；进一步分析表明其更好地平衡了保留有效源证据与吸收互补VLM证据之间的关系。

Conclusion: COSMO通过锚定共享共识的协同适应机制，有效解决了源证据遗忘问题，在利用VLM互补知识的同时保留有效源模型证据，实现了SFDA场景下的性能提升。

Abstract: Source-free domain adaptation (SFDA) adapts a source-trained model to an unlabeled target domain without source data, a practical setting under privacy or storage constraints. Yet its self-generated supervision can reinforce source bias under substantial domain shifts. Pretrained vision-language models (VLMs) offer complementary semantic knowledge, but the relative reliability of the source model and VLM varies across target samples. Existing cross-model guidance does not explicitly account for this variation and may overwrite valid source-derived evidence under conflict, a failure we term source-derived evidence forgetting. We formulate VLM-guided SFDA as a sample-wise reliability-allocation problem and propose Consensus-Driven Shift Modulation (COSMO). COSMO replaces expert-to-expert guidance with co-adaptation through an anchored shared consensus. It first forms a sample-specific initial consensus that favors the more concentrated prediction. During adaptation, COSMO re-aggregates both branches' evolving evidence and regulates how far the resulting consensus moves from its initial anchor based on consensus uncertainty and training progress. This keeps the shared supervision anchored yet adaptive. Across four benchmarks, COSMO achieves state-of-the-art performance under matched VLM backbones. Further analyses indicate that it better balances the retention of valid source-derived evidence with the absorption of complementary VLM evidence.

</details>


### [95] [TRCoRSurg: Temporal-Relational Co-Reasoning for Surgical Video Triplet Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04606)
*Fang Li,Shihao Zou,Weixin Si,Yang Gao,Shuai Li,Aimin Hao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一框架，集成空间、关系和时序线索用于外科手术三元组识别。通过多尺度编码器提取类特定空间先验，由带多尺度类别激活图引导关系提取的标签相关性建模模块（MS-CAMRE）进行精炼，并利用双向时序-关系融合注意力（BTRFA）模块协调时序与关系表示。引入新评估指标Triplet Consistency Error Rate（TCER）来衡量三元组的因果与语义一致性。在CholecT45和ProstaTD数据集上，AP_IVT分别提升5.1%和7.8%，TCER分别相对降低36%和25%以上，达到最先进水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的外科手术三元组识别方法难以在统一框架中同时建模帧内标签依赖关系和帧间时序语义，即无法联合处理空间关系与时序动态。

Method: 提出统一框架，包括三个核心组件：1）多尺度编码器提取类特定空间先验；2）标签相关性建模模块（MS-CAMRE）利用多尺度类别激活图引导的关系提取，捕捉静态共现模式和动态上下文依赖；3）双向时序-关系融合注意力模块（BTRFA）协调时序与关系表示实现连贯时序推理。此外引入新评估指标TCER量化三元组的因果和语义一致性。

Result: 在CholecT45和ProstaTD数据集上均达到最先进性能，AP_IVT分别提升5.1%和7.8%；根据TCER指标，两个数据集上分别实现超过36%和25%的相对降低。

Conclusion: 该框架有效证明了时序-关系协同推理的优势，通过统一建模帧内标签依赖与帧间时序语义，显著提升了外科手术三元组识别的准确性与一致性。

Abstract: Understanding complex surgical scenes requires recognizing multiple interdependent entities, such as instruments, actions, and targets, while maintaining their relational consistency across time. Existing surgical triplet recognition methods struggle to jointly model intra-frame label dependencies and inter-frame temporal semantics in a unified manner. To address these limitations, we propose a unified framework that integrates spatial, relational, and temporal cues for robust surgical triplet recognition. Specifically, class-specific spatial priors are first extracted through a multi-scale encoder. These priors are then refined by a Label Correlation Modeling module with multi-scale class activation map-guided relational extraction (MS-CAMRE), enabling the model to capture both static co-occurrence patterns and dynamic contextual dependencies among triplet components. Furthermore, a Bidirectional Temporal-Relational Fusion Attention (BTRFA) module harmonizes temporal and relational representations to achieve coherent temporal reasoning. We also introduce a new evaluation metric, the Triplet Consistency Error Rate (TCER), which quantitatively measures the model's ability to preserve causal and semantic consistency across triplets. Extensive experiments on the CholecT45 and ProstaTD datasets show that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance, improving AP_IVT by 5.1 percent and 7.8 percent, respectively. Moreover, according to TCER, our approach achieves relative reductions of more than 36 percent and 25 percent on the two datasets, respectively, demonstrating the effectiveness of our framework in temporal-relational co-reasoning.

</details>


### [96] [HiSC: Hierarchical Spatial Clustering Token Compression for Efficient 3D Scene Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04610)
*Jiuhe Qu,Yingping Liang,Ying Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HiSC是一个面向3D视觉语言模型的、无需训练的层次化空间聚类token压缩框架。它通过空间图合并（SGraM）和空间聚类剪枝（SCluP）两个阶段，将token压缩从token级选择提升到cluster级处理，在3D推理基准上实现90%以上的token缩减且性能损失极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D视觉语言模型（3D VLMs）在多样视角场景中实现空间推理时，由于重复观测和大量无信息区域导致严重的token冗余，计算成本高昂。现有的视觉token压缩方法是为2D VLMs设计的，无法捕捉3D场景的结构化特性，导致空间覆盖不完整和细粒度细节丢失。

Method: HiSC包含两个核心策略：（1）空间图合并策略（SGraM）——将跨视图冗余建模为空间连通性，在LLM推理前合并物理一致的冗余token；（2）空间聚类剪枝范式（SCluP）——在LLM推理内部执行簇间和簇内的层次化压缩，保留目标实例完整性的同时保留重要区域的细粒度细节。整个框架无需训练，利用联合几何与语义线索组织空间grounded聚类。

Result: 在多个3D推理基准上的广泛实验验证了HiSC的有效性，特别是在高视觉token剪枝比例下表现优异。HiSC实现了超过90%的token缩减，且仅有极小的性能下降。

Conclusion: HiSC通过将token压缩从token级选择提升到cluster级处理，有效解决3D VLMs中的token冗余问题，实现无需训练的高效压缩，在高剪枝比下保持推理性能，为3D VLMs的高效推理提供了可行方案。

Abstract: 3D vision-language models (3D VLMs) enable spatial reasoning over multi-view scenes but suffer from substantial token redundancy due to duplicated observations and large uninformative regions, leading to high computational cost. Although visual token compression has shown promise in accelerating 2D VLMs, it fails to capture the structured nature of 3D scenes and leads to incomplete spatial coverage and loss of fine-grained details. In this paper, we propose \textbf{HiSC}, a training-free framework for hierarchical spatial clustering token compression in 3D VLMs. HiSC lifts token compression from token-level selection to cluster-level processing by organizing tokens into spatially grounded clusters using joint geometric and semantic cues. Specifically, we first introduce a \textbf{spatial graph-based merging (SGraM) strategy} that models cross-view redundancy as spatial connectivity and consolidates physically consistent regions, effectively merging extremely similar redundant tokens prior to LLM inference. We then propose a \textbf{spatial clustering-based pruning (SCluP) paradigm} within LLM inference, which performs hierarchical compression across clusters and within clusters, preserving object instance completeness while retaining fine-grained details for important regions. Extensive experiments on diverse 3D reasoning benchmarks show validate the effectiveness of HiSC, particularly under high visual token pruning ratios. Besides, HiSC achieves over 90\% token reduction with minimal performance degradation. Code is accessible at https://github.com/elecreak/HiSC.

</details>


### [97] [DAC-Pose: Dual-Agent Collaborative Framework for Pose-Guided Human Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04622)
*Haotian Yang,Zhile Yang,Huiyu Zhou,Xin Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DAC-Pose提出了一种基于双智能体的多模态框架，用于解决姿态引导人体生成中大幅度视角变化下的视觉伪影问题。框架包含Prior Semantic Reasoning (PSR)智能体（负责推测不可见区域的细粒度属性）和Discrepancy-Aware Visual Encoding (DAVE)智能体（负责量化并编码视角引起的空间错位），两者形成反馈闭环，在DeepFashion和Market-1501基准上取得了优于现有方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统姿态引导人体生成方法本质上是被动的像素级映射，在剧烈视角变化下缺乏认知能力去逻辑推导未被观测的图像区域并建模复杂的空间形变，导致产生严重的视觉伪影。作者认为需要引入具备语义推理能力的智能体范式来弥合这一差距。

Method: DAC-Pose将单视角人体生成重构为协同双智能体系统：PSR智能体充当认知引擎，通过协作推理推测不可见区域的细粒度属性；DAVE智能体作为视觉感知模块，量化并编码视角诱导的空间错位，并将鲁棒的空间约束持续反馈到生成过程中，形成语义推理与视觉感知之间的自主反馈闭环。

Result: 在DeepFashion和Market-1501两个基准数据集上的大量实验验证了该智能体驱动范式的优越性，特别是在剧烈视角变化下，DAC-Pose在纹理对齐保真度和身份一致性方面表现出色。

Conclusion: 基于双智能体协同的框架有效克服了传统方法在极端视角变化下的局限性，为姿态引导人体生成提供了一种新的认知驱动范式，代码已开源。

Abstract: AI agents have emerged as a powerful new paradigm in generative image synthesis, enabling systems to perform complex semantic reasoning rather than passive pixel-level mapping. In pose-guided human generation, conventional methods inevitably produce severe visual artifacts under drastic viewpoint shifts, fundamentally because they lack the cognitive capacity to logically deduce unseen regions and model complex spatial deformations. To bridge this gap, we propose DAC-Pose, a novel agent-driven multimodal framework that reformulates single-view human generation as a collaborative dual-agent system. DAC-Pose integrates two complementary components, namely, the Prior Semantic Reasoning (PSR) agent and the Discrepancy-Aware Visual Encoding (DAVE) agent. Functioning as a cognitive engine, PSR utilizes collaborative reasoning to deduce the fine-grained attributes of unseen regions. Concurrently, acting as a specialized visual perception agent, DAVE quantifies and encodes viewpoint-induced spatial misalignments, continuously feeding robust spatial constraints back into the generative process. This autonomous feedback loop between semantic deduction and visual perception ensures high-fidelity detail synthesis. Extensive experiments on the DeepFashion and Market-1501 benchmarks validate the superiority of our agent-driven paradigm. Notably, DAC-Pose excels in preserving texture alignment and identity consistency under drastic viewpoint changes. The code is available at https://github.com/AIVRC/DAC-Pose.

</details>


### [98] [Visual Anchoring in Diffusion: Multimodal Zero-Shot Skeleton Action Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04623)
*Zehao Bao,Shujun Guo,Bruce X. B. Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TDSM-MM，一种基于"分类即生成"范式的零样本骨架动作识别方法。它通过让文本和RGB联合条件化一个去噪器来对骨架特征加噪预测，避免了多模态分支独立打分后融合权重难以选择的问题，在NTU-60/120多个split上取得了最佳归纳精度，并在NTU-120 96/24上超过转导式SOTA（71.3% vs 69.1%），且无需测试时自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零样本骨架动作识别中，当未见动作具有相似的骨架关节动态但物体或场景上下文不同时，仅靠骨架信息存在歧义。RGB可提供缺失线索，但现有多模态方法通常独立维护骨架和RGB打分分支再融合输出，存在两大问题：(1) 固定融合权重无法捕捉类别对间模态可靠性的差异；(2) 自适应融合规则缺乏目标侧反馈来判断哪个分支应占主导。作者希望绕过权重选择问题。

Method: 采用"分类即生成"（classify-by-generation）范式：每个类别通过一个文本条件化的去噪器对骨架特征添加噪声的预测精度来打分。该方法将逐步被损坏的骨架特征与固定的条件信息分离，使RGB和文本能够联合条件化单一类别打分函数，而非产生独立分数。具体实现为多模态三元组扩散骨架-文本匹配（TDSM-MM），在文本条件化的去噪Transformer基础上增广一个非扩散的RGB条件token，作为骨架数据重建过程中的稳定视觉锚点。

Result: 在NTU-60/120的四个split中的三个上取得最佳归纳精度，并在NTU-120 96/24上超越转导式SOTA（71.3% vs 69.1%），且完全无需测试时自适应。

Conclusion: TDSM-MM通过扩散生成范式统一多模态条件化，有效解决了多模态打分融合中的权重选择难题，证明了扩散方法在零样本学习中的潜力。

Abstract: Zero-shot Skeleton Action Recognition (ZSAR) remains ambiguous when unseen actions share similar skeleton joint dynamics but differ in objects or scene context. RGB provides these missing cues, yet existing multimodal methods typically maintain independent skeleton and RGB scoring branches and fuse their outputs. Without using unlabeled test data for adaptation or fusion calibration, a fixed fusion weight cannot capture class-pair-dependent modality reliability, while an adaptive rule lacks target-side feedback for deciding which branch should dominate. We bypass this weight-selection problem via the classify-by-generation paradigm, where each class is scored by how accurately a text-conditioned denoiser predicts the noise added to the skeleton feature. This formulation separates the progressively corrupted skeleton from fixed conditioning, allowing RGB and text to jointly condition a single class-scoring function rather than produce independent scores. We instantiate this idea as Multimodal Triplet Diffusion for Skeleton-Text Matching (TDSM-MM), augmenting a text-conditioned denoising Transformer with a non-diffused RGB condition token that serves as a stable visual anchor during skeleton data reconstruction. Our proposed TDSM-MM has been ablated via extensive experiments and achieved the best inductive accuracy on three of four NTU-60/120 splits and surpasses the transductive state-of-the-art on NTU-120 96/24 (i.e., 71.3% vs. 69.1%), without test-time adaptation, suggesting that diffusion-based methods can be a promising direction for zero-shot learning.

</details>


### [99] [YOLO-PVC: 2D-to-3D Consolidation of Slice-wise Detections for Volumetric Liver Tumor Localization in MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04642)
*Talha Waqas,Mounir Lahlouh,Kawther Taibouni,Mahnoor Waqas,Salar Ahmed,Sébastien Mulé,Yasmina Leroul-Chenoune*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出YOLO-PVC，一种轻量级且与模型无关的框架，用于将切片级2D目标检测结果整合为连贯的3D检测。该方法通过强制深度连续性、使用稳健百分位统计聚合边界框坐标，并借助轻量MLP校准模块细化轴向范围。在3D肝脏MRI三类别肿瘤数据集上，校准变体将3D IoU从0.665提升至0.710，显著优于朴素堆叠和平均策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 切片式2D检测器因其计算高效和可扩展性被广泛应用于体数据，但在深度轴上常产生碎片化、不稳定的预测结果，缺乏对3D几何一致性的显式建模。

Method: 提出YOLO-PVC框架，包含三个核心组件：(1)强制深度连续性约束；(2)利用稳健百分位统计量聚合边界框坐标，以应对缺失检测和异常切片；(3)基于轻量MLP的校准模块进一步细化轴向范围。该框架是模型无关的，可即插即用于现有2D检测器。

Result: 在3D肝脏MRI数据集的三类肿瘤上，启发式PVC达到0.665的整体3D IoU，校准变体将性能提升至0.710，且平面重叠高（BEV IoU约0.78），一致优于多个聚合基线。

Conclusion: 结构化几何整合为临床MRI中的体积肝脏肿瘤定位提供了有效且实用的解决方案，证明了通过显式建模深度维度的几何一致性，可以显著改善切片级2D检测在3D场景下的表现。

Abstract: Slice-wise 2D object detectors are increasingly applied to volumetric data due to their computational efficiency and scalability, yet they often yield fragmented and unstable predictions along the depth axis. We propose YOLO-PVC, a lightweight and model-agnostic framework for 2D-to-3D consolidation of slice-wise detections. The method enforces depth continuity, aggregates bounding box coordinates using robust percentile statistics, and further refines axial extent through a lightweight MLP-based calibration module. Unlike naïve stacking or averaging strategies, YOLO-PVC explicitly addresses missing detections and outlier slices along the depth dimension. Experiments on 3D liver MRI volumes across three tumor categories demonstrate consistent improvements over multiple aggregation baselines. The heuristic PVC achieves an overall $\mathrm{IoU}_{3D}$ of $0.665$, while the calibrated variant further improves performance to $0.710$, with high planar overlap ($\mathrm{BEV\ IoU} \approx 0.78$). These results demonstrate that structured geometric consolidation provides an effective and practical solution for volumetric liver tumor localization in clinical MRI.

</details>


### [100] [DisMix: Order-Aware Mixup for Medical Imaging via Disentangling Ordinal and Non-Ordinal Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04652)
*Dileepa Pitawela,Gustavo Carneiro,Hsiang-Ting Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DisMix是一个面向序数分类任务（如医学疾病分级）的顺序感知数据增强框架。它通过双码本VQ-VAE将序数特征和非序数特征解耦，分别进行插值和变异混合，在四个医学影像数据集上相比六种mixup基线搭配六种序数分类器表现出最佳综合性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准图像mixup数据增强策略不适用于序数分类任务（如医学疾病严重程度分级）。因为标签编码的是疾病进展的严重程度（有序），而标准mixup盲目混合序数级别的疾病严重度线索和非序数的外观变化，会产生扭曲序数结构的样本，损害临床分级中的有序结构。

Method: 引入DisMix，一个面向序数分类的顺序感知mixup框架。核心方法是通过双码本VQ-VAE将图像的序数特征和非序数特征进行解耦，使两个子空间可以独立混合：序数码通过插值生成有意义的中等级别，而非序数码通过变异引入外观多样性，同时不破坏序数信号。

Result: 在四个医学影像数据集上，将DisMix与六种图像mixup基线配合六种序数分类器进行对比，DisMix取得了最佳的综合性能；并且在数据稀缺和临床分级变异的情况下依然保持有效。

Conclusion: DisMix通过主动分离并独立处理序数与非序数特征，解决了标准mixup在序数分类任务中的结构破坏问题，为医学疾病分级等序数分类场景提供了一种更可靠的数据增强方案。

Abstract: Image mixup is a widely adopted data augmentation strategy, yet it is ill-suited for ordinal classification tasks such as medical disease grading, where labels encode a progression of severity. By indiscriminately blending disease-severity cues (ordinal) with appearance-level variation (non-ordinal), standard mixup produces samples that distort the very ordinal structure that underpins clinical severity grading. We introduce DisMix, an order-aware mixup framework for ordinal classification. DisMix disentangles ordinal and non-ordinal features via a dual-codebook VQ-VAE, allowing each subspace to be mixed independently: ordinal codes are interpolated to produce meaningful intermediate ranks, while non-ordinal codes are varied to introduce appearance diversity without corrupting the ordinal signal. Across four medical imaging datasets, DisMix shows the best aggregate performance among six image mixup baselines paired with six ordinal classifiers and remains effective under data scarcity and clinical grading variability.

</details>


### [101] [Overcoming Statistical Bias in Action-Controllable World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04653)
*Yuhong Shi,Zhenhao Chu,Jie Wei,Jun Hao,Jianyi Liu,Jingwen Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CoCo框架，通过多步反事实一致性和动作-空间反事实一致性两种约束，减少动作条件世界模型对统计捷径的依赖，提升动作可控性。引入新的评估指标ARC和DE，以及Mini-SSMB基准，在多个数据集上实现了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动作条件世界模型旨在预测视觉环境在智能体动作下的演化，但未来帧往往可以从视觉惯性和周期性运动模式中高度预测，这导致模型可以通过利用统计偏差来拟合数据，而无需让可见动态真正依赖于动作。结果就是不同动作产生相似未来，零动作下运动依然持续。核心问题是减少模型对统计捷径的依赖，使动作真正控制预测结果。

Method: 作者提出CoCo（Counterfactual Consistency）框架，通过两个互补约束增强动作可控性：(1) 多步反事实一致性（multi-step counterfactual consistency），约束参考、逆动作和零动作的展开轨迹；(2) 动作-空间反事实一致性（action-spatial counterfactual consistency），在镜像场景和变换动作下强制一致预测。此外设计了评估指标ARC（Action Response Consistency）和DE（Drift Energy），以及Mini-SSMB同状态多动作反事实评估基准。

Result: 在Mini-SSMB上，完整模型ARC_inv达到0.412，ARC_ref达到0.483，DE相对基线降低17.07%。在VP2视觉规划任务上取得最高平均成功率73.1%。BAIR和RoboNet实验表明这些增益保持了视频预测质量，并能跨模型设置迁移。

Conclusion: 仅向模型注入动作特征不足以实现动作控制，必须通过反事实一致性约束强制模型在动作和观测发生反事实变化时保持一致性。CoCo框架有效减轻了统计捷径对动作条件预测的主导作用，提升了动作可控性，同时保持了视频预测质量，且该方法具有良好的泛化性。

Abstract: Action-conditioned world models aim to predict how visual environments evolve under an agent's actions. Yet future frames are often highly predictable from visual inertia and recurring motion patterns alone. This creates a shortcut: models can fit the data by exploiting statistical biases without making their visible dynamics meaningfully depend on the action. As a result, different actions may produce similar futures, while motion may persist even under zero action. The key question is how to reduce reliance on statistical shortcuts from dominating action-conditioned prediction. We argue that action control requires more than injecting action features; it requires enforcing consistency under counterfactual changes to actions and observations. Based on this insight, we introduce CoCo, a Counterfactual Consistency framework to enhance action controllability through two complementary constraints. Multi-step counterfactual consistency constrains reference, inverse-action, and zero-action rollouts, while action-spatial counterfactual consistency enforces consistent predictions under mirrored scenes and transformed actions. Together, they reduce reliance on statistical shortcuts from substituting for action-dependent dynamics. We further introduce Action Response Consistency (ARC) and Drift Energy (DE) to assess action controllability, together with Mini-SSMB for same-state, multi-action counterfactual evaluation. On Mini-SSMB, our full model achieved ARC_inv of 0.412 and ARC_ref of 0.483, while reducing DE by 17.07% relative to the baseline. On VP2 visual planning, it achieves the highest average success rate among SOTA models, at 73.1%. Experiments on BAIR and RoboNet further show that these gains preserve video prediction quality and transfer across model settings.

</details>


### [102] [CSGen: A Multi-Domain Curvilinear Structure Generation Model via Hierarchical Multimodal Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04655)
*Zhe Shan,Ziming Yang,Lei Zhou,Wenwen Zhang,Cong Lin,Xia Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CSGen，一个层级多模态扩散模型，用于精确生成具有弯曲结构（curvilinear structure）对象的高保真图像，通过构建多域多模态数据集、层级渐进控制策略以及稀疏感知损失重加权机制，实现对多个控制条件的精确对齐，显著提升结构准确性与视觉真实感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弯曲结构分析是多媒体中的重要基础任务，但可控生成具有精确弯曲结构对象的图像仍是开放挑战。现有方法难以在多个控制条件下保持稀疏结构的拓扑完整性与语义一致性。

Method: CSGen基于三个关键创新：1）构建包含5个域、24K+样本、7类标注的多域多模态数据集；2）提出层级渐进控制策略，通过分阶段信号注入将拓扑线索与视觉上下文解耦，缓解语义漂移并保证稀疏结构的拓扑完整性；3）设计稀疏感知损失重加权机制，针对弯曲结构的极端稀疏性增强优化中对细薄脆弱结构的关注。

Result: 大量实验表明CSGen生成图像具有更优的结构准确性和视觉真实感，显著提升下游分割性能，并在多样化提示下保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: CSGen被验证为一种可扩展的、以数据为中心的范式，适用于多种多媒体应用中复杂弯曲结构的分析。代码与数据集已公开。

Abstract: Curvilinear structure analysis is an important and fundamental task in multimedia. However, the controllable generation of images with precise curvilinear structure objects remains an open challenge. To address this, we propose CSGen, a hierarchical multimodal diffusion model that synthesizes high-fidelity images precisely aligned with multiple control conditions. The CSGen is built upon three key innovations: 1) We construct a multi-domain and multimodal dataset, including over 24K samples from 5 domains and 7 different types of annotations, to train the unified generation model. 2) We propose a novel hierarchical progressive control strategy that decouples topology clues from visual context by a phased signal injection, mitigating semantic drift while ensuring the topological integrity of sparse structures. 3) We design a sparsity-aware loss re-weighting mechanism to address the extreme sparsity of curvilinear structures, significantly enhancing the attention on thin and fragile structures during optimization. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CSGen generates images with superior structure accuracy and visual realism, significantly improving downstream segmentation performance while maintaining robustness across diverse prompts. Our results confirm CSGen as a scalable, data-centric paradigm for the analysis of complex curvilinear structures in diverse multimedia applications. Code and dataset are available at https://github.com/ShanZard/CSGen.

</details>


### [103] [MobileWAM: Bridging World Action Models to Mobile Manipulation with Chain-of-Foresight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04657)
*Zehua Fan,Junjie He,Wenxuan Song,Xi Wang,Wenqi Lyu,Linge Zhao,Fuhao Li,Zihan You,Yifei Yang,Kaiming Xu,Qi Jiang,Yue Jiang,Haoang Li,Cheng Chi,Bailin Li,Yan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MobileWAM是一种基于视频生成主干（video diffusion transformer）构建的世界动作模型（WAM），通过混合Transformer架构将预训练视频扩散模型与轻量级动作专家通过逐层联合注意力融合，实现移动操作（mobile manipulation）的全身控制。提出了三专家混合（共享/移动/操作专家）的前馈层设计，以及Chain-of-Foresight（CoF）中间表示监督方案，在ManiSkill-HAB基准和真实ARX Lift2移动机械臂上超越现有SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的世界动作模型（WAMs）主要局限于桌面操作场景，而移动操作需要同时处理移动（locomotion）和全身操作（whole-body manipulation），且面对场景级动态。当前移动操作策略仍依赖动态盲视的视觉编码器和手工设计的协调机制，缺乏利用视频生成模型中蕴含的大规模运动先验的能力。

Method: 1) 混合Transformer架构：将预训练视频扩散Transformer与轻量级动作专家通过逐层联合注意力（layerwise joint attention）融合，将互联网规模的运动先验转化为全身控制信号。2) 三专家混合前馈层：动作专家的每个前馈层设计为共享专家、移动专家和操作专家的混合，由动作token中的运动意图（motion intent）进行软路由，以协调移动和操作之间的异构动力学。3) Chain-of-Foresight (CoF)：中间表示顺序预测一系列未来潜在块（future latent chunks），每一步以其前驱为条件，与解耦的视频-动作去噪方案配合，使监督信号更密集。4) 推理时不使用CoF和视频生成，仅将WAM作为纯当前帧编码器，控制成本低。

Result: MobileWAM在ManiSkill-HAB基准上超越了现有最优的移动操作策略，并在真实ARX Lift2移动机械臂上通过微调在多种任务中展现出强大的泛化能力。

Conclusion: MobileWAM成功将视频生成大模型的世界模型先验从桌面操作扩展到移动操作领域，验证了混合Transformer架构和运动意图路由机制对异构动力学协调的有效性，同时通过解耦视频-动作去噪和CoF监督方案实现了高效推理，为移动操作提供了新的范式。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) built on video generation backbones are a rising recipe for robot learning, yet remain confined to tabletop manipulation. Mobile manipulation demands simultaneous locomotion and whole-body manipulation amid scene-scale dynamics, yet is still dominated by dynamics-blind visual encoders with hand-crafted coordination. We bridge this gap with MobileWAM, a mixture-of-transformers architecture that fuses a pretrained video diffusion transformer with a lightweight action expert through layerwise joint attention, translating internet-scale motion priors into whole-body control. To reconcile the heterogeneous dynamics of moving and manipulating, each feed-forward layer of the action expert becomes a three-expert mixture of shared, locomotion, and manipulation experts, softly routed by the motion intent in the action tokens. To densify supervision, we further propose Chain-of-Foresight (CoF): intermediate representations sequentially predict a chain of future latent chunks, each step conditioned on its predecessor. CoF pairs naturally with our decoupled video--action denoising scheme. At deployment, the WAM serves as a pure current-frame encoder; foresight acts only through gradients, so at inference the foresight chain and video generation are discarded, leaving only policy-level cost. MobileWAM surpasses state-of-the-art mobile manipulation policies on ManiSkill-HAB and fine-tunes to a real ARX Lift2 mobile manipulator across diverse tasks with strong generalization. Code will be released soon.

</details>


### [104] [Differential 6-DOF Pose Estimation with Provable First-Order Immunity to Camera Calibration Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04673)
*Yueqiang Zhang,Liang Deng,Yi Zhang,Baoqiong Wang,Wenjun Chen,Shuixin Pan,Yulan Guo,Qifeng Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种差分位姿估计方法，直接从帧间图像位移和已知3D控制点恢复平台6自由度微动，避免独立绝对位姿估计，支持单目和多相机系统。通过差分透视投影方程、深度不变近似和SE(3)运动建模，证明平移外参误差完全抵消，旋转误差产生有界扰动。在合成和真实实验中，以5个控制点和0.5像素噪声，单目求解器实现旋转RMSE 10.09角秒、平移RMSE 3.70毫米、运行时间0.34毫秒，显著优于代表性PnP和广义PnP方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统3D-2D方法在每个时刻独立估计绝对相机位姿，再通过相机到平台的外参恢复平台运动，对外参标定误差非常敏感，尤其在微动场景中误差被放大。需要一种不依赖独立绝对位姿估计、对外参误差更鲁棒的直接运动估计方法。

Method: 采用差分位姿估计框架：对透视投影方程进行差分（不同帧之间），利用深度不变近似将问题线性化，并在SE(3)李群上建模平台运动。理论上证明了平移外参误差在差分中精确抵消，旋转外参误差仅引起有界扰动。同时推导了通用可观测性条件、Cramer-Rao下界和偏差消除的一致估计器，并刻画了近似有效性的极限条件。算法同时支持单目和双/多相机系统。

Result: 在合成和真实实验中，该方法在精度、标定鲁棒性和计算效率方面均超越代表性PnP和广义PnP方法。单目求解器在5个控制点和0.5像素噪声下，旋转(pitch-yaw)RMSE为10.09角秒、平移RMSE为3.70毫米、运行时间0.34毫秒；双目求解器旋转RMSE为10.58角秒、平移RMSE为3.91毫米、运行时间为0.27毫秒。

Conclusion: 该差分位姿估计方法为6-DOF平台微动估计建立了新的最先进水平，通过理论分析保证外参误差鲁棒性，并提供偏差消除的一致估计，在机器人操作、自主系统和结构位移监测等应用中具有显著价值。代码将公开发布。

Abstract: Accurate six-degree-of-freedom (6-DOF) motion estimation is essential for robotic manipulation, autonomous systems, and structural displacement monitoring. Conventional 3D-2D methods estimate absolute camera poses independently at each time and recover platform motion through camera-to-platform extrinsics, making them sensitive to extrinsic calibration errors, especially for micromotion. We present a differential pose estimation method that directly recovers platform motion from inter-frame image displacements and known 3D control points. By differencing perspective projection equations, using a depth-invariance approximation, and modeling motion on SE(3), the method avoids independent absolute-pose estimation and supports both monocular and multi-camera systems. We prove that translational extrinsic errors cancel exactly, while rotational errors induce a bounded perturbation determined by calibration error, motion magnitude, and observation geometry. We also derive generic observability conditions, a Cramer-Rao lower bound, and a bias-eliminated consistent estimator, and characterize the validity limits of the approximations. Extensive synthetic and real-world experiments establish a new state of the art for 6-DOF platform micromotion estimation, outperforming representative PnP and generalized-PnP methods in accuracy, calibration robustness, and computational efficiency. With five control points and 0.5-pixel image noise, the monocular solver obtains a combined pitch-yaw rotation RMSE of 10.09 arcsec, a translation RMSE of 3.70 mm, and a runtime of 0.34 ms. The binocular solver achieves a rotation RMSE of 10.58 arcsec, a translation RMSE of 3.91 mm, and a runtime of 0.27 ms. Code will be released upon publication at https://github.com/zyoungszu/pami2026.

</details>


### [105] [SurgNarrator: A Generative Retrieval Framework for Surgical Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04676)
*Yuqing Feng,Jiawei Ma,Kevin Qinghong Lin,Kun Yuan,Nicolas Padoy,Daniel S. Elson,Anh Nguyen,Stamatia Giannarou,Baoru Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SurgNarrator是一个面向手术视频理解的新型生成式检索框架，通过手术专属词汇空间、时序感知对比学习目标以及分层流程感知检索策略，在12个基准上实现了零样本设置下的一致性能提升，同时相较生成式基线将输出阶段延迟降低了两个数量级以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手术视频理解方法存在两难困境：自回归视频语言模型支持全面推理但延迟高，不适合时间敏感的临床应用；对比模型延迟低但复杂场景理解能力不足。将生成式检索从通用领域迁移到手术领域存在两大挑战：视觉外观相近但语义不同的事件难以区分，以及手术术语高度专业化。

Method: SurgNarrator包含三个核心组件：(1)从手术字幕构建精心策划的手术中心词汇表，定义临床有意义的检索空间；(2)基于预训练的Qwen3-VL-Embedding-8B，使用时序感知对比目标学习判别性临床表示；(3)在推理阶段采用分层、流程感知的检索策略，将搜索空间缩小至相关手术类型。

Result: 在12个基准的零样本设置下，SurgNarrator相比最先进基线取得了一致的性能提升，且相较生成式基线将输出阶段延迟降低了两个数量级以上（即超过100倍）。

Conclusion: SurgNarrator证明了生成式检索框架可以有效迁移到手术视频理解领域，在保持高推理能力的同时显著降低延迟，为时间敏感的临床决策支持提供了一种实用且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Surgical procedures unfold as structured and recurring clinical events, whose real-time understanding via intraoperative surgical videos is critical for intraoperative decision-making and support. However, existing video understanding methods force a trade-off: autoregressive video-language models support comprehensive reasoning but are not practical for time-sensitive clinical applications, whereas contrastive models offer low latency but struggle with complex scene understanding. Recently, generative retrieval has been explored for general-domain video understanding, but transferring it to surgery is not trivial because near-identical visual appearances may indicate semantically distinct events, and the terminology involved is highly surgery-specific. To this end, we propose SurgNarrator, a new generative retrieval framework tailored for surgical video understanding. We construct a well-curated surgery-centric vocabulary from surgical captions to define a clinically meaningful retrieval space. We then adapt the pre-trained Qwen3-VL-Embedding-8B to learn discriminative clinical representations with a temporally-aware contrastive objective. During inference, a hierarchical, procedure-aware retrieval strategy narrows the search space to the relevant procedure type, delivering fast and effective responses. Our method is comprehensively evaluated on twelve benchmarks in a zero-shot setting and achieves consistent performance gains over state-of-the-art baselines, while reducing output-stage latency by more than two orders of magnitude compared with the generative baseline.

</details>


### [106] [Teaching MLLMs to Say No: Generalized Referring Expression Comprehension via Refusal Calibrated GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04698)
*Xuzheng Yang,Jun Ling,Tao Huang,Caiyan Qin,Peng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RC-GRPO，一种校准的强化学习策略，用于解决广义指代表达理解（GREC）中MLLM在负样本上产生幻觉边界框的问题，在增强拒绝能力的同时保持正样本上的定位精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM在定位已存在物体方面表现出色，但由于训练时缺乏负样本，往往无法拒绝不存在的物体，产生幻觉边界框。现有的后训练方法（如SFT和RL）虽然增强了拒绝行为，但通常会降低正样本上的定位精度，损害模型的核心能力。因此需要一种既能增强拒绝能力又不损害定位性能的平衡策略。

Method: 提出Refusal-Calibrated Group Relative Policy Optimization (RC-GRPO)，包含两个关键设计：(1) 在rollouts中强制负样本输出"None"以获得有效的优势估计，从而校准RL训练；(2) 对正样本施加惩罚以防止过度拒绝，实现准确性与可靠性的平衡。此外，引入第二阶段推理强化以巩固因果理解与可解释性。

Result: 在三个GREC基准上的实验表明，RC-GRPO在保持强拒绝能力的同时，取得了优越的定位精度。

Conclusion: RC-GRPO通过校准的RL策略有效解决了MLLM在GREC任务中幻觉定位的问题，实现了定位准确性与拒绝可靠性之间的平衡，为多模态大语言模型的可靠推理提供了新的方向。

Abstract: We tackle the challenging yet underexplored task of Generalized Referring Expression Comprehension (GREC), which requires a model to localize the object described by a textual expression when it exists (positive sample) and to refuse output when it does not (negative sample). Although Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) excel at localizing existing objects, they often fail to reject nonexistent ones due to the absence of negative samples during training, producing hallucinated bounding boxes. Existing post-training approaches such as supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning (RL) enhance refusal behavior but usually degrade localization accuracy on positive samples, undermining the model's core competence. To address this, we propose Refusal-Calibrated Group Relative Policy Optimization (RC-GRPO), a calibrated RL strategy that strengthens the refusal ability of MLLMs while preserving localization performance. It enforces "None" outputs in rollouts for valid advantage estimation on negative samples and applies a penalty to prevent over-refusal on positives, achieving a balanced trade-off between accuracy and reliability. A second-stage reasoning reinforcement further consolidates causal understanding and interpretability. Experiments on three GREC benchmarks demonstrate that RC-GRPO attains superior localization accuracy while maintaining strong refusal capability.

</details>


### [107] [UniWorld-View: Large-Baseline View Synthesis via Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04701)
*Haiyang Zhou,Wangbo Yu,Chaoran Feng,Xunyu Zhou,Yonghong Tian,Li Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出UniWorld-View，一种统一的框架，用于从单目输入进行可控的大基线新视角合成。该框架结合了显式3D引导（通过遮挡感知的点云渲染策略）与生成式扩散建模，实现了精确的相机控制和几何一致的视角生成，在WorldScore基准和零样本NVS基准上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体上大量的单目视频和图像为沉浸式内容创作提供了宝贵素材，但从稀疏观测生成新视角仍面临巨大挑战。基于重建的方法（如NeRF和3DGS）在稀疏输入下性能严重退化且无法显式处理遮挡；生成式方法虽然降低了数据要求，但因不准确或隐式的几何引导而难以处理大基线的视角合成。

Method: UniWorld-View将显式3D引导与生成式扩散建模集成到统一框架中。其几何引导通过一种遮挡感知的点云渲染策略获得，该策略解决了可见性歧义问题，为扩散合成提供了准确的先验信息。框架将这一渲染策略与强大的视频扩散主干网络耦合，实现高保真的新视角生成，并可进一步提供多视角视频用于下游动态3DGS重建。

Result: 在WorldScore基准和零样本NVS基准上的实验证明了UniWorld-View在可控性、几何一致性和视觉保真度方面的有效性，即使在极端相机运动和宽基线变化下也能实现高保真的新视角生成。

Conclusion: UniWorld-View通过将显式几何引导与生成式扩散建模结合，有效解决了大基线稀疏输入下的新视角合成难题，同时支持下游动态3DGS重建，为该领域提供了一种统一且可控的解决方案。

Abstract: The abundance of casually captured monocular videos and images on social media provides a valuable source for immersive content creation, where generating novel views from such sparse observations can greatly enhance user experiences. However, producing photorealistic and geometrically consistent views with precise camera control remains challenging when input coverage is extremely limited. Reconstruction-based approaches such as NeRF and 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) deteriorate severely under sparse inputs and fail to explicitly handle occlusions. Generative methods ease data requirements but still struggle with large-baseline view synthesis due to inaccurate or implicit geometric guidance. To overcome these limitations, we introduce UniWorld-View, a unified framework for controllable large-baseline novel view synthesis from monocular inputs. UniWorld-View integrates explicit 3D guidance with generative diffusion modeling to enable precise camera control and geometrically consistent view generation. The geometric guidance is obtained through an occlusion-aware point cloud rendering strategy that resolves visibility ambiguities and provides accurate priors for diffusion-based synthesis. By coupling this rendering strategy with powerful video diffusion backbones, UniWorld-View achieves high-fidelity novel view generation even under extreme camera motions and wide-baseline changes, and can further provide multi-view videos for downstream dynamic 3DGS reconstruction. Experiments on the WorldScore benchmark and zero-shot NVS benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of UniWorld-View in controllability, geometric consistency, and visual fidelity.

</details>


### [108] [Visual Representation Matters: Exploiting Temporal Differences in Video-to-Audio Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04902)
*Zehua Chen,Junyou Wang,Yuxuan Jiang,Zhenying Fang,Yusheng Dai,Jianfei Chen,Ziwei Liu,Jun Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TD-V2A，一种基于时间差分（Temporal Differences）表示的视频到音频（V2A）生成框架。通过利用连续帧之间的时间差分作为区分V2A与I2A的关键特征，以最小的架构修改增强视觉条件，配合分层持续学习策略和退火时间差分引导方法，在基准数据集上显著提升了端到端V2A生成质量，甚至超越了专门设计的对比视听预训练表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的条件扩散式V2A方法通常需要借助额外的音视频监督、声学结构预测或大语言模型推理来增强视觉条件，这引入了额外的网络或强归纳偏置。作者希望找到一种更简洁、最小架构修改的方式来区分V2A与I2A，并提升V2A生成质量。

Method: 1) 提出将时间差分（TD）作为区分V2A与I2A的关键表示，在帧级和特征级两个层面系统探究TD对视觉表征的补充作用。2) 基于研究发现，设计分层持续学习（hierarchically continual learning）策略，在扩散训练过程中渐进式地学习和利用TD信息。3) 提出退火时间差分引导（annealed temporal differences guidance）方法，在采样过程中逐步利用TD信息来引导生成。

Result: 在基准数据集上的大量实验表明，通过所提出的框架有效利用TD可以显著提升端到端V2A生成质量，甚至超越了专门的V2A表示方法（如对比视听预训练，contrastive audio-visual pretraining）。

Conclusion: 有效利用时间差分信息是V2A生成的关键，只需最小架构修改即可获得显著的性能提升，为V2A生成提供了一种高效、简洁的新范式。

Abstract: Video-to-audio (V2A) generation extends image-to-audio generation (I2A) by introducing consecutive frames that provide essential temporal cues for audio synthesis. However, existing conditional diffusion-based V2A methods typically enhance visual conditioning with additional audio-visual supervision, acoustic structure prediction, or reasoning from large multimodal models, requiring extra networks or strong inductive biases. Inspired by recent advances in visual representation learning, we introduce TD-V2A, which leverages temporal differences (TD) as the key representation that distinguishes V2A from I2A, enriching visual conditioning with minimal architectural modification. We first investigate TD at both the frame and feature levels to identify the most effective representation level at which TD complements visual representations. Based on these findings, we develop a hierarchically continual learning strategy and an annealed temporal differences guidance method to progressively learn and exploit TD information during diffusion training and sampling process, respectively. Extensive experiments on benchmark datasets demonstrate that effectively exploiting TD through our proposed framework significantly improves end-to-end V2A generation quality, even outperforming dedicated V2A representations such as contrastive audio-visual pretraining.

</details>


### [109] [A Multi-Sensor Dataset for Monitoring the Operational Environment of Rail Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04704)
*Claudio Diotallevi,Rodrigo Gudiño,Zaharia Pachalieva,Philipp Neumaier,Patrick Naumann,Erik Bochinski,Volker Eiselein,Martin Köppel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 德国铁路基础设施公司（DB InfraGO AG）与understandAI GmbH在"Digitale Schiene Deutschland"计划下开发了一套面向铁路环境感知的多传感器数据集，包含超过700万个高质量标注，涵盖铁路特定及通用感知对象，可用于自动化铁路系统（GoA2至GoA4）的AI环境监测训练与验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠的铁路环境监测对自动化铁路系统（从GoA2到GoA4）的安全高效运行至关重要，而AI驱动的感知系统需要大量精确标注的数据进行训练和验证，但当前缺乏针对铁路环境感知的综合数据集。

Method: 在Digitale Schiene Deutschland计划下，DB InfraGO AG与understandAI GmbH合作构建了多传感器数据集，包含超过700万个高质量标注，覆盖铁路特定对象与通用感知对象，并在多种运行场景下采集数据。

Result: 该数据集现已完成并可向DB InfraGO AG申请获取，可作为推动铁路领域AI环境监测发展的宝贵资源。

Conclusion: 该多传感器数据集为铁路环境感知提供了大规模、高质量的训练与验证数据基础，有助于推进自动化铁路系统的AI驱动环境监测能力。

Abstract: Reliable environment monitoring is essential for the safe and efficient operation of automated railway systems, covering all Grades of Automation (GoA), from partially automated (GoA2) to fully automated operation (GoA4). Artificial Intelligence (AI) plays a central role in enabling these systems to detect, classify, and react to potential hazards in real time. The development of such AI-based perception systems requires large volumes of accurately annotated data for training and validation.
  Within the Digitale Schiene Deutschland (DSD) program, DB InfraGO AG and understandAI GmbH have developed a comprehensive multi- sensor dataset tailored to the needs of railway environment perception. This dataset contains over 7 million high-quality annotations of both railway-specific and general perception objects, captured under varying operational scenarios. The finalized dataset can now be requested at the DB InfraGO AG and serve as a valuable resource for advancing AI-driven environment monitoring in the railway domain.

</details>


### [110] [YOLOv14:Unified Cross-Domain Real-Time Object Detectionwith Adaptive Multi-View Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04720)
*Jinling Jia,Jian Lu,Jone Yawl,Chenbin Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: YOLOv14是一个统一的目标检测框架，通过四项协同创新（可变形区域注意力、游戏到真实域适应、多视角条件注入和自适应增强策略）解决鱼眼畸变、游戏渲染角色、航拍视角和360°全景等非理想输入下的检测性能退化问题，在COCO val2017上达到49.1 mAP、2.91ms推理速度，并在各类非理想基准上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有实时目标检测器在受控条件下表现优异，但在非理想输入（鱼眼畸变、游戏渲染、航拍视角、360°全景）下精度显著退化，传统刚性检测框架缺乏对这些复杂几何和分布偏移的自适应能力。

Method: YOLOv14包含四项核心创新：(1) 可变形区域注意力（D-AAttn），用学得的2D变形场替代刚性注意力网格，实现几何畸变下的自适应采样；(2) Game2Real域适应，通过AdaIN和对抗域混淆对齐游戏渲染与真实照片的特征分布；(3) 多视角条件注入，将学得的视角嵌入注入骨干网络，并采用跨视角对比损失拉近不同视角同类特征；(4) 自适应增强策略，自动分类输入场景类型并路由至最优增强，配合动态尺度路由器学习逐输入特征金字塔权重。

Result: 在COCO val2017上达到49.1 mAP，T4 GPU上推理仅2.91ms；在鱼眼（+4.1 mAP）、全景（+6.6 mAP）、无人机（+6.4 mAP）和游戏角色（+26.1 mAP）基准上均获得显著提升。

Conclusion: YOLOv14通过可变形注意力、域适应、多视角学习和自适应增强策略的协同设计，成功构建了一个能够统一应对多种非理想输入场景的实时检测框架，在保持高速度的同时大幅提升了跨域和畸变条件下的检测鲁棒性。

Abstract: Real-time object detectors achieve remarkable accuracy under controlled conditions, yet degrade sharply on non-ideal inputs: fisheye distortion, game-rendered characters, aerial viewpoints, and 360° panoramas. We present YOLOv14, aunified detection framework addressing these challenges through four synergisticinnovations. (1) Deformable Area-Attention (D-AAttn) replaces rigid attentiongrids with learned 2D deformation fields, enabling adaptive sampling under geometric distortion. (2) Game2Real Domain Adaptation aligns rendered-game and photographic feature distributions via Adaptive Instance Normalization (AdaIN)and adversarial domain confusion, allowing game characters are detected as realhumans. (3) Multi-View Conditioning injects learned viewpoint embeddings intothe backbone with a cross-view contrastive loss that pulls same-class features fromdifferent perspectives closer. (4) An Adaptive Augmentation Policy automaticallyclassifies each input' scene type and routes to optimal augmentations, while a DynamicScaleRouter learns per-input feature pyramid weights. Together, YOLOv14achieves 49.1 mAP on COCO val2017 at 2.91 ms (T4 GPU), and delivers substantial gains on fisheye (+4.1 mAP), panorama (+6.6 mAP), drone (+6.4 mAP), andgame-character (+26.1 mAP) benchmarks

</details>


### [111] [Towards Physics of Multimodal Pretraining: Knowledge Flow, Modality Synergy, Early Unification, and Recipes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05000)
*Junlin Han,Shengbang Tong,David Fan,Minghao Chen,Philip Torr,Filippos Kokkinos,Mike Lewis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过系统性的控制实验，在合成数据和真实大规模数据上探索了多模态预训练的设计空间与内在机制，提出四项关键发现：模态间知识流动的规律与不对称性、数据复杂度决定模态协同或竞争、早期统一训练优于后期对齐（并揭示"视觉懒惰"现象），以及仅需5%计算预算的高效预训练配方。研究在13.5B MoE模型、2T token规模上验证了这些发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管视觉是推进基础模型统一多模态预训练的关键方向，但多模态统一训练中的设计空间以及模态间交互的根本机制仍未被充分探索。作者旨在通过实证研究填补这一空白，为理解和规模化多模态预训练提供原则性基础。

Method: 采用受控实验方法，在合成数据集和大规模真实数据集上进行系统性探索，解耦语言、视觉理解与视觉生成之间的知识迁移方向与不对称性；分析数据复杂性与架构选择（共享注意力、归一化、模态专属前馈层）对模态协同/竞争的影响；对比早期统一、后期对齐与顺序训练等不同策略；据此推导高效预训练配方，并在13.5B参数MoE模型（2T token）上进行规模化验证。

Result: ①揭示了模态间知识流的不同模式与不对称影响；②证明数据复杂度决定模态是协同还是竞争，并识别出促进协同的架构选择（共享注意力+归一化+模态专属FFN），该行为在不同视觉tokenizer下具有泛化性；③发现早期统一训练优于后期对齐，并发现"视觉懒惰"现象——延迟整合会导致模型过度依赖语言先验；④推导出仅需5%计算预算即可获得强生成性能的预训练配方，并在13.5B MoE、2T token规模上得到验证。

Conclusion: 该研究为多模态预训练的"物理机制"提供了实证清晰的系统性理解，给出了可复用的高效训练配方，为后续多模态基础模型的架构设计与训练策略提供了原则性指导。

Abstract: Vision offers a critical axis for advancing foundation models, driving a shift towards natively unified multimodal pretraining. Despite this momentum, the design space and the fundamental mechanisms of how modalities interact during unified training remain underexplored. We provide empirical clarity through a systematic exploration of multimodal pretraining. Our controlled experiments on both synthetic and large-scale real-world datasets yield four key insights into the physics of multimodal pretraining: (i) Knowledge Flow: We disentangle how language, visual understanding, and visual generation transfer knowledge across modalities, revealing distinct patterns of influence and asymmetry; (ii) Synergy vs. Competition: We show that data "complexity" largely determines whether modalities are synergistic, identify architectural choices that promote synergy: such as shared attention and normalization with modality-specific feed-forward layers, and find that these behaviors generalize across different visual tokenizer designs; (iii) Early Unification: Unifying modalities from the very early stages and training them jointly is shown to be more effective than late alignment or sequential training. This process uncovers a vision laziness phenomenon, where delayed integration leads models to rely on language priors; (iv) Recipes: We derive efficient pretraining recipes that achieve strong generative performance using only 5% of the compute budget. These core findings are subsequently validated at scale by training multiple 13.5B MoE models on 2T tokens. We hope this study provides a principled foundation for understanding and scaling multimodal pretraining.

</details>


### [112] [Multi-View Face and Gesture Animation with Dynamic Gaussians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04722)
*Alireza Javanmardi,Vippin Kumar Jeetmal,Christen Millerdurai,Alain Pagani,Didier Stricker*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MVFGA，一种多视角一致的上半身虚拟人重建管线，将面部和手部分别建模并与参数化上半身网格融合，再通过3D高斯溅射实现高质量动态渲染，同时引入多视角上半身动作捕捉数据集MVFGA-MoCap。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么仅关注头部而忽略手部手势，要么重建全身但无法保真面部细节和手部姿态精度，难以捕捉自然人际交流中关键的面部表情和手势的细微动态，亟需一种能同时保持面部与手部精度的上半身虚拟人重建方法。

Method: 1) 将面部和手部分别建模，融合到参数化上半身网格模型中，以捕获细粒度面部表情和手部姿态；2) 将3D高斯溅射到所得网格上，实现动态虚拟人从新视角的高质量渲染；3) 引入MVFGA-MoCap多视角上半身动作捕捉数据集，包含受控面部表情序列、多样手势和自由交流场景。

Result: 实验表明MVFGA能生成具有高保真面部表情和手部动作的逼真虚拟人，在上半身虚拟人动画任务上优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过将面部与手部独立建模并与上半身网格融合，再结合3D高斯溅射渲染，MVFGA有效解决了一体化建模中面部与手部精度不足的问题，为自然交流场景下的上半身虚拟人重建提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Creating photorealistic 3D human avatars with realistic upper-body motion remains challenging. Existing approaches either focus on the head and overlook hand gestures, or reconstruct the full body but fail to preserve fine-grained facial fidelity and hand pose accuracy. As a result, current methods struggle to capture the subtle dynamics of facial expressions and hand gestures that are crucial for natural human communication. While methods based on full-body parametric models enable avatar reconstruction from monocular or multi-view inputs, they often lack accurate facial animation and detailed hand articulation. To address these limitations, we propose MVFGA, a novel multi-view-consistent pipeline for generating realistic upper-body avatars. Our approach models the face and hands separately and fuses them with a parametric upper-body mesh model, enabling the capture of fine-grained facial expressions and hand poses for accurate upper-body avatar reconstruction. We then splat 3D Gaussians onto the obtained mesh, enabling high-quality rendering of dynamic avatars from novel viewpoints. Furthermore, we introduce MVFGA-MoCap, a multi-view upper-body motion capture dataset featuring controlled facial expression sequences, diverse hand gestures, and free-form communication. Experiments show that MVFGA generates visually realistic avatars with high-fidelity facial expressions and hand motions, outperforming baselines for upper-body avatar animation. Project page: https://dfki-av.github.io/MVFGA/

</details>


### [113] [Dense Metric Depth Completion from Sparse Direct Time-of-Flight Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04737)
*Hakyeong Kim,Ruicheng Wang,Chengtang Yao,Jiaolong Yang,Min H. Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个通用框架，用于从稀疏直接飞行时间（dToF）传感器测量中完成密集度量深度补全。模型采用深度引导的双分支Vision Transformer编码器，分别处理RGB图像和稀疏dToF测量，并通过掩码联合注意力机制让深度token引导图像特征。同时引入全面的dToF仿真管线来生成训练数据。该模型完全在合成数据上训练，在6个数据集和3个真实dToF设备上实现了强大的零样本泛化能力，在准确性和计算效率上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: dToF传感器虽然能提供高精度度量深度，但制造成本高、光电二极管阵列规模受限，导致深度图极其稀疏、低分辨率且有噪声，难以直接用于VR/XR、机器人和3D感知等需要密集度量深度的任务。现有的单目和深度补全方法无法有效处理dToF设备的独特采样模式与硬件伪影，在严重稀疏或噪声条件下性能显著下降。因此需要一种能跨传感器类型、稀疏度和噪声条件泛化的密集深度补全框架。

Method: 提出深度引导的双分支Vision Transformer编码器，分别处理RGB图像和稀疏dToF测量；引入掩码联合注意力模块，使深度token可靠地引导图像特征而不被覆盖；采用轻量级解码器高效重建密集度量深度，无需基于扩散或精修的后处理。为缓解配对训练数据稀缺问题，设计了综合dToF仿真管线，模拟flash、sub-VGA flash和旋转传感器特性，包括硬件退化、不规则稀疏性和真实噪声分布。

Result: 模型完全在合成数据上训练，在6个数据集和3个真实dToF设备上实现了强零样本泛化能力，在准确性和计算效率两方面均超越现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 该工作为稀疏直接ToF传感器的密集度量深度补全提供了鲁棒且实用的解决方案，代码和模型已开源。

Abstract: Direct Time-of-Flight (dToF) sensors provide highly accurate metric depth and are more robust than indirect ToF systems in challenging real-world conditions. However, their high manufacturing cost and limited photodiode array size produce depth maps that are extremely sparse, low-resolution, and noisy, making them unsuitable for VR/XR, robotics, and 3D perception tasks that require dense metric depth. Existing monocular and depth completion methods struggle to handle the unique sampling patterns and hardware artifacts of dToF devices, and their performance often deteriorates significantly under severe sparsity or noise. We present a generalizable framework for dense metric depth completion from sparse dToF measurements, capable of operating across diverse sensor types, sparsity levels, and noise conditions. Our model employs a depth-guided dual-branch Vision Transformer encoder that processes RGB images and sparse dToF measurements separately, while a masked joint attention module allows depth tokens to reliably guide image features without being overwritten by them. A lightweight decoder reconstructs dense metric depth efficiently, without diffusion-based or refinement-heavy post-processing. To address the scarcity of paired training data, we introduce a comprehensive dToF simulation pipeline that reproduces the characteristics of flash, sub-VGA flash, and rotating sensors, including hardware-induced degradation, irregular sparsity, and realistic noise distributions. Trained entirely on synthetic data, our model achieves strong zero-shot generalization across 6 datasets and 3 real dToF devices, outperforming state-of-the-art approaches in both accuracy and computational efficiency. This establishes a robust and practical solution for dense metric depth completion from sparse direct ToF sensors. Our code and models are open-sourced. See https://vclab.kaist.ac.kr/cvpr2026p3.

</details>


### [114] [Simile Understanding in Text-to-Image Models: An Evaluation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04750)
*Luecheng Wang,Shintaro Ozaki,Hidetaka Kamigaito,Katsuhiko Hayashi,Jingun Kwon,Manabu Okumura,Taro Watanabe*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个针对文本到图像（t2i）模型中明喻理解能力的可扩展评估框架，通过受控数据集、基于YOLO的自动接地度量和Diffusion Lens文本编码器层分析，系统性地揭示了前沿t2i模型在明喻理解中普遍存在的"字面化"失败模式，并探讨了改进策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 明喻是文本提示中紧凑而富有表现力的视觉描述方式，但即使是最前沿的文本到图像模型也经常误解隐喻本体（vehicle），将其与描述对象混淆。这种系统性失败揭示了t2i模型在比喻语言与对象级视觉接地之间存在差距，但目前缺乏系统性的评估框架来量化这一问题。

Method: 提出了一个三部分的可扩展评估框架：(1) 受控明喻数据集——隐喻本体从预定义的可检测对象类别中抽取，并与多样化模板组合；(2) 基于YOLO目标检测的自动接地度量指标；(3) 使用Diffusion Lens进行文本编码器层分析，以追踪隐喻本体在生成过程中的涌现方式。

Result: 在多种架构不同的t2i模型上进行的实验揭示了一致的字面化失败模式（literalization failure patterns），表明明喻中隐喻本体的理解失败是跨模型的普适性问题。

Conclusion: 本文提出的评估框架能够系统性地量化t2i模型对明喻的理解能力，并针对明喻接地（simile grounding）问题讨论了潜在的缓解策略，为改善t2i模型的比喻语言理解指明了方向。

Abstract: Similes provide a compact and expressive way to describe visual characteristics in text prompts. Recent text-to-image models (t2i models) can produce visually compelling outputs from simile prompts, yet even frontier models frequently misinterpret the metaphorical vehicle and confuse it with the object. These systematic failures reveal a gap between figurative language and object-level visual grounding in t2i models. To investigate this issue, we propose a scalable evaluation framework for simile understanding. Our framework includes (1) a controlled simile dataset in which metaphorical vehicles are drawn from a predefined set of object-detectable categories and combined with diverse templates, (2) automatic grounding metrics based on YOLO (You Only Look Once) detection, and (3) text encoder layer analysis using Diffusion Lens to track how metaphorical vehicles emerge during generation. Experiments across architecturally diverse t2i models reveal consistent literalization failure patterns. We further discuss potential mitigation strategies for improving simile grounding in t2i models.

</details>


### [115] [Robust and Efficient Motion Reasoning for Privacy-Aware Classroom Incident Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05115)
*Paritosh Parmar,Landy Lan,Hong Yang,Chen Yi,Chiat Pin Tay*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向隐私保护课堂安全监控的轻量级运动推理框架，基于生成式CCTV风格视频与真实姿态数据的混合基准，通过层次运动学表示和从大模型到小模型的知识蒸馏，在十分之一计算成本下超越大型基线模型，并展现出更强的跨域泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 课堂事故识别在实际部署中面临隐私保护、计算效率和泛化能力三大挑战，而现有研究在此场景下缺乏公开基准和专门方法。作者观察到许多课堂事件在运动方向、速度、加速度和强度上比单纯姿态更有区分度，因此希望构建兼顾隐私与效率的轻量级方案。

Method: 方法包含三个关键组件：(1) 提出混合基准，结合生成式CCTV风格视频与真实课堂姿态数据；(2) 构建人类动作的层次运动学表示；(3) 通过知识蒸馏，将大型教师模型中的多层次多阶运动推理能力压缩到小型单阶学生模型中，实现高效的逐人推理。

Result: 实验表明所提模型在不到十分之一计算成本的情况下超越了明显更大的基线模型，同时展现出更强的域外运动推理能力和零样本合成到真实的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 研究验证了隐私感知且计算高效的课堂事故识别方案的可行性，并将在公开发布基准、代码库及辅助工具，以促进隐私保护课堂安全领域的进一步研究。

Abstract: Can computer vision help make classrooms safer? In this pilot study, we investigate privacy-aware and computationally efficient classroom incident recognition from CCTV-style observations. This setting remains underexplored, with limited benchmarks and few methods designed for the privacy, efficiency, and generalization demands of real-world deployment. We introduce a novel hybrid benchmark combining generative CCTV-style videos with real-world classroom pose data, and propose a lightweight, but robust motion-reasoning framework motivated by the observation that many incidents differ more in motion direction, speed, acceleration, and intensity than in pose alone. To that end, our method first constructs hierarchical kinematic representations of human actions. Our method then distills hierarchical, multi-order kinematic reasoning from a large teacher into a much smaller single-order student, enabling efficient per-person inference while preserving expressive motion understanding. Experiments show that our model outperforms substantially larger baselines at less than one-tenth of their computational cost, while also demonstrating stronger out-of-domain motion reasoning and zero-shot synthetic-to-real generalization. We will publicly release the benchmark, codebase, and supporting tools to facilitate further research in privacy-aware classroom safety.

</details>


### [116] [Revisiting Pose Sensitivity in Splat-based Computed Tomography under Sparse-view Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04752)
*Kiseok Choi,Hyeongjun Cho,Inchul Kim,Min H. Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对基于3D高斯溅射的锥束稀疏视角CT重建在真实系统中因采集几何位姿误差而产生严重条纹伪影的问题，通过分析发现伪影主要源于位姿不准确而非视角稀疏性，并提出了一种稳定的梯度框架，在重建过程中联合细化几何参数，显著提升重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管溅射式层析成像在锥束稀疏视角CT中展现了高质量重建和快速收敛的优势，但在真实CT系统（视角有限且非均匀分布）中会出现比传统重建方法更明显的条纹和条带伪影。作者通过详细分析发现这些伪影主要源于采集几何中的位姿不准确，而非视角稀疏本身，因此需要解决几何失准带来的脆弱性问题。

Method: 作者重新审视了溅射公式中的位姿敏感性问题，推导出一个稳定的基于梯度的框架，在重建过程中联合细化几何参数。该框架分析了位姿扰动如何通过可微投影算子传播，并揭示了溅射式CT对几何失准特别脆弱的根本原因。该方案轻量且易于集成到现有管线中。

Result: 所提出的联合位姿细化框架显著改善了真实世界稀疏视角条件下的重建保真度，有效抑制了由位姿误差引起的条纹和条带伪影。

Conclusion: 该工作不仅识别了溅射式CT中位姿误差传播的机制，还提供了轻量、易集成的解决方案，使溅射式层析成像在实际CT系统中具备更强的鲁棒性，弥合了理想仿真与真实部署之间的差距。

Abstract: X-ray computed tomography (CT) reconstructs volumetric representations of objects from projection images obtained by transmitting X-rays through a target. Recent splat-based tomography, which represents a volume as a continuous distribution of 3D Gaussians, has demonstrated both high reconstruction quality and fast convergence in cone-beam sparse-view CT. However, when deployed in real CT systems with limited and non-uniform view distributions, we observe distinctive streak and strip artifacts that are far more pronounced than in conventional reconstruction methods. Through detailed analysis, we show that these artifacts primarily originate from pose inaccuracies in the acquisition geometry rather than from view sparsity itself. We revisit pose sensitivity in the splatting formulation and derive a stable gradient-based framework that jointly refines geometric parameters during reconstruction. Our study not only identifies how pose perturbations propagate through the differentiable projection operator but also reveals why splat-based CT is particularly vulnerable to geometric misalignment. The resulting formulation remains lightweight and easily integrable into existing pipelines while substantially improving reconstruction fidelity under real-world sparse-view conditions.

</details>


### [117] [Predicting Brain Morphometry with MT-GNN: Mesh Evolution in Continuous Time with Graph-Based Metric Tensor Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05132)
*Hao Ding,Daniel Semchin,Paul M. Thompson,Boris Gutman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MT-GNN，一种基于图神经网络的连续时间网格演变预测模型，通过预测每个顶点的第一基本形式（度量张量）并结合可微ARAP求解器解码为表面，实现对皮层下结构形状演变的端到端预测。在ADNI数据集的14个皮层下结构上，MT-GNN在所有预测时间范围内均优于现有方法（相比时间均值降低2.29%平均顶点误差，击败全部14/14结构）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从少量先前的扫描预测皮层下结构形状的演变，对预后判断和临床试验富集具有重要意义。已有的纵向量网格预测方法要么通过高维嵌入外推形状轨迹，要么直接回归顶点形变，但都未充分利用表面内蕴几何信息，且预测精度有限。

Method: 提出一种单图网络（每个结构一个），在连续时间内预测表面的内蕴几何——即每个顶点随时间的第二基本形式（度量张量）。网络以任意的多时间访问历史为输入，以傅里叶编码的提前时间量为条件，预测任意时间范围的未来逐顶点度量。预测的度量通过可微的刚性保持（As-Rigid-As-Possible, ARAP）求解器解码为表面，模型以刚体对齐的顶点误差为损失进行端到端训练。

Result: 在ADNI数据集的14个皮层下结构上，MT-GNN在每个预测时间范围上均取得最优性能：相比时间均值降低2.29%的平均顶点误差（p=6.1×10⁻⁵），在14/14结构上全部胜出；优于测地形状回归方法DCM（-0.19%）和网格Transformer方法TransforMesh（-0.45%；p=1.2×10⁻⁴），且优势随预测时间范围增大而扩大。

Conclusion: 通过重建进行训练可以保证解码后的预测始终是有效的表面，并持续提升预测质量。将表面内蕴几何（度量张量）作为预测目标，相比直接回归顶点位置或使用高维嵌入外推，在纵向形状演变预测任务上更具优势。

Abstract: Predicting how a subcortical structure's shape will evolve from a few prior scans could support prognosis and clinical-trial enrichment. Existing longitudinal mesh predictors either extrapolate shape trajectories via high-dimensional embeddings or regress vertex deformations directly. We instead predict the surface's intrinsic geometry in continuous time: a single per-structure graph network predicts the future per-vertex first fundamental form (metric tensor) for an arbitrary causal multiple-visit history and an arbitrary prediction horizon, conditioned on a Fourier encoding of the lead time. The predicted metric is decoded into a surface by a differentiable As-Rigid-As-Possible solver, and the model is trained end-to-end on the rigid-aligned vertex error. Training through the reconstruction keeps the decoded prediction a valid surface and consistently improves it. On 14 subcortical structures from the ADNI dataset, the proposed mesh evolution model (MT-GNN) predicts best among the evaluated methods at every horizon ($-2.29\%$ mean vertex error vs. the temporal mean, $p{=}6.1{\times}10^{-5}$, beating it on 14/14 structures), ahead of geodesic shape regression (DCM, $-0.19\%$) and a mesh transformer (TransforMesh, $-0.45\%$; $p{=}1.2{\times}10^{-4}$), with the lead widening as the horizon grows.

</details>


### [118] [Trace, Verify, and Correct: A Training-Free Framework for Spatial Reasoning in Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04759)
*Yang Yang,Jiawei Chen,Tairan Chen,Zhaoxia Yin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个模块化、免训练的空间推理验证与修正框架，通过构建空间证据图（SEG）和空间证据可靠性评估（SERA），识别与大模型输入图像矛盾的中间推理步骤，并引导模型修正后续推理链，在15个模型-数据集组合下平均准确率达68.94%，优于基线8.55个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在空间推理中可能产生与输入图像不一致的中间判断，错误会在推理链中传播并影响最终答案。现有方法通过训练或额外空间信息提升空间推理能力，但未考虑推理过程本身对模型输入是否忠实。
