Many of these weaknesses are categorized under the Common Weakness Enumeration (CWE) framework and include improper access control, exposure of sensitive information, and unintended privilege escalation. To improve the detection of such vulnerabilities, we propose a methodology that leverages a Large Language Model (LLM) to identify potential hardware CWEs directly from hardware designs in Verilog. The proposed approach is evaluated iteratively on a dataset of single-module Verilog designs to assess its effectiveness in detecting hardware security weaknesses. Our results demonstrate the potential of LLMs to augment traditional hardware security analysis by providing automated, scalable assistance for identifying security vulnerabilities during the hardware design process.

</details>


### [185] [Toward Practical Decentralized Proof-of-Location via Physical Witnessing Zones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04957)
*Tamor Tomson,Eduardo Brito,Amnir Hadachi,Ulrich Norbisrath*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文基于去中心化位置证明（PoL）的理论架构，构建了一个低成本硬件的物理原型系统，通过见证者节点测量、本地网格交换和防篡改账本记录，实现了可验证、抗重放和抗恶意测距攻击的物理位置证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字服务日益依赖位置声明，但自我报告的位置数据容易被伪造且难以事后验证。现有PoL系统多停留在理论层面，缺乏物理原型来验证其在真实无线、网络和时间条件下的可行性。

Method: 构建一个见证区域，其中固定的邻近设备负责测量验证者的存在性，通过本地网状网络交换位置声明，并以防篡改账本记录。将抽象协议适配到物理约束，采用了见证者发起的测距、跨见证者一致性检查和新鲜性绑定的抗重放机制。

Result: 受控室内评估表明，系统能够生成准确、低延迟的证明对象，同时有效检测模拟重放攻击和恶意测距攻击。

Conclusion: 该原型为下一代数字信任基础设施提供了可复用的实验基线，并揭示了去中心化位置证据仍面临的校准、验证者独立性、无线电完整性和规模化扩展等剩余需求。

Abstract: Digital services increasingly rely on claims that a person, device, or asset was in a specific place at a specific time. Today, those claims often depend on self-reported location data, which is easy to falsify and difficult to verify after the fact. Proof-of-Location (PoL) systems address this gap by turning presence claims into evidence that an independent verifier can later inspect. This paper builds upon recent theoretical work on decentralized PoL architectures and demonstrates how they can move from emulation to a physical prototype built with low-cost hardware. We implement a witnessing zone in which fixed nearby devices measure a prover's presence, exchange claims over a local mesh, and record them in a tamper-evident ledger. Building the prototype required adapting the abstract protocol to physical constraints through witness-initiated ranging, cross-witness consistency checks, and freshness binding against replay. Our controlled indoor evaluation shows that the system can produce accurate, low-latency proof objects while detecting simulated replay and malicious-ranging attacks. The result is a reusable experimental baseline for next-generation digital trust infrastructure, that exposes the remaining calibration, verifier-independence, radio-integrity, and scaling requirements for decentralized location evidence, where physical presence claims can be independently checked under real radio, networking, and timing conditions.

</details>


### [186] [When Do PEFT Adaptations Leak Structure? Measuring Black-Box Structural Bounds in Public-Base Model Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05036)
*Zhongjiang Yao,Shuangshuang Liang,Chun Yang,LiWei Chen,Gang Shi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出VectorHijack-SR，一种量化测量方法，用于评估部署了参数高效微调（PEFT）适配器的公共基础模型服务的信息泄漏风险。该方法将受害服务与基础模型的残差转换为对PEFT家族、层局部性和秩的结构化校准界，并通过服务级分类器和分层拒绝器进行验证。实验表明在多组任务上结构泄漏显著，版本链接AUC达0.940，但存在可见性-可利用性差距（后验选择PEFT表现不佳，自由运行生成接近随机）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 服务日益通过私有PEFT适配器部署公共基础模型，审计者或攻击者可以本地运行公共基础模型并观察受害者输出，从而产生差分信息泄漏风险。需要一种系统的测量方法量化这种结构信息的泄漏程度及其实际可利用性。

Method: VectorHijack-SR将成对的受害/基础模型残差转换为PEFT家族、层局部性和粗秩的校准结构界。聚合查询级幅度、排名、熵、边界、长度、模板、局部性和频谱统计为服务级表示。使用服务不相交分类器量化结构证据，并使用交叉拟合分层拒绝器评估受害者是否位于校准的LoRA流形之外。实验覆盖BERT、RoBERTa和DeBERTa-v3骨干，在MNLI和AG News任务上评估。

Result: 分类骨干上家族泄漏显著优于均匀随机机会，如RoBERTa/MNLI达21/24。秩推断任务依赖，BERT/MNLI和DeBERTa/AG News达8/9，而DeBERTa/MNLI仅4/9且经校正后与机会兼容。十种子BERT开集网格上，拒绝器池化AUROC 0.804，已知准确性0.956。五个保留LoRA-r64服务的版本链接AUC达0.940。但后验选择PEFT性能（0.356）低于蒸馏-转换PEFT（0.517），自由运行生成仍接近机会。

Conclusion: 已知基础模型、富输出的PEFT服务可以泄漏可操作的结构和版本信息，但封闭集置信度本身不能建立通用适配器恢复能力。存在显著的可视性-可利用性差距：即使检测到结构泄漏，实际利用（查询节省、适配器恢复、生成操纵）仍然受限。

Abstract: Services increasingly deploy public foundation models with private parameter-efficient adaptations, creating a differential information leakage risk when auditors or adversaries can execute the public base model locally and observe victim outputs. We present VectorHijack-SR, a measurement methodology that converts paired victim/base residuals into calibrated structural bounds over PEFT family, layer locality, and coarse rank, while separating metadata visibility from open-world validity and operational exploitability. Our estimator aggregates query-level magnitude, ranking, entropy, margin, length, template, locality, and spectral statistics into service-level representations. A service-disjoint classifier quantifies structural evidence, and a cross-fitted hierarchical rejector evaluates whether a victim lies outside the calibrated LoRA manifold. Across classification backbones, family leakage exceeds uniform chance on BERT/MNLI (8/12), RoBERTa/MNLI (21/24), and DeBERTa-v3 on MNLI (12/18) and AG News (15/18). Rank inference is task dependent: BERT/MNLI and DeBERTa/AG News reach 8/9, whereas DeBERTa/MNLI achieves 4/9 and is statistically compatible with chance after correction. On a ten-seed BERT open-set grid, the rejector achieves pooled AUROC 0.804 (95% CI [0.660, 0.927]) and known accuracy 0.956, but remains limited on structurally close DoRA and LoRA+head variants. Exact-version linkage on five held-out LoRA-r64 services reaches AUC 0.940. Experiments reveal a visibility-exploitability gap: two-stage recovery provides no fair-budget query savings, posterior-selected PEFT underperforms distill-then-convert PEFT (0.356 vs. 0.517), and free-running generation remains near chance. These results show that known-base, rich-output PEFT services can leak actionable structural and version information, while closed-set confidence alone does not establish universal adapter recovery.

</details>


### [187] [Private Direct Preference Optimization for LLM Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05040)
*Yangfan Jiang,Fei Wei,Ergute Bao,Xiaokui Xiao,Yaliang Li,Bolin Ding*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出偏好隐私（Preference Privacy）概念，并设计PrivDPO方法，通过仅沿一维偏好轴添加校准随机噪声来保护DPO训练中的相对偏好信号，在三个对齐基准和三个LLM家族上实现了优于隐私保护基线的隐私-效用权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DPO已是大语言模型对齐的标准方法，但每个训练样本中的相对偏好反映了标注者或最终用户的主观判断，可能泄露敏感属性。现有的隐私保护方法（如通用DP）不适配这一特定结构，导致不必要的噪声注入和有偏的梯度更新。因此需要一种专门针对偏好信号设计的、与大规模LLM训练兼容的隐私形式。

Method: PrivDPO基于一个关键观察：仅偏好信号不同的相邻样本，其梯度差位于由文本决定的单一维度"偏好轴"上，所有偏好信息都通过该轴流动。PrivDPO通过对DPO目标函数进行无偏随机重缩放，仅沿这个轴添加校准随机噪声，避免了逐样本梯度运算，从而与大规<｜place▁holder▁no▁｜｜DSML｜｜｜>模LLM训练兼容并保持无偏性。

Result: 在三个对齐基准和三个LLM家族上的实验表明，PrivDPO相比隐私保护基线持续取得了更好的隐私-效用权衡。

Conclusion: PrivDPO形式化了偏好隐私（一种label-DP风格的隐私概念），开创性地只保护候选响应之间的相对偏好而非整个样本，并通过一维噪声注入实现高效的隐私保护DPO训练，在隐私与模型效用之间取得了更优的平衡。

Abstract: Direct preference optimization (DPO) is now a standard method for aligning large language models (LLMs) using human preference data. Each DPO example contains a prompt and a pair of candidate model responses. While prompts and responses are often public or model-generated, the relative preference between responses reflects subjective judgments and can reveal sensitive attributes of annotators or end users. Off-the-shelf privacy-preserving approaches are not well matched to this structure, leading to unnecessary noise injection and biased updates in training.
  In this paper, we formalize preference privacy, a label-DP-style privacy notion for DPO that protects only the relative preference between candidate responses, assuming an adversary who already knows the prompt and responses. We then design PrivDPO, a DPO variant that enforces preference privacy while remaining compatible with large-scale LLM training. Our main observation is that, for neighboring examples differing only in their preference signal, the gradient difference lies on a one-dimensional preference axis determined solely by the text; all preference information flows through this axis. PrivDPO adds calibrated randomness only along this axis via an unbiased randomized rescaling of the DPO objective, avoiding per-example gradient operations. Our experiments on three alignment benchmarks and three LLM families show that PrivDPO consistently achieves strong privacy-utility trade-offs compared with privacy-preserving baselines.

</details>


### [188] [Gradient Immunity: Null-Space Resistance to Malicious Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05045)
*Yuxuan Huang,Xingyu Zeng,Tianhang Zheng,Chaochao Lu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种单向安全门（USG），通过空空间三次层和逆适配器的组合，在开源权重发布阶段保护模型权重，使下游微调时有害样本的梯度被抑制，从而在不影响正常功能的前提下抵御恶意微调攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的对齐大型语言模型在发布后仍易受恶意下游微调攻击。已有防御手段要么针对微调即服务（FTaaS）范式设计，要么依赖下游用户主动遵守安全协议，无法应对提供方控制的"部分保护开放权重"（PPOW）发布场景，即大部分权重可训练而少量安全关键组件被保护的场景。

Method: 提出单向安全门（USG），由Null Space Cubic Layer和Inverse Adapter组成，插入在最后一个Transformer层之后。立方层在微调过程中对落入校准保护区域的有害样本隐藏状态进行梯度抑制或阻断，Inverse Adapter则恢复基础模型的前向行为。使用防御方持有的有害数据校准阈值，使防护能泛化到邻近分布的有害样本。

Result: 在六个模型-数据集设置下，USG在固定发布阈值下将微调后攻击成功率保持在发布前水平附近，同时在较简单设置上维持高安全通过率，在BeaverTails的不安全样本上展现出更清晰的安全-效用权衡。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，发布时的表示空间阻断可以提高恶意下游适配的成本，且无需下游用户配合，为开放权重模型的安全发布提供了一种有效方案。

Abstract: Released aligned large language models remain vulnerable to malicious downstream finetuning. Existing defenses are largely designed for the fine-tuning-as-a-service (FTaaS) paradigm or rely on downstream users to follow additional safety procedures, and therefore do not directly address the setting we study: a provider controlled partially protected open-weight (PPOW) release setting in which most weights remain trainable while a small safety-critical component is preserved at release. We propose a Unidirectional Safety Gate (USG), instantiated as a Null Space Cubic Layer together with an Inverse Adapter inserted after the final Transformer layer. During downstream fine-tuning, the cubic layer suppresses or blocks gradients from harmful samples whose hidden states fall in a calibrated protected region, while the Inverse Adapter restores the base model's forward behavior. In practice, we calibrate a threshold using defender-held harmful data, allowing protection to generalize to nearby in-distribution harmful samples. Across six evaluated model-dataset settings, USG keeps post-finetuning attack success rate close to the pre-release level under a fixed release threshold, while maintaining high safe-pass rates on easier settings and exhibiting a clearer safety-utility trade-off on unsafe samples from BeaverTails. These results suggest that release-time representation-space blocking can raise the cost of malicious downstream adaptation without requiring downstream cooperation. The code is available at https://github.com/OpenCausaLab/Gradient-Immunity.

</details>


### [189] [Kerckhoffs-Compliant Watermarking for Physical Design IP Protection: From Placement to Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05055)
*Andrew B. Kahng,Yiting Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PDMarks是一种符合Kerckhoffs原则的物理设计(PD)IP水印框架，其安全性仅依赖于32字节密钥。它通过HMAC-SHA256在布局、时钟树综合和布线等多个PD阶段嵌入所有权证据，并集成到OpenROAD-flow-scripts中。实验表明其在NanGate45和ASAP7设计上优于现有方法，联合巧合概率低于10^{-32}，且具备抵御错误密钥和攻击的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理设计IP包含精心调优的布局、时钟分布和布线决策，是VLSI实现中的宝贵资产。随着PD工具的普及，未授权重用已布局布线数据库的问题日益突出。现有PD水印方法要么只保护单一PD阶段，要么依赖隐藏的构造细节，无法抵抗白盒攻击者。因此需要一种安全性仅依赖于密钥（而非算法保密）的多阶段水印框架。

Method: PDMarks采用Kerckhoffs原则设计的水印框架，核心方法包括：(1) 使用32字节密钥结合HMAC-SHA256确定性派生所有水印实例和目标值，支持一致的嵌入与验证流程；(2) 在多个PD阶段（布局、CTS、布线）同时嵌入所有权证据，实现跨阶段覆盖；(3) 集成到OpenROAD-flow-scripts工具链中实现实际落地。

Result: 在NanGate45和ASAP7设计上的实验表明，PDMarks优于现有的物理设计水印方法，提供更强有力的所有权证据，同时保持相当或更小的PPA开销。所有评估设计的近似联合全阶段巧合概率低于10^{-32}。错误密钥无法重现完整的所有权证明，削弱水印需要对受保护实现进行大面积扰动。

Conclusion: PDMarks验证了通过密钥驱动的多阶段水印方案可以同时满足强所有权证据和低PPA开销，其安全性完全建立在密钥保密而非构造细节保密之上，为解决物理设计IP盗用问题提供了稳健且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Physical design (PD) intellectual property (IP) is a valuable artifact of modern VLSI implementation. It includes optimized cell placement, clock distribution, and routing decisions produced by carefully tuned PD flows. As access to PD tools expands, unauthorized reuse of placed-and-routed databases becomes an increasing concern. Existing PD watermarking methods either protect only one PD stage or rely on hidden construction details, leaving them vulnerable to a white-box adversary. In this work, we develop PDMarks, a Kerckhoffs-compliant watermarking framework whose security depends only on a secret key. PDMarks embeds ownership evidence across multiple stages of the PD flow, including placement, clock tree synthesis (CTS), and routing. All watermark instances and target values are deterministically derived from a 32-byte secret key using HMAC-SHA256, enabling consistent embedding and verification. PDMarks has been integrated into OpenROAD-flow-scripts. Experiments on NanGate45 and ASAP7 designs show that PDMarks outperforms prior physical design watermarking methods by providing much stronger ownership evidence with comparable or smaller PPA overhead. The approximate joint all-stage coincidence probability is below 10^{-32} for every evaluated design. Wrong-key and attack evaluations further show that incorrect keys do not reproduce the complete ownership proof and that weakening the watermark requires broad perturbation of the protected implementation.

</details>


### [190] [Hardware Design and Security in the Era of Chiplets and LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05063)
*Johann Knechtel,Ozgur Sinanoglu,Paul V. Gratz,Ramesh Karri*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对半导体行业两大前沿——异构2.5D chiplet系统和LLM驱动的EDA流程——进行了统一的安全分析，涵盖从架构、逻辑到物理层面的攻击向量，并综述了相应的防御方法（包括2.5D分割制造与主动中介层的Ro T架构，以及针对LLM-EDA管道的原生威胁防御技术），最后探讨了LLM如何反哺硬件安全研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半导体行业正经历双重变革：向异构2.5D chiplet系统转型，以及将LLM集成到EDA流程中。虽然这些范式在良率、模块化、设计生产力等方面带来前所未有的收益，但它们从根本上扩大了硬件攻击面，亟需统一的分析框架来理解这些新威胁并制定防御策略。

Method: 本文采用统一分析方法，从架构、逻辑和物理三个层次系统性地综述针对chiplet系统（包括用于LLM加速的硬件栈）的攻击，以及对LLM驱动EDA管道的各类攻击。防御方面，针对chiplet系统推荐了基于2.5D分割制造和主动中介层的物理隔离可信根架构；针对LLM-EDA管道则先识别原生威胁，再综述最先进的防御技术。

Result: 文章综合呈现了chiplet系统与LLM-EDA流程两方面的攻击面全景，并提出相应防御方案：分割制造与主动中介层实现物理隔离的可信根，以及对LLM-EDA原生威胁的防御清单，同时指出LLM可被用于增强现代系统（含chiplet）的硬件安全。

Conclusion: 本文为chiplet系统与LLM驱动EDA流程提供了统一的安全分析视角，强调了在双重技术革命背景下硬件安全的重要性，并展示了LLM既能引入新威胁也能作为安全工具的双向角色。

Abstract: The semiconductor industry is undergoing a dual revolution: the shift toward heterogeneous 2.5D chiplet systems and the integration of Large Language Models (LLMs) into Electronic Design Automation (EDA) flows. While these paradigms offer unprecedented benefits in yield, modularity, design productivity, etc., they radically expand the hardware attack surface. This paper provides a unified analysis of these frontiers, ranging from attacks on chiplet systems (including hardware stacks for LLM acceleration) across architectural, logical, and physical levels, to various exploits against LLM-driven EDA pipelines. To secure chiplet systems, we review a powerful defense approach that leverages 2.5D split manufacturing and active interposers for physically isolated Root of Trust (RoT) architectures. To secure LLM-driven EDA pipelines, we first identify native threats and then review state-of-the-art defense techniques. Finally, we discuss how LLM systems can advance hardware security efforts for modern systems, including chiplets.

</details>


### [191] [Agent Against Agent: An Agentic System for Automatic Prompt Injection Red Teaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05108)
*Yanting Wang,Chenlong Yin,Runpeng Geng,Jinyuan Jia*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出PIMiner，一个用于提示注入红队的智能体系统。该系统在训练时通过一系列(数据集,目标模型)对构建策略库，测试时可将学到的策略库直接迁移到未见过的目标LLM，无需额外训练，且每个测试样本仅需少量查询（如10次）。实验表明PIMiner在IPIArena和AgentDojo基准上对Gemini-2.5-Pro、GPT-5.1和Claude-Sonnet-4.5等模型取得了较高的攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提示注入对LLM智能体构成重大安全风险，需要进行高效有效的红队测试来评估风险并收集防御训练数据。现有的最先进提示注入红队方法主要依赖强化学习（RL），但训练出的攻击模型往往对新的目标LLM泛化能力差，需要改进。

Method: PIMiner采用智能体系统架构：训练阶段在一系列(数据集,目标模型)对上训练，从零开始构建策略库；测试阶段将学到的策略库直接迁移至未见过的新目标LLM，无需额外训练，每个测试样本仅需约10次查询即可完成。

Result: 在IPIArena上，PIMiner对Gemini-2.5-Pro达到76.2% ASR，对GPT-5.1达到61.9% ASR，对Claude-Sonnet-4.5达到42.9% ASR；在AgentDojo上，对Gemini-2.5-Pro达到86.7% ASR，对GPT-5.1达到53.3% ASR，对Claude-Sonnet-4.5达到40.0% ASR。

Conclusion: PIMiner通过构建可迁移的策略库，克服了现有RL-based提示注入红队方法泛化性差的问题，在不需额外训练的情况下即可有效攻击新目标模型，展示了强大的跨模型迁移能力和查询效率。

Abstract: Prompt injection poses significant security risks to LLM agents. Efficient and effective red-teaming is therefore critical, both for evaluating these risks and for collecting training data to improve defenses. Existing state-of-the-art prompt injection red-teaming methods primarily rely on reinforcement learning (RL), producing attacker models that often generalize poorly to new target LLMs. In this work, we develop PIMiner, an agentic system for prompt injection red-teaming. During training, PIMiner is trained on a sequence of (dataset, target model) pairs and builds a strategy library from scratch. At test time, the learned strategy library can be directly transferred to a previously unseen target LLM without additional training. PIMiner requires only a small number of queries to a target agent (e.g., 10) per test sample. Experimental results demonstrate that PIMiner achieves strong performance. On IPIArena, it attains a 76.2% ASR against Gemini-2.5-Pro, 61.9% ASR against GPT-5.1, and 42.9% ASR against Claude-Sonnet-4.5. On AgentDojo, it achieves an 86.7% ASR against Gemini-2.5-Pro, 53.3% ASR against GPT-5.1, and 40.0% ASR against Claude-Sonnet-4.5.

</details>


### [192] [Temporal Context Awareness: A Defense Framework Against Multi-turn Manipulation Attacks on Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.15560)
*Prashant Kulkarni,Assaf Namer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出时间上下文感知（TCA）框架，用于防御针对大型语言模型的多轮对话操纵攻击，通过语义漂移分析、跨轮意图一致性验证和渐进式风险评分来检测并缓解复杂的对抗性对话模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型面临日益复杂的多轮对话操纵攻击，攻击者通过看似良性的对话轮次逐步构建上下文以规避安全措施。现有单轮检测方法无法有效利用对话的时间性特征，构成严重的安全漏洞。

Method: TCA框架包含三个核心组件：动态上下文嵌入分析、跨轮意图一致性验证和渐进式风险评分，持续分析对话中的语义漂移和演进模式以识别操纵行为。

Result: 在模拟对抗场景的初步评估中，TCA框架展示了识别传统检测技术经常遗漏的细微操纵模式的能力，并对多种多轮攻击向量及其演进过程进行了分析。

Conclusion: TCA框架是保护大型语言模型同时保持其在合法应用中效用的一个有前景的方向，凸显了对话式AI系统需要强健且上下文感知的防御机制，实现代码已开源供进一步研究。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly vulnerable to sophisticated multi-turn manipulation attacks, where adversaries strategically build context through seemingly benign conversational turns to circumvent safety measures and elicit harmful or unauthorized responses. These attacks exploit the temporal nature of dialogue to evade single-turn detection methods, representing a critical security vulnerability with significant implications for real-world deployments.
  This paper introduces the Temporal Context Awareness (TCA) framework, a novel defense mechanism designed to address this challenge by continuously analyzing semantic drift, cross-turn intention consistency and evolving conversational patterns. The TCA framework integrates dynamic context embedding analysis, cross-turn consistency verification, and progressive risk scoring to detect and mitigate manipulation attempts effectively. Preliminary evaluations on simulated adversarial scenarios demonstrate the framework's potential to identify subtle manipulation patterns often missed by traditional detection techniques, offering a much-needed layer of security for conversational AI systems. In addition to outlining the design of TCA , we analyze diverse attack vectors and their progression across multi-turn conversation, providing valuable insights into adversarial tactics and their impact on LLM vulnerabilities. Our findings underscore the pressing need for robust, context-aware defenses in conversational AI systems and highlight TCA framework as a promising direction for securing LLMs while preserving their utility in legitimate applications. We make our implementation available to support further research in this emerging area of AI security.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [193] [Statistical learning theory and Occam's razor: Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04049)
*Tom F. Sterkenburg*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文从统计学习理论出发，为正则化（即用拟合换取简洁性）提供了方法论上的正当性论证，表明对简洁性的偏好是实现理论可靠性和"所见即所得"保证的必然手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奥卡姆剃刀原则（偏好简洁性）在科学哲学和机器学习中一直缺乏合理的辩护。此前关于简洁性偏好的理由要么陷入纯粹实用主义的循环（为简洁而简洁），要么依赖本体论假设（真理总是简单的），两者都不令人满意。

Method: 作者在前期"核心论证"（core argument）的基础上展开，运用统计学习理论框架，论证正则化这一具体程序（在拟合与简洁性之间进行权衡）是实现理论可靠性和"所见即所得"（WYSIWYG）保证所必需的手段-目的（means-ends）论证。

Result: 文章表明，从统计学习理论中可以严格推演出：为了获得理论上的可靠性保证和"所见即所得"性质，必须实施某种对简洁性优于拟合的偏好。这构成了一种真正的、非实用的方法论辩护。

Conclusion: 该论证为奥卡姆剃刀原则提供了一条既非纯粹实用主义（不是因为简洁本身值得偏好）、也非本体论假设（不是因为真理就是简单的）的正当性路径，确立了简洁性偏好在归纳推理中的方法论必要性。

Abstract: The principle of Occam's razor, which instructs us to prefer simplicity in inductive inference, has attracted much scrutiny both in the philosophy of science and in machine learning. In either field, however, a justification for the principle has been elusive. In this paper, building on an earlier "core argument," I spell out a justification from statistical learning theory for the procedure of regularization: for trading off fit for simplicity. The means-ends argument is that in order to profit from theoretical reliability and "what-you-see-is-what-you-get" guarantees, one must implement a certain preference for simplicity over fit. This is a genuine methodological justification, which neither collapses to a purely pragmatic principle that we prefer simplicity for its own sake, nor to an ontological assumption that the truth is simple.

</details>


### [194] [Automatic Statistical Test for Rationally Expressible Algorithms by Selective Inference, with Applications to Feature Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04667)
*Teruyuki Katsuoka,Tomohiro Shiraishi,Shuichi Nishino,Ichiro Takeuchi*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: AutoSI是一个自动选择性推断（Selective Inference）框架，通过从算法的基本操作自动构建选择事件，将精确SI从仅限线性或二次不等式的狭窄类别扩展到可由数据有理函数表达的任何算法，并证明其在有限样本下p值精确有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确选择性推断长期以来只能应用于狭窄的算法类别，因为为每个新算法开发SI程序需要专家手工推导并实现选择事件（即假设被选中的条件）。这种重复的专业工作阻碍了SI的广泛应用，需要一种自动化框架来消除这一障碍。

Method: AutoSI分两步实现自动化：（1）从算法的各个操作自动构造选择事件，用户只需以普通NumPy风格代码编写算法，无需手工推导；（2）将SI可处理的选择事件类别从线性或二次不等式扩展到任何可通过数据有理函数（多项式之比）表达的算法。作者证明了AutoSI计算的p值在有限样本下具有精确有效性。

Result: AutoSI在三种特征选择方法上进行了演示，每种方法仅需几十行代码。其中一个方法（通过交叉验证R²选择正则化参数的lasso）在现有精确SI框架中无法处理，而AutoSI使其成为可能。合成和真实数据实验表明，所得p值在标称水平上控制I型错误率（假阳性率）同时保持较高功效。

Conclusion: AutoSI通过自动化选择事件的构造和扩展可处理的选择事件类别，显著拓宽了精确SI的适用范围，使研究人员无需专家手工推导即可为更广泛的算法计算统计上有效的条件p值。

Abstract: Selective inference (SI) provides statistically valid $p$-values for hypotheses selected by applying an algorithm to the data, correcting for the bias that arises when the same data are used both to select and to test a hypothesis. Developing an SI procedure for a new algorithm, however, has required an expert to derive, and then implement, the selection event, i.e., the conditions under which the hypothesis is selected. Repeating this specialized effort for every new algorithm is why exact SI has so far been available for only a narrow class. We propose AutoSI, a framework that removes this barrier in two ways. First, AutoSI constructs the selection event automatically from the algorithm's individual operations, so the user only writes the algorithm as ordinary NumPy-like code and derives nothing by hand. Second, AutoSI broadens the class of selection events SI can handle: existing exact methods are limited to selection events characterized by linear or quadratic inequalities in the data, whereas AutoSI covers any algorithm expressible through rational functions of the data (ratios of polynomials). We prove that the $p$-values computed by AutoSI are exactly valid in finite samples. We demonstrate AutoSI on three feature-selection methods, each written in a few dozen lines of code. One of these methods, the lasso with its tuning parameter selected by cross-validated $R^2$, cannot be handled within existing exact SI frameworks and is made possible by AutoSI. Experiments on synthetic and real datasets show that the resulting $p$-values control the type I error rate (i.e., the false positive rate) at the nominal level while retaining high power.

</details>


### [195] [Intrinsic-Hybrid Latent Diffusion Models for Generative Modeling on Unknown Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04827)
*Yizhu Wang,Mu Niu,Xiaochen Yang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出内在混合潜在扩散模型（ILDM），将概率降维与未知流形上的几何感知扩散相结合，通过将潜在空间解释为黎曼流形的坐标图，并采用基于局部不确定性的混合扩散过程（黎曼与欧几里得动力学切换），显著提升生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型（DM）依赖大规模训练数据且忽略内在几何结构；潜在扩散模型（LDM）虽降低维度但强加欧几里得结构，无法捕捉底层流形几何，尤其在数据稀疏场景下问题突出。

Method: ILDM将潜在空间视为未知黎曼流形的一个坐标图（chart），通过概率解码器量化几何与不确定性；前向过程为混合扩散，根据局部不确定性在黎曼与欧几里得动力学之间切换，黎曼分量由解码器导出的概率度量张量控制；提出适配混合扩散的近似去噪分数匹配方法，通过混合朗之万动力学实现反向生成。

Result: 在COIL-100、MNIST和心脏MRI数据集上的实验表明，ILDM在FID和LPIPS指标上均优于标准扩散模型和潜在扩散模型，显著提升生成质量。

Conclusion: ILDM通过将流形几何融入潜在扩散框架，在数据稀疏场景下实现了更好的生成性能，验证了几何感知的混合扩散方法的有效性。

Abstract: We introduce the Intrinsic Hybrid Latent Diffusion Model (ILDM), a generative framework that integrates probabilistic dimensionality reduction with geometry-aware diffusion on unknown manifolds. While diffusion models (DMs) have achieved state-of-the-art results in high-dimensional data synthesis, they rely on large training datasets and ignore intrinsic geometric structure. Latent diffusion models (LDMs) address the high dimensionality by learning a latent space, but they typically impose a Euclidean structure, failing to capture the underlying manifold geometry, especially problematic in data-sparse regimes. ILDM addresses these limitations by interpreting the latent space as a chart of an unknown Riemannian manifold, with geometry and uncertainty quantified through a probabilistic decoder. The forward process is a hybrid diffusion that switches between Riemannian and Euclidean dynamics based on local uncertainty, where the Riemannian component is governed by a probabilistic metric tensor derived from the decoder. To learn the generative dynamics, we introduce an approximate denoising score matching method tailored to the hybrid diffusion setting, enabling a backward process defined by hybrid Langevin dynamics. Experiments on COIL-100, MNIST, and cardiac MRI datasets demonstrate that ILDM significantly improves generation quality, achieving lower FID and LPIPS scores compared to standard diffusion and latent diffusion models.

</details>


### [196] [Stable Density Ridges: Consistency and Convergence of Subspace Constrained Mean Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05112)
*Wanli Qiao*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文揭示了子空间约束均值偏移（SCMS）算法实际收敛的目标并非传统静态密度脊，而是通过动力系统视角定义的"稳定脊"，并提出了一种广义SCMS框架，实现均匀R-线性收敛、拓扑满射性及更优的计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文献中长期认为SCMS轨迹收敛到经典静态密度脊，但作者发现这一假设在一般情况下不成立，因为静态定义未考虑沿算法向量场流动时尾随特征空间的旋转。这一理论缺陷导致对SCMS算法收敛目标的理解存在根本性错误。

Method: 作者从动力系统视角出发，通过投影密度梯度的Jacobian定义"稳定脊"这一新型几何结构，并证明其才是SCMS算法的真实理论目标。在此基础上，提出采用恒定步长的广义SCMS框架，建立其均匀R-线性收敛性及到稳定脊的拓扑满射性，并推导了基于Hausdorff距离的估计收敛速率。同时，分析了原SCMS算法因Mean Shift算子将步长与平滑带宽隐式耦合而导致的多项式级计算复杂度问题。

Result: 证明了静态脊并非SCMS算法的收敛目标，稳定脊才是其真实理论目标；广义SCMS框架具有均匀R-线性收敛和拓扑满射性；给出了稳定脊估计在Hausdorff距离下的收敛速率；揭示了原SCMS算法的计算瓶颈并提供更高效、统计一致的替代方案。

Conclusion: 本文通过概念性范式转变（从静态脊到稳定脊），从理论上修正了SCMS算法的收敛目标，并提出了兼顾统计一致性与计算效率的广义框架，为高维数据的密度脊提取提供了更坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: The Subspace Constrained Mean Shift (SCMS) algorithm is a popular nonparametric method for extracting density ridges, which serve as a low-dimensional representation of high-dimensional data. It is a widely held belief in the literature that SCMS trajectories converge to the classical density ridge, which we call the "static ridge", defined via the density gradient and the eigenvalues and eigenvectors of the density's Hessian. In this paper, we demonstrate that this assumption does not hold in general, as the static definition fails to account for the rotation of the trailing eigenspace along the continuous flow of the algorithm's underlying vector field. To resolve this, we propose a paradigm shift by introducing the "stable ridge", a novel geometric structure defined through the lens of dynamical systems and the Jacobian of the projected density gradient. We prove that this stable ridge is the true theoretical target of the SCMS algorithm. Building upon this foundation, we develop a generalized SCMS framework utilizing a constant step size, establishing its uniform R-linear convergence and topological surjectivity onto the stable ridge. We further derive the rates of convergence for estimating the stable ridge in terms of the Hausdorff distance. Finally, we expose that the original SCMS algorithm suffers from polynomial-time computational complexity, which is caused by implicitly coupling the step size to the smoothing bandwidth via the Mean Shift operator, and demonstrate how our generalized framework provides a statistically consistent and more efficient solution.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [197] [Multimodal Alignment Through Joint Kernel Entropic Gromov--Wasserstein Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04234)
*Yixuan Florence Wu,Yilun Zhu,Naichen Shi*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出联合核熵Gromov-Wasserstein最优传输（JK-EGW）框架，用于在跨模态配对数据稀缺时，将多个预训练编码器的嵌入对齐到共享表示空间，通过二次最优传输目标、全局亲和核以及低秩核近似与变分离的提升算法，实现结构保持的对齐，并在多模态检索任务上优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预训练单模态编码器效果强大，但将多模态数据对齐到共享表示空间时，通常需要大量跨模态配对数据。在配对数据稀缺（data-scarce）的现实场景下，现有对齐方法性能受限，且难以对嵌入的几何与分布进行显式控制。因此需要一种能够在有限配对数据下仍能保持内部结构并高效对齐多模态表示的框架。

Method: 提出联合核熵Gromov-Wasserstein最优传输（JK-EGW）框架：通过最小化二次最优传输目标将多模态映射到公共潜在空间；利用模态内与模态间的细粒度相似关系构建全局亲和核，而非依赖原始特征空间距离；理论上给出n^{-1/2}的参数化样本复杂度界；算法上通过低秩核近似与变分离提升，将二次目标拆解为可使用现有熵最优传输（EOT）求解器的交替过程。

Result: 理论方面建立了与标准、熵正则化和Gromov-Wasserstein最优传输相匹配的n^{-1/2}样本复杂度率；算法上推导出可扩展的交替求解过程，通过变分离提升有效缓解二次目标的计算负担；实验上在数据稀缺场景下的预训练编码器嵌入事后对齐中，多模态检索性能显著优于现有对齐基线。

Conclusion: JK-EGW提供了一种在跨模态配对数据稀缺条件下，利用预训练单模态编码器进行结构保持多模态对齐的有效方案，兼具理论保证（最优样本复杂度）、算法可扩展性（利用现有EOT求解器）和实证优越性（多模态检索性能提升），为后续多模态对齐研究提供了新思路。

Abstract: We study the problem of aligning data from multiple modalities into a shared representation space, focusing on settings where strong pretrained unimodal encoders are available but cross-modal paired data are scarce. We propose a structure-preserving alignment framework, joint kernel entropic Gromov--Wasserstein Optimal Transport (JK-EGW), which maps multiple modalities into a common latent space by minimizing a quadratic optimal transport objective. JK-EGW leverages fine-grained similarity relationships within and across modalities to construct a global affinity kernel instead of relying on raw feature-space distances. Our framework naturally provides explicit control over the geometry and distribution of the latent embedding. On the theory side, we establish parametric sample complexity rate of $n^{-1/2}$, matching the corresponding rates for standard, entropic and Gromov--Wasserstein optimal transport. On the algorithmic side, we derive a scalable alternating procedure to solve JK-EGW with entropic optimal transport (EOT) updates through a low-rank kernel approximation and a variational lifting. This lifting scheme effectively relieves the burden of a quadratic objective, and allowing us to take the advantage of existing EOT solvers. Empirically, we focus on post-hoc alignment of embeddings from pretrained encoders in data-scarce regimes, and show that our proposed method achieves improved multimodal retrieval performance compared to existing alignment baselines.

</details>


### [198] [Fixed-Point Characterisations of Extremal Distributions under Partial Distributional Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04315)
*Kizito Salako,Rabiu Tsoho Muhammad*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出一个方法论框架，用于解决参数空间可测子集上部分指定分布下的稳健推断问题。目标是确定统计量（目标函数为解析函数、连续函数、分段连续函数及分段连续函数一致极限的期望之比）在可容许分布集上的极值。极值由满足支撑位置不动点条件的极限极值分布序列逼近，并建立了相应分布与极值目标值的收敛性和渐近性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在部分指定的分布族上进行稳健推断是统计推断中的核心挑战，现有方法（如稳健贝叶斯推断）缺乏统一的框架来处理复杂目标函数（如期望之比）的极值优化问题。本文旨在通过结合极值分布约简、不动点刻画与近似分析，为这类问题提供统一且实用的求解框架。

Method: 提出方法论框架，将部分指定的分布族定义为可容许分布集合；将目标函数广义化为期望之比（覆盖解析、连续、分段连续及其一致极限等函数类别）；证明极值由可容许分布序列逼近，其极限极值分布的特征由支撑位置上的不动点条件刻画；基于该刻画建立计算框架，并证明极值分布和极值目标函数值的收敛性与渐近性质。

Result: 证明了极值可通过序列逼近获得，极限极值分布的支撑位置满足不动点条件，这刻画了极值分布概率质量放置的位置；建立了相应的收敛性和渐近性质；提供了实用的计算方法解决对应优化问题。

Conclusion: 该工作将极值分布约简、不动点刻画和近似分析统一到一个框架中，扩展了稳健推断方法（如稳健贝叶斯推断）的适用范围，使更广泛的目标函数类别（期望之比）能够在此框架下被有效求解。

Abstract: We present a methodological framework for solving robust inference problems with partially specified distributions over measurable subsets of a parameter space. Partial specifications define a set of admissible distributions. The goal is to determine extremal values (over these admissible distributions) for statistical quantities, where these quantities---these objective functions---are ratios of expectations of analytic functions, continuous functions, piecewise continuous functions, and uniform limits of piecewise continuous functions. We show that extremal values are approached by sequences of admissible distributions, whose limiting extremal distributions are characterised by fixed-point conditions on their support locations. This characterises where extremal distributions place probability mass and yields a practical computational framework for solving the corresponding optimisation problems. We establish convergence and asymptotic properties of the resulting extremal distributions and extremal objective function values. This work extends robust inference methods (e.g. robust Bayesian inference) by combining extremal-distribution reduction, fixed-point characterisation, and approximation-based analysis within a unified framework.

</details>


### [199] [Optimal minimization of an unknown function in a nonparametric multivariate regression model thanks to a dimension reduction approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04566)
*Cédric Adam,Ilaria Giulini,Céline Lévy-Leduc*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种两阶段方法：先进行变量选择识别活跃变量，再利用投影梯度下降估计光滑函数的最小值及其位置，并在噪声条件下达到了与已知活跃变量时几乎相同的最优收敛速率（仅差一个对数项的幂次因子）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高维回归问题中，估计函数的最小值及其位置是一个具有挑战性的问题。当函数只依赖于少数活跃变量而非全部d个变量时，传统的全变量方法效率低下且难以达到最优收敛速率。已有方法通常假设活跃变量已知，而实际中往往需要对活跃变量进行识别，因此需要提出一种能同时处理变量选择和最小值估计的方法。

Method: 该方法分为两个步骤：(1) 变量选择：识别出函数f实际依赖的r个活跃变量；(2) 最小值估计：利用投影梯度下降法估计最小值点，其中梯度通过局部多项式逼近在已识别的活跃变量上估计；最小值的估计则通过在所得最小值点估计器处对回归函数进行局部多项式逼近来获得。

Result: 建立了最小值点估计器和最小值估计器的二次风险的渐近上界，证明了该方法的收敛速率达到了即使活跃变量预先已知时所能期望的最优速率，仅相差一个小于对数项幂次的因子。

Conclusion: 该方法成功地将变量选择与最小值估计相结合，在活跃变量未知的情况下实现了与已知活跃变量情形几乎相同的最优估计效率，为非参数回归中高维优化问题提供了理论保障。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a novel approach for estimating the minimum of a smooth function and its location from observations corresponding to a multivariate regression function depending a priori on d variables but actually only on r < d active variables and corrupted by some additional noise. Our method consists of two steps: The rst one is a variable selection approach which is used for identifying the r active variables on which f depends and the second one consists in estimating the minimum of the function and its location. The estimation of the minimizers is obtained by using a projected gradient descent where the gradient is estimated using a local polynomial approximation of the regression function limited to its active variables obtained in the rst step. The estimation of the minimum is obtained by evaluating the estimator of the regression function using a local polynomial approach at the estimator of one of the minimizers previously obtained. We establish non asymptotic upper bounds for the quadratic risk of the estimators of the minimizers and of the minimum and prove that they reach the optimal rate that could be expected as if the active variables were known beforehand up to a factor smaller than a power of a logarithmic term.

</details>


### [200] [Consistent community recovery in stochastic block Ornstein-Uhlenbeck processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04700)
*Anders Norlyk,Almut E. D. Veraart*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出随机块奥尔恩斯坦-乌伦贝克（SBOU）过程，一种连续时间多变量模型，其漂移矩阵编码组件间的潜在组结构。主要贡献是提出一种社区检测算法，在填充、长跨度和高维渐近组合下误分类比例收敛到零，这是离散观测连续时间多变量模型中潜在组恢复的首个一致性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离散时间随机块VAR模型仅支持组间正动态交互，缺乏连续时间框架下潜在组恢复的一致性理论研究；同时高维Lévy驱动的OU过程文献中尚无关于潜分组恢复的一致性问题研究。

Method: 提出SBOU过程（连续时间多变量模型，漂移矩阵编码潜在分组结构）；建立离散观测下漂移矩阵MLE的一致性（扩展高维Lévy驱动的OU文献）；开发可行模型选择程序估计漂移矩阵支持集，实现数据驱动的潜在组数选择；采用填充、长跨度和高维组合渐近框架。

Result: 社区检测算法在组合渐近机制下误分类比例收敛到零（首个此类一致性结果）；漂移矩阵的离散观测MLE估计在相同机制下具有一致性；RE-Europe风电容量数据集上恢复了与地理基准一致的国家级分组。

Conclusion: SBOU框架作为离散时间随机块VAR模型的连续时间推广，支持组间正负动态交互而非仅正向，在理论一致性、模型选择可行性及实际应用（风电数据分组）方面均展现了良好性能。

Abstract: We propose the stochastic block Ornstein-Uhlenbeck (SBOU) process, a continuous-time multivariate model in which the drift matrix encodes a latent group structure among its components. Our main contribution is a community-detection algorithm whose misclassification proportion converges to zero in a regime combining infill, long-span, and high-dimensional asymptotics. To our knowledge, this is the first consistency result of this kind for latent group recovery in a discretely observed continuous-time multivariate model. As a key intermediate result, we establish consistency of the discretely observed maximum likelihood estimator of the drift matrix in the same regime, thereby extending the high-dimensional Lévy-driven Ornstein-Uhlenbeck literature. For practical implementation, we develop a feasible model-selection procedure for estimating the support of the drift matrix, which enables data-driven selection of the number of latent groups. The SBOU framework can be viewed as a continuous-time generalisation of the discrete-time stochastic-block VAR model, allowing for both positive and negative dynamic interactions between groups as opposed to only positive. We illustrate the methodology on the RE-Europe wind-capacity dataset and recover a country-level grouping consistent with the geographic benchmark.

</details>


### [201] [Debiasing the Lasso under Weaker Tail Assumptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04800)
*Leonardo Voltarelli,Roberto Imbuzeiro Oliveira*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究高维推断中lasso估计量的问题，提出通过对数据集进行"标准化"处理，可以在比传统方法更弱的假设下（如不需要次高斯设计和误差独立假设）获得相同的推断结果，仅需为不假设轻尾分布付出较小的代价。核心技术创新在于利用自归一化过程的集中性质，并将结果推广到两种与"去偏lasso"密切相关的方法，其中第二种方法即使在线性模型设定错误的情况下也能进行有效推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的高维推断方法（如"双重选择"技术和多种"去偏lasso"变体）虽然取得了显著成功，但其理论保证大多依赖较强的假设条件，包括次高斯设计、误差与数据的独立性等。这些假设在实际数据中往往难以满足，限制了方法的适用性。

Method: 本文的核心技术手段是对数据集进行"标准化"处理（practical penalized regression中的自然步骤），这一步骤使得可以利用自归一化过程的集中性质来建立理论保证。作者证明了在两种与"去偏lasso"密切相关的方法下，标准化处理可以在远弱于次高斯和独立性假设的条件下获得相同的推断结果。第二种方法还在温和的稀疏性条件下（类似"双重选择"文献中的假设）对模型误设具有鲁棒性。

Result: 证明了对数据集进行标准化后，lasso的高维推断在更弱的假设下仍然成立，代价仅为不假设轻尾分布的小代价。两种方法均被验证有效，其中第二种方法在模型误设情况下也能进行有效推断。

Conclusion: 标准化数据集是一个简单而有效的策略，能够在显著放宽模型假设的前提下保持高维推断的有效性，为实际应用中处理非次高斯、非独立的数据提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We consider the problem of high-dimensional inference with the lasso estimator. Different methods including 'double selection' techniques and multiple versions of the 'debiased lasso' have been proposed for this task with noticeable success. However, most guarantees assume strong hypotheses on the underlying data process and the errors in the linear regression model, such as subgaussian designs and independence between errors and the data itself. We show that 'standardizing' one's dataset -- a natural procedure in practical penalized regression -- leads to same results under much weaker hypotheses, paying only a small price for not assuming light tails. The key technical point allowed by this step is exploiting the concentration properties of self-normalized processes. Importantly, we prove our results for two different methods closely related to the 'debiased lasso'. The second method performs valid inference even for a misspecified linear model, under mild sparsity conditions similar to the 'double selection' literature.

</details>


### [202] [Exchangeable Testing Against an Unknown Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04838)
*Alexander Gnedin*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于“潜基准”排序的贝叶斯框架，用于生成无限二元可交换序列。通过将数据点与未观测基准的排序比较，在纯组合意义上更新运行排序 \( R_n \) 的后验分布，得到可显式计算的“战胜基准”预测概率。归一化运行排序收敛到具有多项式密度的潜强度变量 \( X \)（可能带 Beta 倾斜）。在某些最小-最大锦标赛设置下，预测概率可简化为乘法公式，对应推广 Topp–Leone 分布的先验；文章进一步分析了相关固定 \( n \) 上下马尔可夫链的渐近性，极限扩散具有经典 Wright–Fisher 方差但带有由基准先验密度显式表达的非线性漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mixtures of Beta 密度是交换序列理论中的经典对象，但以往缺乏基于排序的显式更新机制。本文旨在提供一种组合式的排序更新机制，使得在未知基准下的序贯检验具有可显式计算的预测法则。

Method: 作者设定基准秩 \( R_0 \) 的先验分布，通过贝叶斯机制在纯组合层面推导运行秩 \( R_n \) 的后验分布；对 min-max 锦标赛场景推导出简洁的乘法预测公式，并研究固定 \( n \) 上下马尔可夫链的渐近行为（极限扩散为 Wright–Fisher 方差加非线性漂移）。

Result: 得到了可显式计算的战胜基准的预测概率；归一化运行秩收敛到具有多项式密度的潜强度变量（可 Beta 倾斜）；在 Topp–Leone 推广类先验下，预测概率呈现简单乘法形式；极限扩散漂移项可由基准先验密度显式表达。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献在于组合性的基于秩的更新机制，以及针对未知基准的序贯检验所得到的显式预测法则，将交换序列理论与排序锦标赛模型建立起新的联系。

Abstract: We generate infinite binary exchangeable sequences by sequential comparison of data points against a latent benchmark. Assuming a prior distribution of the benchmark rank \(R_0\) within an unobserved group, we set up the Bayesian machinery that determines the posterior distribution of the running rank \(R_n\) in purely combinatorial terms. This yields an explicitly computable predictive probability of winning against the benchmark. The normalised running rank converges to a latent strength variable \(X\) with polynomial density, possibly Beta-tilted.
  Some min-max tournaments lead to particularly simple multiplicative formulae for predictive probabilities related to priors that generalise the Topp--Leone distribution; for that class we analyse the asymptotics of the associated fixed-\(n\) up-down Markov chains. The limiting diffusion has the classical Wright--Fisher variance but a nonlinear drift expressed explicitly via the prior density of the benchmark.
  Mixtures of Beta densities are classical objects in the theory of exchangeable sequences. The contribution of the present work is the combinatorial rank-based updating mechanism and the resulting explicit predictive laws for sequential testing against an unknown benchmark.

</details>


### [203] [A Design-Based Minimax Theory for Network Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04909)
*Vardis Kandiros,Christopher Harshaw,Fredrik Sävje*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出了网络实验中最小最大风险的设计基础理论，该风险由冲突图的局部与全局连通性决定，并推广到直接处理效应和全局平均处理效应的最小最大分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络实验广泛用于社会科学和医学领域以研究存在干扰情况下的因果效应，但尽管已有大量改进的统计程序，这些设置下统计估计的基本极限仍未被充分理解。

Method: 开发并研究了任意邻域干扰模型下网络实验的最小最大风险的设计基础理论，将最小最大风险表达为冲突图的函数，并通过冲突图的局部和全局连通性性质推导出一系列最小最大率的上界和下界。

Result: 展示了最小最大风险与冲突图之间的对应关系，并基于冲突图的连通性特征给出了最小最大率的上下界，且成功应用于直接处理效应和全局平均处理效应这两种常用效应的最小最大分析。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了网络实验中因果估计精度的理论极限可由冲突图结构刻画，为设计最优统计程序提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Network experiments are used throughout the social and medical sciences to investigate causal effects under the presence of interference. While a large body of work has developed improved statistical procedures, the fundamental limits of statistical estimation in these settings is less well understood. In this paper, we develop and investigate a design-based theory of minimax risk for network experiments under an arbitrary neighborhood interference model. Our notion of minimax risk describes the optimal precision among all statistical procedures for investigating a particular causal effect on the observed interference network. We show that the minimax risk is a function of the corresponding conflict graph, which captures inherent unobservability of estimand-relevant potential outcomes given the observed interference network. Our main contribution is a series of upper and lower bounds on the minimax rate in terms of local and global connectivity properties of the conflict graph. To illustrate their utility, we apply these general results to obtain minimax analyses for two commonly studied effects: the direct treatment effect and global average treatment effect.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [204] [C$^2$MOE: Consistency and Complementarity-guided Mixture of Experts for Incomplete Multimodal Emotion Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04013)
*Yuntao Shou,Tao Meng,Wei Ai,Keqin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出C2MOE框架，通过信息论框架将多模态知识分解为一致性和互补性组件，利用混合专家机制实现不完整多模态情感识别中的鲁棒缺失模态补全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的多模态情感识别（MERC）常因传输错误或用户行为导致模态缺失，严重降低模型性能。现有方法虽通过跨模态一致性学习增强鲁棒性，但忽略了模态互补性，导致重建结果存在偏差。

Method: 提出了一个基于一致性和互补性引导的混合专家（Mixture of Experts）框架C2MOE。将多模态知识分解为一致性和互补性组件：通过最大化跨模态可预测性捕获一致性，通过最大化模态间条件熵保留互补性。框架包含双分支预测机制：一致性分支通过最小化不确定性对齐联合分布，互补性分支通过熵最大化利用模态独有线索。最后采用可学习的重加权模块动态分配各专家输出的重要性分数，实现鲁棒自适应的融合补全。

Result: 在多个MERC基准数据集上的大量实验表明，C2MOE在各种缺失模态设置下均持续超越现有最先进方法，验证了其鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: C2MOE通过统一的信息论框架同时考虑模态一致性和互补性，有效解决了现有方法在缺失模态重建中的偏差问题，为不完整多模态情感学习提供了更稳健的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in Multimodal Emotion Recognition in Conversations (MERC) highlight its reliance on complete multimodal inputs. However, real-world data often suffer from missing modalities due to transmission errors or user behavior, severely degrading model performance. Existing methods enhance robustness via cross-modal consistency learning but largely ignore modality complementarity, leading to biased reconstructions. To address this limitation, we propose C$2$MOE, a novel Consistency and Complementarity-guided Mixture of Experts framework for incomplete multimodal emotion learning. Our approach unifies representation learning and missing modality imputation within a principled information-theoretic framework. Specifically, multimodal knowledge is factorized into consistency and complementarity components via interaction-aware experts. Consistency is captured by maximizing cross-modal predictability, while complementarity is preserved by maximizing conditional entropy between modalities. Building upon this decomposition, C$2$MOE introduces a dual-branch prediction mechanism for robust imputation under missing modalities. The consistency branch aligns imputed features with the joint distribution by minimizing uncertainty, and the complementarity branch exploits modality-unique cues via entropy maximization. Finally, C$2$MOE employs a learnable reweighting module that dynamically assigns importance scores to each expert's output, yielding a robust and adaptive fusion for imputation. Extensive experiments on multiple MERC benchmarks demonstrate that C$2$MOE consistently surpasses state-of-the-art methods across various missing-modality settings, validating its robustness and generalization.

</details>


### [205] [On Hamming-Lipschitz Type Stability of the Subdominant (Minmax) Ultrametric: Theory and Simple Proofs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04014)
*Alokendu Mazumder,Arnab Roy,Punit Rathore*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发展了亚主（最小最大）超度量算子的一种ℓ₀型（稀疏）稳定性理论，揭示稀疏编辑对超度量值的传播路径完全由最小生成树（MST）的几何结构决定，给出逐编辑暴露切分数与仅依赖树的全局包络，并证明了Hamming-Lipschitz界及若干尖锐性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的超度量稳定性理论通常在ℓ∞或Gromov–Hausdorff框架下表述，但这些范数型界不适用于只修改少数几个成对距离的稀疏扰动场景。对于稀疏编辑，需要一种ℓ₀类型（即关注受影响条目数量）的稳定性理论来刻画扰动传播的局部性和结构性。

Method: (1) 建立ℓ₀型稳定性框架，分析稀疏编辑如何影响单链接聚类诱导的亚主超度量；(2) 证明编辑传播路径仅经由MST——若某对点的超度量值改变，其树路径必须穿越被编辑的边或被编辑的树外边新暴露的切口；(3) 提出逐编辑暴露切口评分和仅依赖树的全局包络，推导Hamming–Lipschitz界；(4) 证明树几何依赖不可避免的尖锐性结果，包括严格切割分离下树边界的精确达到，以及树外编辑导致单个编辑影响Θ(n²)条超度量条目的显式族；(5) 证明多编辑条件下的条件近可加性原理；(6) 在深度嵌入图上进行实验验证结构评分的漏洞诊断能力。

Result: 得到了稀疏编辑传播仅通过MST的刻画、逐编辑暴露切口分数、Hamming–Lipschitz界、树几何依赖的尖锐性结果（包括Θ(n²)级传播的显式构造），以及多编辑的近可加性原理。实验表明这些结构评分可有效用于层次表示的漏洞诊断。

Conclusion: 亚主超度量在稀疏扰动下的稳定性本质上由树的离散结构而非连续尺度决定，MST几何是预测编辑影响范围的关键。该ℓ₀型稳定性理论为层次表示（如深度嵌入）的鲁棒性分析提供了新的结构性诊断工具。

Abstract: The subdominant (minmax) ultrametric is a canonical tree-structured summary of a dissimilarity matrix, arising equivalently as the ultrametric induced by single-linkage clustering. While its classical stability theory is usually formulated in $\ell_\infty$ or Gromov--Hausdorff terms, such bounds are poorly suited to sparse perturbations that alter only a few pairwise distances. We develop an $\ell_0$-type stability theory for this operator. Our analysis shows that sparse edits propagate only through the minimum spanning tree (MST): a pairwise ultrametric value can change only if its tree path crosses an edited edge or a cut newly exposed by an edited off-tree edge. This yields a sharp per-edit exposed-cut score and a tree-only global envelope, leading to Hamming--Lipschitz bounds on the number of ultrametric entries that can change. We also prove sharpness results showing that this dependence on tree geometry is unavoidable: under strict cut separation the tree-edge bound is attained exactly, and for off-tree edits there are explicit families in which one edited distance changes $Θ(n^2)$ ultrametric entries. In addition, we prove a conditional near-additivity principle for multiple edits under certified large per-edit changed regions and negligible aggregate overlap. Experiments on deep-embedding graphs show that the resulting structural scores provide useful vulnerability diagnostics for hierarchical representations.

</details>


### [206] [A Trust-region Framework for Moment Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04026)
*Oluwasegun A. Somefun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个信任域框架（trust-region framework）来统一理解Adam等自适应矩估计算法在随机梯度优化中的行为。该框架通过p阶矩约束（p∈[2,4]）限制每个权重更新步的幅度，推导出一族基于二阶矩估计和归一化p阶矩估计的学习率机制（Gmake），其中p=4时涉及峰度估计。实验表明，第四阶矩实现（kurtosis-like）在弱信任域约束下最具优势，而随着约束增强，二阶矩实现更具竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自适应矩估计算法（如Adam）虽然在实际应用中表现优异，但其理论基础尚不清晰，各组件（归一化、学习率调度、动量、谱归一化）之间的内在联系缺乏统一解释。作者希望通过一个统一的信任域框架来理解这些机制的本质联系。

Method: 提出信任域优化框架：将每个权重更新步的幅度约束在由p阶矩约束（p∈[2,4]）决定的信任域内。由此推导出Gmake机制族，包含基于二阶矩估计和归一化p阶矩估计的学习率机制。p=4时涉及峰度（kurtosis）估计。该框架统一解释了归一化、学习率调度、作为动量的谱低通滤波以及算子级谱归一化。

Result: 在GPT2-124M模型上使用FineWeb-Edu和TinyStories数据集进行实验。结果显示：当信任域约束较弱时，第四阶矩（峰度）实现收益最大；当引入逐渐增强的信任域控制后，二阶矩实现变得更具竞争力，通常能达到略低于第四阶矩实现的验证损失。

Conclusion: Gmake家族提供了一个统一的信任域视角来解释多种自适应优化机制。第四阶矩估计在弱约束下更优，而二阶矩在强约束下更优，这揭示了不同阶矩估计在不同信任域强度下的适用性差异，为选择合适的学习率机制提供了理论指引。

Abstract: In this paper, we develop a trust-region framework for understanding the behavior of adaptive moment estimation mechanisms, such as \textsc{Adam}, in stochastic gradient optimization. Specifically, in this framework, the magnitude of the update step for each individual weight is constrained within a trust-region governed by a moment constraint of order $p\in[2,4]$. The resulting derivation then leads to a family of learning-rate mechanisms based on second-moment estimation and a normalized $p$-th moment estimation. When $p=4$, this involves kurtosis-like estimation. The general mechanism, referred to as \textsc{Gmake}, provides a unified interpretation of normalization by moment estimation, learning-rate scheduling, spectral lowpass filtering as momentum, and operator-level spectral normalization within a common trust-region framework. Experiments on GPT2-124M trained on FineWeb-Edu and TinyStories suggest that the fourth-moment realization provides its greatest benefit when trust-region constraints are weak. As progressively stronger trust-region controls are introduced, the second-moment realization becomes increasingly competitive, often achieving slightly lower validation loss than its corresponding fourth-moment realization.

</details>


### [207] [Learning to Resolve Neutron Resonances with Fully Convolutional Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04027)
*Nataly R. Panczyk,Athanasios Stamatopoulos,Josef Svoboda,Majdi I. Radaideh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探索将全卷积神经网络与传统R-Matrix代码结合，用于自动检测中子透射光谱中的共振峰，初步实现93%的分类准确率，但泛化能力不足，需更大规模训练数据和物理约束来提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统R-Matrix代码拟合中子透射数据高度依赖先验评估，且需要大量人工干预；常规峰值识别方法难以处理复杂且含噪的数据。作者旨在加速实验后处理流程并减少先验评估带来的偏差。

Method: 采用全卷积神经网络（FCN）对透射光谱中每个点进行分类，区分为共振区与非共振区。训练和评估使用了七条透射光谱，包括两条评估数据和五条实验数据。

Result: 模型在分类任务上达到约93%的准确率，但进一步分析显示该指标高估了模型的泛化能力。尽管在PHYSOR 2026分析基础上增加了训练数据，模型仍无法可靠泛化到未见过的同位素。

Conclusion: 当前方法在特定数据集上有潜力，但缺乏对未见过同位素的可靠泛化能力。未来工作应探索更大且更多样化的训练数据集，并将中子共振的已知物理特性融入模型以提升性能。

Abstract: This work investigates the feasibility of augmenting traditional R-Matrix codes with a robust machine learning framework for automatically detecting neutron resonances in transmission spectra. Neutron transmission data are often complex and noisy, making them difficult to analyze using traditional peak-identification methods. The state-of-the-art R-Matrix codes currently used by physicists to fit these data often depend on prior evaluations and require substantial manual effort. This preliminary study demonstrates a method for accelerating the post-experimental processing of neutron transmission data and reducing bias associated with dependence on prior evaluations. We employ a fully convolutional neural network to classify individual points as belonging to resonance or non-resonance regions in seven transmission spectra---two evaluated and five experimental. Although the model achieves classification accuracies in the range of 93\%, further analysis shows that this metric overstates its ability to generalize. Building on our prior analysis in PHYSOR 2026, we find that, despite the inclusion of additional training data, the method does not generalize reliably to previously unseen isotopes. To address these limitations, future work should evaluate whether a larger and more diverse training dataset can produce a generalizable model and should incorporate known physical characteristics of neutron resonances to improve model performance.

</details>


### [208] [Lindblad-Inspired Multi-Timescale Reservoir Computing with Separable Rotation and Dissipation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04028)
*Jyotiranjan Beuria,Amit Shukla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种受Lindblad开放系统动力学启发的多时间尺度回声状态网络（Echo-State Network），通过将循环算子分解为精确离散化的阻尼旋转模态，使旋转（相位混合）和衰减（记忆遗忘）成为独立可调的设计变量，并提供直接的全局稳定性保证。在十个基准测试上，该方法在NARMA-20上取得最佳固定水库性能，在Lorenz-63上取得最低平均误差，且不需要事后谱半径重缩放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统回声状态网络将信号混合、记忆保留和稳定性统一放在单一随机循环矩阵中，难以对可逆混合与不可逆遗忘进行独立的模态控制，也缺乏直接的全局稳定性保证。现有结构化设计虽改善了拓扑、范数保持、泄漏或深度，但未能同时解决这三个问题。

Method: 提出一种经典Lindblad启发的多时间尺度水库（multi-timescale reservoir）架构。循环算子由精确离散化的阻尼旋转模态组装而成，旋转和衰减成为两个独立的设计变量，分别控制相位混合与记忆丢失。正交模态混合保持算子的正规性（normality），衰减谱直接决定回声状态稳定性边界，无需事后的谱半径重新缩放。

Result: 在十个对齐随机种子下，与标准、泄漏、深度、正交、循环、下一代水库以及紧凑训练的GRU对比，覆盖线性记忆、非线性递推、混沌预测、延迟逻辑和真实传感器校准等任务。该水库在NARMA-20上取得最佳固定水库性能，在Lorenz-63上取得最低平均误差，在线性记忆任务上与最强结果持平，整体具有广泛竞争力。消融实验表明旋转增加状态多样性，耗散提供受控遗忘并改善预测条件性。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种可解释的循环架构，其中混合、记忆和稳定性是显式且可独立调节的设计变量，弥合了开放系统动力学原理与结构化状态空间建模之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Echo-state networks enable efficient temporal learning by fixing the recurrent dynamics and training only a linear readout. However, conventional reservoirs typically accommodate signal mixing, memory retention, and stability within a single random recurrent matrix. Existing structured designs improve topology, norm preservation, leakage, or depth, but generally do not provide separate modal control of reversible mixing and irreversible forgetting together with a direct global stability guarantee. We introduce a classical Lindblad-inspired multi-timescale reservoir that bridges open-system dynamical principles with structured state-space modeling. The recurrent operator is assembled from exactly discretized damped rotational modes, so rotation and decay become independent design variables governing phase mixing and memory loss. Orthogonal mode mixing preserves normality, while the decay spectrum directly determines the echo-state stability margin without post-hoc spectral-radius rescaling. We evaluate the method over ten aligned seeds against standard, leaky, deep, orthogonal, cycle, and next-generation reservoirs, together with a compact trained gated recurrent unit, across linear memory, nonlinear recurrence, chaotic forecasting, delayed logic, and real sensor calibration. Across the benchmark suite, the proposed reservoir achieves the best fixed-reservoir performance on bounded NARMA-20 and the lowest mean error on Lorenz-63, matches the strongest linear-memory result, and remains broadly competitive across broad range of benchmarks. Ablation studies show that rotation increases state diversity, whereas dissipation provides controlled forgetting and improves predictive conditioning. The resulting framework offers an interpretable recurrent architecture in which mixing, memory, and stability are explicit and independently tunable design variables.

</details>


### [209] [An Explainable LLM Agent Layer for Open-World Anomaly Detection in Oil Wells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04041)
*Lucas Gouveia Omena Lopes,Thales Miranda de Almeida Vieira,Eduardo Toledo de Lima Junior,William Wagner Matos Lira*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个部署在开放世界学习(OWL)异常检测流水线下游的大语言模型(LLM)代理层，旨在弥合现有OWL管线的可解释性缺口。该代理使用Qwen3.5-397B-A17B MoE模型（经NVIDIA NIM服务），接收上游传感器指标和分类/新颖性断言，输出自然语言推理、置信度排序的批评意见以及新颖性的统一命名。在3W数据集989个真实井段上，该代理在9类分类中达到35.1% top-1/63.9% top-3准确率，7类探测中71.7% top-2验证准确率（精确率0.91），89.7%新颖性检测率（5/7隐藏类稳定命名）。代理角色是上游方法的"伴侣"而非替代品，职责包括确认决策、论证决策、标记分歧和命名新颖性，从而弥合部署障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OWL管线（基于自编码器检测、多类分类、马氏距离新颖性检测）虽能回答"发生了什么"，但无法解释"模型为什么这样认为"或"操作员下一步该做什么"，也不能为发现的新颖簇提供可读命名。这一可解释性缺口阻碍了OWL管线在实际运维场景中的部署。本文旨在通过引入LLM代理层来填补这个缺口。

Method: 在OWL流水线下游部署LLM代理层：使用Qwen3.5-397B-A17B Mixture-of-Experts模型（经NVIDIA NIM推理服务），代理接收结构化传感器指标和上游分类/新颖性断言，输出三类结果：自然语言论证、置信度排序的批评和评论、以及检测到的新颖事件的统一命名。代理被设计为上游方法的"伴侣"（companion）而非替代品，其四项核心职能为：确认上游决策、提供传感器接地论证、标记不可信的上游标签分歧、以及为新发现簇命名。

Result: 在3W数据集的989个真实井段上进行三项研究：(1) 全9类分类top-1为35.1%、top-3为63.9%（95% CI [56.9, 70.4]）；(2) 7类探测的top-2验证准确率71.7%（CI [64.8, 77.6]），精确率0.91（CI [0.84, 0.95]）；(3) 新颖性检测率89.7%（CI [87.0, 91.9]），7个隐藏类中5个实现稳定命名。

Conclusion: LLM代理并非独立分类器，其核心价值在于四重角色：(1) 在传感器证据支持时确认上游决策；(2) 以传感器接地的可审计语言论证决策；(3) 在上游标签不合理时标记分歧；(4) 为新发现异常事件命名，使聚类的未标记事件以一致的人类可读标签送达工程师。该方案弥合了OWL管线部署中阻碍性的可解释性缺口。

Abstract: Open-World Learning (OWL) pipelines for oil well anomaly detection have recently been shown to combine autoencoder-based detection, multiclass classification, and Mahalanobis-based novelty detection on the public 3W dataset. These pipelines answer \textit{what happened}, but they do not explain \textit{why the model believes it} or \textit{what the operator should do next}, and they do not put a human-readable name on the novelty clusters they discover. This paper evaluates a Large Language Model (LLM) agent layer placed downstream of the OWL pipeline, designed as a \textbf{companion} to the published upstream methods rather than a replacement. Using the Qwen3.5-397B-A17B Mixture-of-Experts model served via NVIDIA NIM, the agent receives structured sensor metrics and upstream classification or novelty assertions, and returns natural-language justifications, confidence-ranked critiques, and consolidated names for detected novelties. Across three studies spanning 989 real well-file segments from the 3W dataset, the agent achieved $35.1\%$ top-1 / $63.9\%$ top-3 (95\% CI [56.9, 70.4]) classification on all nine classes, $71.7\%$ top-2 validation [64.8, 77.6] with precision $0.91$ [0.84, 0.95] across 7 probed classes, and $89.7\%$ novelty detection [87.0, 91.9] with stable cluster naming on 5 of 7 hidden classes. The agent is not a standalone classifier. Its role is to: (1) confirm upstream decisions when sensor evidence supports them, (2) justify decisions in sensor-grounded language operators can audit, (3) flag disagreement when upstream labels are implausible, and (4) name novelties so that clustered unlabeled events arrive at the engineer with a consolidated human-readable label. The goal is to close the explainability gap that currently blocks deployment of OWL pipelines in operational settings.

</details>


### [210] [Tactus: Open-Vocabulary Object Recognition from Low-Cost Pressure Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04043)
*Abdul Basit Tonmoy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Tactus是一个仅基于电阻式压力阵列传感器数据回答文本查询的开源模型。在STAG基准上达到0.771±0.062的top-1准确率（top-3为0.935），匹配并有时超过有监督CNN基线（0.76），且无需训练分类头。该模型仅用187个训练记录，结合144k无标注帧的掩码自编码器预训练和传感器自有校准仿射变换实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电阻式压力阵列是最廉价、出货量最大的触觉传感器，但现有触觉表征学习主要集中于光学传感器（对变形凝胶成像）。作者希望弥合这一差距，证明仅靠压力数据即可实现高质量的文本查询触觉理解。

Method: 1) 基于掩码自编码器在144k无标注同传感器帧上进行预训练；2) 仅用187个训练记录进行微调；3) 使用传感器自身的校准仿射变换作为输入标准化，该步骤贡献的精度提升超过所有架构改动之和；4) 不训练分类头，直接以表征匹配文本查询。

Result: STAG基准（27个对象，保留录制）上四次运行top-1为0.771±0.062（top-3 0.935），匹配并最佳情况下超过有监督CNN基线的0.76。误差集中于少量接触歧义类别，与文本目标几何无关（Spearman rho≤0.05，702个类别对）；两帧即可恢复八帧精度的89%。同时报告了失败案例：跨传感器预训练无增益、视觉联合训练损害触觉、错误归一化丢失97%动态范围。

Conclusion: Tactus证明了电阻式压力阵列数据足以支持高质量的开集文本查询触觉理解，且无需光学传感器或训练分类头。关键洞见是数据效率和传感器校准的正确使用远比架构创新重要。模型权重、代码和内存层均已开源发布。

Abstract: Resistive pressure arrays are the cheapest and most widely shipped tactile sensors, yet tactile representation learning has concentrated on optical sensors that image a deforming gel. We present Tactus, an open model that answers text queries from pressure data alone: on the STAG benchmark (27 objects, held-out recordings), it reaches 0.771 +/- 0.062 top-1 over four runs (top-3 0.935), matching, and at best exceeding, the dataset's supervised closed-set CNN at 0.76, with no trained classifier head. The recipe is small-data: 187 training recordings, masked-autoencoder pretraining on 144k unlabeled same-sensor frames, and the sensor's own calibration affine, which recovered more accuracy than every architecture change combined. The released model's errors concentrate in a few contact-ambiguous classes, are uncorrelated with text-target geometry (Spearman rho <= 0.05 over 702 class pairs), and survive paraphrased and even bare-name queries within one point; two diverse frames recover 89% of eight-frame accuracy. Failures are reported with equal precision: cross-sensor pretraining pooling gave no gain, vision co-training degraded touch, and a mis-normalized input pipeline silently discarded 97% of the sensor's dynamic range while producing plausible intermediate results. Weights, code, and the memory layer the model plugs into are released openly.

</details>


### [211] [Robust and Personalized Federated Learning for Aircraft-Engine Prognostics under Benign and Adversarial Client Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04045)
*Chinmoy Mitra,Md. Mehedi Hasan Nipu,Mohammad Sakib Mahmood,Md. Rakibul Islam,M. F. Mridha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对联邦学习在飞机发动机剩余寿命预测中的应用，研究了良性异质性（不同运行条件/故障模式）和对抗性异质性（投毒攻击）两大挑战，通过C-MAPSS基准上的多任务一维卷积神经网络实验，比较了多种个性化与稳健聚合方法，发现共享表示个性化可缩小约70%的误差差距，Krum聚合是唯一能抵御协同攻击的方法，而个性化与稳健聚合结合能在几乎不损失精度的前提下将攻击成功率降至2.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习使机队运营商无需共享原始传感器数据即可联合训练剩余寿命预测模型，但面临双重挑战：良性异质性（诚实运营商面临不同运行条件和故障模式）与对抗性异质性（被攻陷运营商提交投毒更新）。已有研究孤立地处理这两类问题，缺少在安全导向的受控评估下对两者交互作用的系统研究。

Method: 基于多任务一维卷积神经网络和C-MAPSS基准的结构性非IID数据划分，开展受控实验。良性异质性方面比较四种补救方案；对抗性方面设计五种攻击（包括物理动机的传感器值后门攻击）并对四种聚合方法进行评估。通过组合个性化与稳健聚合方法测试两者协同的鲁棒性与精度平衡。

Result: 共享表示个性化缩小约70%的本地到集中式RMSE差距（远优于近端正则化21%和服务器端重加权10%）；物理后门攻击对标准平均聚合达到94.9%成功率且不改变清洁精度；Krum将攻击成功率降低一个数量级且是唯一抵御协同攻击的聚合器；个性化本身无助防御，但将其与稳健聚合结合可将攻击成功率降至2.8%且精度损失很小。结果在客户端数量变化和更难的六条件数据集上保持一致。

Conclusion: 仅凭清洁精度无法认证模型安全性，攻击成功率必须被显式评估；稳健更新选择与协作表示学习之间存在权衡；个性化与稳健聚合的组合能在保持精度的同时恢复鲁棒性，为FL在航空安全关键应用中的部署提供了可复现的评估框架。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables aircraft fleet operators to jointly train remaining-useful-life (RUL) models from engine sensor telemetry without sharing raw data. This study examines two complementary challenges: benign heterogeneity, where honest operators observe different operating conditions and fault modes, and adversarial heterogeneity, where compromised operators submit poisoned updates. We conduct a controlled, safety-oriented evaluation using a multi-task one-dimensional convolutional neural network and a structurally non-IID partition of the Commercial Modular Aero-Propulsion System Simulation (C-MAPSS) benchmark. We compare four remedies for benign heterogeneity and evaluate five attacks against four aggregation methods, including a physically motivated sensor-value backdoor designed to mask engine degradation.
  Shared-representation personalization closes approximately 70% of the local-to-centralized root-mean-square-error gap, compared with 21% for proximal regularization and 10% for server-side reweighting. The backdoor achieves a 94.9% attack success rate against standard averaging while leaving clean accuracy statistically unchanged, demonstrating that accuracy alone cannot certify model safety and that attack success must be evaluated explicitly. Krum reduces attack success by an order of magnitude and is the only evaluated aggregator that withstands coordinated attackers, whereas personalization alone provides no protection. Combining personalization with robust aggregation restores robustness (2.8% attack success) with only a small accuracy cost, revealing a trade-off between robust update selection and collaborative representation learning. Results remain consistent across client counts and on a harder six-condition dataset. Code and data partitions are released for reproducibility.

</details>


### [212] [Recurrent Residual Quantization: A Progressive Multi-Precision Representation for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04048)
*Yu Luo,Bo Dong,Wenhua Cheng,Haihao Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出递归残差量化（RRQ）框架，通过从2-bit基础模型开始逐步添加2-bit残差来构建4/6/8-bit表示，实现从单一checkpoint获得多种有效精度，无需校准且避免联合多比特优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多样化的部署约束下，LLM服务需要在精度、内存占用和吞吐量之间灵活权衡。然而传统量化方法通常需要为每种目标位宽独立训练/保存checkpoint，导致存储和计算开销巨大。

Method: RRQ将权重表示为低比特量化基座加上一系列量化残差修正。从通过PTQ或RTN得到的2-bit模型出发，逐步使用RTN生成轻量级2-bit残差来构建4/6/8-bit表示。该方法免校准，且不需要联合多比特优化。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B上，完整的全RTN 2/4/6/8-bit包在1,293秒内构建完成，比MatGPTQ快3.3倍。在六个近期LLM上的实验表明6-bit和8-bit精度具有竞争力，4-bit表现依赖具体模型。

Conclusion: RRQ提供了一种高效且灵活的PTQ方案，通过递归残差累积实现多样化的精度-内存权衡，在保持竞争力的同时大幅提升量化构建效率。

Abstract: Serving large language models (LLMs) under diverse deployment constraints requires flexible trade-offs between accuracy, memory footprint, and throughput. However, conventional quantization methods typically require a separate checkpoint for each target bit-width. We introduce Recurrent Residual Quantization (RRQ), a post-training quantization (PTQ) framework that represents weights as a low-bit quantized base together with a sequence of quantized residual corrections, enabling multiple effective precisions from a single checkpoint. Starting from a 2-bit model obtained via post-training quantization (PTQ) or round-to-nearest (RTN), RRQ progressively adds lightweight 2-bit residuals generated via RTN to construct 4-, 6-, and 8-bit representations. The method is calibration-free and avoids joint multi-bit optimization. In our Qwen3-8B setup, the full all-RTN 2-/4-/6-/8-bit package is constructed in 1,293 seconds, 3.3 times faster than the measured MatGPTQ construction. Experiments on six recent LLMs show competitive accuracy at 6 and 8 bits, with model-dependent behavior at 4 bits. The code will be made publicly available upon publication.

</details>


### [213] [CAMP: A Cycle-Aware Multi-Scale Patch Mixer for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04051)
*Jung Min Choi,Vijaya Krishna yalavarthi,Lars Schmidt-Thieme*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CAMP（Cycle-Aware Multi-Scale Patch Mixer），一种面向长时间序列预测的周期感知多尺度补丁混合器。它通过自适应周期学习模块为每个输入窗口识别主导频率并生成历史及未来周期分量，通过水平引导补丁混合器实现位置相关的精细化处理，并通过多分辨率表示建模去周期化残差。在7个长时预测基准和4个PEMS交通基准上取得最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实时间序列常受周期性模式支配，但其主导周期在不同数据集、预测设置和输入窗口间可能变化。现有周期感知预测器通常在数据集级别选择单一周期，难以处理周期随时间变化或多周期共存的情况。此外，基于补丁的模型对所有补丁位置统一处理，忽略了距离预测边界不同的补丁需要不同上下文细化程度的问题。去除周期后残差动态横跨多种时间分辨率，难以用单一尺度描述。

Method: CAMP包含三个核心模块：(1) 自适应周期学习模块——针对每个输入窗口单独识别主导频率，无需预定义周期长度，生成历史和未来周期分量；(2) 水平引导补丁混合器——引入位置相关的细化机制，允许较远的补丁融入更广泛的时序上下文，同时保留靠近预测边界的直接信息；(3) 多尺度残差建模——通过时间对齐的多分辨率表示捕获去周期化残差中不同尺度的互补动态。

Result: 在7个长时预测基准中，CAMP在6个数据集上取得最佳平均MSE，在6个数据集上取得最佳或并列最佳MAE；在4个PEMS交通基准的16个设置中取得最高的MSE胜出次数。

Conclusion: CAMP通过窗口级别的自适应周期识别、位置感知的补丁混合以及多尺度残差建模，有效解决了现有方法在周期变化、补丁处理不均衡和残差多尺度表达方面的局限性，在多个长时预测基准上验证了其优越性。

Abstract: Real-world time series are often governed by recurring patterns, but their dominant periods may vary across datasets, forecasting settings, and individual input windows. Existing cycle-aware forecasters commonly rely on a single period selected at the dataset level, which can be restrictive when periodic behavior changes over time or when multiple cycles coexist. Moreover, patch-based models typically process all patch positions uni- formly, although patches farther from the forecast boundary may require broader contextual refinement, while recent patches contain information that should be preserved more directly. Af- ter cyclic behavior is removed, the remaining dynamics may also span multiple temporal resolutions and cannot be adequately de- scribed at a single scale. We introduce CAMP, a Cycle-Aware Multi-Scale Patch Mixer designed to address these challenges. The Adaptive Cycle Learning module identifies dominant fre- quencies separately for each input window and generates both historical and future cyclic components without requiring a pre- defined cycle length. The Horizon-Guided Patch Mixer intro- duces position-dependent refinement, allowing earlier patches to incorporate broader temporal context while preserving infor- mation close to the forecast boundary. CAMP further models the de-cycled residual through temporally aligned multi-resolution representations, enabling complementary dynamics at different scales to be captured within one forecasting framework. Across seven long-term forecasting benchmarks, CAMP achieves the best average MSE on six datasets and the best or tied-best MAE on six. It also obtains the highest MSE win count across sixteen settings on four PEMS traffic benchmarks.

</details>


### [214] [LaPrune: Controllable Differentiable Sparsity at Million Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04057)
*Jakub Antczak,Joanna Wojciechowicz,Łukasz Struski,Jacek Tabor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LaPrune，一种数学上精确预算的可微分层，通过LapSum屏障保持选择质量并用归一化二阶矩约束将掩码从稠密等质量分配推向硬top-k，同时提供总体预测、近二元极限律和最坏情况保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Top-k选择虽能决定稀疏模型的活跃组件，但硬选择会阻断梯度，而连续松弛方法往往将掩码硬度与选择质量耦合，难以在保持精确预算的同时实现可微分优化。

Method: 提出LaPrune可微分层：使用LapSum屏障保持选择质量，通过归一化二阶矩约束控制掩码从均匀质量分布逐步逼近硬top-k。推导了饱和分数的总体预测、近二元极限律，以及近零分数的紧最坏情况保证。

Result: 归一化硬度参数对分数尺度具有不变性，而固定的LapSum温度不具备这种不变性；该方法在精确预算下实现数学上严格的硬top-k逼近。

Conclusion: LaPrune通过解耦掩码硬度与选择质量，在保持精确预算的同时实现可微分、数学上严格的稀疏选择机制，且归一化参数具有尺度不变性优势。

Abstract: Top-$k$ selection determines which components of a sparse model remain active. Hard selection blocks gradients, while continuous relaxations often couple mask hardness to the selected mass. We introduce LaPrune, a mathematically exact-budget differentiable layer that controls the normalized second moment while preserving the selected mass. A LapSum barrier preserves the selection mass, and a normalized second-moment constraint moves the mask from a dense equal-mass allocation toward hard top-$k$ at each budget. We derive a population prediction of the saturated fraction, a near-binary limiting law, and a tight worst-case guarantee on the near-zero fraction. The normalized hardness parameter is invariant to score scale, while a fixed LapSum temperature is not.

</details>


### [215] [SJEPA: Learning Elegant Latent Dynamics with Hybrid Symbolic-Neural Predictors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04060)
*Yongchao Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SJEPA，一种无重建的联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA），其学习到的预测表示所诱导的动力学能够获得紧凑的符号化描述。其混合转移模型结合了符号定律与正则化的神经修正项，核心原则是学习最简单且充分的动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联合嵌入预测架构虽然能学习抽象状态，但其转移模型通常是不透明的神经映射，缺乏可解释性和符号化表征能力，作者希望在不进行重建的前提下获得可解释、符号化的预测动力学。

Method: SJEPA采用混合转移模型：符号定律加上正则化的神经修正项。其核心原则是学习最简单充分的动力学，通过表示约束保留信息丰富且非坍缩的预测坐标，并通过算子压缩偏向低复杂度的符号-神经转移。理论分析上形式化了诱导动力学复杂度，分析了预测坐标的非可辨识性，并证明无约束的算子压缩会导致表示坍缩的捷径。

Result: 在受控摆实验中，联合学习比事后拟合发现了更简单的符号动力学，且长时程 rollout 误差和散度更低。无约束的单步诊断实现了预测的坍缩捷径。在语法设定错误下，修正正则化保留了可表示的符号机制，并将神经组件导向残差动力学。

Conclusion: 实验结果揭示了预测保真度、表示质量、符号简洁性和符号-神经分配之间可控制的权衡关系，验证了SJEPA框架的有效性与理论分析的准确性。

Abstract: Joint-embedding predictive architectures learn abstract states by predicting target embeddings from context embeddings, but their transition models are typically opaque neural maps. We introduce SJEPA, a reconstruction-free JEPA framework that learns predictive representations whose induced dynamics admit compact symbolic descriptions. Its hybrid transition combines a symbolic law with a regularised neural correction for dynamics outside the selected grammar. The central principle is to learn the simplest adequate dynamics: representation constraints preserve informative, non-collapsed predictive coordinates, while operator compression favours low-complexity symbolic-neural transitions that remain predictively adequate. We formalise this principle through induced-dynamics complexity, analyse predictive-coordinate non-identifiability, and show that unconstrained operator compression creates a direct shortcut to representation collapse. The framework supports both alternating representation-equation learning and symbolic dynamics fitted to fixed representations. In controlled pendulum experiments, joint learning discovers substantially simpler symbolic dynamics with lower long-horizon rollout error and divergence than post-hoc fitting, while an unconstrained one-step diagnostic realises the predicted collapse shortcut. Under grammar misspecification, correction regularisation preserves the representable symbolic mechanism and directs the neural component towards residual dynamics. The results expose a controllable trade-off among predictive fidelity, representation quality, symbolic parsimony, and symbolic-neural allocation.

</details>


### [216] [Spend Bits Where Queries Look: KV Cache Vector Quantization with Attention-Preserving Transforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04074)
*Samuel Fernández-Menduiña,Amir Ziashahabi,Eduardo Pavez,Antonio Ortega,Salman Avestimehr*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NOVA-KV方法，将长上下文LLM解码中的KV缓存量化问题形式化为变换编码问题。通过从校准统计量中推导出最优变换（键变换为非正交且满足广义Parseval关系），并结合等体积分组的定宽向量量化器，在两比特每元素的设定下显著恢复了标量量化方法丢失的检索精度，同时保持可比吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文LLM解码每步都要加载KV缓存，其加载时间超过计算注意力的时间，导致吞吐量受带宽限制。减小缓存大小可以同时提升解码速度和服务器容量。然而现有方法存在缺陷：数据无关的变换（随机或Hadamard旋转）只均衡方差而不压缩能量，且使用定宽标量量化器在低比特率下不最优；此外没有从失真准则角度推导变换。

Method: 1) 将KV缓存量化定义为变换编码问题，失真定义为注意力乘积的误差。2) 在高分辨率模型下，从校准统计量中解析推导键和值的最优变换闭式解。3) 证明最优键变换非正交且满足广义Parseval关系，使注意力感知失真等价于变换域的MSE。4) 基于此使用MSE最优的向量量化器直接应用于变换后的键系数。5) 为满足定宽布局要求，将系数分组为等体积分区，使等大小码本在高分辨率模型下达到变速率最优。

Result: 在两比特每元素设定下，NOVA-KV方法恢复了标量量化方法在长上下文检索中丢失的大部分精度，同时保持可比的吞吐量。

Conclusion: 通过将KV缓存量化严格建模为以注意力误差为失真的变换编码问题，NOVA-KV表明：（a）最优键变换从校准统计中导出且非正交；（b）注意力感知失真在最优变换下简化为MSE；（c）等体积分组结合等大小码本可实现定宽约束下的变速率最优。这为KV缓存量化提供了理论上更优的框架。

Abstract: Long-context LLM decoding reads the key-value (KV) cache at every step. Loading it takes longer than computing attention over it, so throughput is bandwidth-bound. Hence, reducing the cache size can raise both decoding speed and serving capacity. The challenge is to reduce cache size while preserving the attention products, keeping reconstruction cheap, and using a fixed per-token bit count. At two bits per element, the most competitive methods rely on orthogonal transforms. However, existing techniques are either data-oblivious or use the query statistics without deriving the transform from a distortion criterion. Moreover, they rely on transforms built on top of random or Hadamard rotations, which equalize variances across entries rather than compacting energy, and fixed-width scalar quantizers, which are suboptimal at low rates. In this paper, we formulate KV cache quantization as a transform coding problem in which distortion is the error in the attention products. We derive closed-form optimal transforms for keys and values from calibration statistics, under a high-resolution model. We show that the optimal key transform is not orthogonal and satisfies a generalized Parseval relation: the attention-aware distortion becomes mean-squared error (MSE) in the transform domain. Thus, we can use MSE-optimal vector quantizers applied directly to the transformed key coefficients. To meet the fixed-width layout requirement, we show that grouping coefficients into equal-volume partitions makes equal-size codebooks attain the variable-rate optimum under the same high-resolution model. At two bits per element, our method, termed NOVA-KV, recovers most of the long-context retrieval accuracy lost by scalar quantization methods at comparable throughput.

</details>


### [217] [Spatiotemporal Graph Transformer for Traffic Intelligence in Edge Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04075)
*Laha Ale,Letian Lin,Na Cao,Zheng Ma,Peng Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种时空图Transformer框架用于边缘计算中的流量预测，通过图神经网络捕获空间相关性、Transformer自注意力学习长时程时间依赖，在真实蜂窝网络数据集上优于GCN-RNN、GCN-LSTM和GCN-GRU等基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘计算中的流量预测具有强烈的空间相关性和长时程时间依赖性，现有基于递归神经网络的预测方法虽能捕捉短期动态，但在非平稳条件下难以建模长时程流量演化，因此需要一种能够同时建模空间交互和时间依赖的预测框架。

Method: 构建时空图Transformer框架：使用图神经网络捕获服务区域间的空间相关性，利用Transformer的自注意力机制从历史流量观测中学习长程时间模式，并将空间表示学习与时间推理进行解耦，以支持大规模时空流量建模。

Result: 在真实蜂窝网络数据集上的大量实验表明，所提出的图Transformer在多个预测时域上一致优于基于递归图网络的基线模型（包括GCN-RNN、GCN-LSTM和GCN-GRU），生成的预测结果使主动资源预留更有效，并能降低过载风险。

Conclusion: 图增强注意力机制在构建智能自适应边缘计算系统方面具有显著潜力，所提出的时空图Transformer为边缘网络中的主动式资源管理提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate traffic forecasting is essential for proactive resource management in edge computing, where service demand evolves dynamically across both space and time. In practical cellular edge systems, traffic exhibits strong spatial correlations among neighboring service regions and long-range temporal dependencies driven by user mobility and application behavior. Existing recurrent forecasting approaches can capture short-term dynamics but often struggle to model long-horizon traffic evolution under non-stationary conditions. To address this challenge, we propose a spatiotemporal graph Transformer framework that jointly models spatial interactions and temporal dependencies for traffic forecasting in edge computing. The framework employs graph neural networks to capture spatial correlations among service regions and leverages Transformer-based self-attention to learn long-range temporal patterns from historical traffic observations. By decoupling spatial representation learning from temporal reasoning, the proposed approach provides an effective mechanism for large-scale spatiotemporal traffic modeling. Extensive experiments on a real-world cellular network dataset demonstrate that the proposed graph Transformer consistently outperforms recurrent graph-based baselines, including GCN-RNN, GCN-LSTM, and GCN-GRU models, across multiple forecasting horizons. The resulting forecasts enable more effective proactive resource provisioning and reduce overload risk compared with reactive management strategies. These results highlight the potential of graph-enhanced attention mechanisms for building intelligent and adaptive edge computing systems.

</details>


### [218] [SpecDrop: Parameter-Free Category-Conditioned Routing for Modular Specialization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04084)
*Boyao Wang,Zhihan Lei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过提出SpecDrop（一种无参数、固定权重的路由方案）来探究MoE网络中路由性能瓶颈的本质。实验表明，当标签粒度与训练目标严格对齐时（如图像分类），SpecDrop能显著超越参数匹配的基线；当训练单元跨越多个类别（模糊划分）时，路由机制的优势消失。结论是：划分粒度与训练信号的对齐，而非路由算法本身，决定了MoE路由能否带来增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE网络通过学得的路由器、门控和负载均衡损失追求专家专业化，但在总参数量匹配的情况下，学得路由器可能不如等权重的No-Routing基线。作者希望定位瓶颈：是路由算法本身有问题，还是训练信号粒度与目标类别之间的对齐不匹配。

Method: 提出SpecDrop：一种固定的无参数路由方案。每个分支K对指定类别分配权重p_a，对其他类别分配小泄露权重p_i（>0），通过类别无关的固定分母进行合并，不学习路由参数，也没有辅助损失，但推理时需要类别标签。在清晰划分任务（CIFAR-100/ResNet-110、ImageNet-1K/ViT-S/16）和模糊划分任务（SlimPajama-6B语言建模、SuperNI指令微调）上分别测试。

Result: CIFAR-100达79.23%，ImageNet-1K达79.89%，分别超过不使用标签的参数匹配基线4.75和6.53个点。但对比使用相同标签的部署方式，直接掩码稠密模型输出可得更高精度（85.2/83.7）。SpecDrop实现了58%/100%的分支-类别对齐，掩码增益仅为0.00（CIFAR）/+1.06（ImageNet），说明输出空间限制在训练中被内部化了。在模糊划分任务中，路由机制退化为匹配的No-Routing对照（在种子噪声范围内）。
