A branch-derived recoverability proxy achieves an AUC of 1.000, substantially outperforming divergence alone at 0.392. Across frozen evaluations, recoverability-aware control achieves the strongest recorded performance, reaching 0.578 success on held-out AIME2025 compared with 0.517 for the best baseline. It also improves AIME2024-2025 average@32 from 0.2656 to 0.3125 and GPQA-Diamond average@32 from 0.2702 to 0.3070. Component ablations further show that retaining teacher-correctable prefixes provides the largest individual contribution. These findings establish recoverability as an outcome-grounded decision variable for selective supervision in OPD.

</details>


### [252] [SPOT: Sparse Probing and Outcome Calibration for On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04419)
*Zikun Qu,Min Zhang,Mingze Kong,Zhiwei Shang,Yikun Ban,Shuang Qiu,Zhongxiang Dai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SPOT是一种针对on-policy蒸馏（OPD）的改进方法，通过采集-探索-利用三阶段流程解决"在哪里探测"和"蒸馏什么"两个耦合决策问题，在多个学生模型和推理基准上提升了推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准反向KL训练在OPD中可能给其他合理延续分配不足的概率；教师熵无法区分不确定性集中于少数候选还是分散于长尾，也无法反映学生是否已覆盖这些候选；局部教师概率无法预测下游成功。因此需要同时解决探测位置的选择和蒸馏目标的确定。

Method: SPOT包含三个阶段：(1) 采集阶段：利用融合归一化教师熵、top-k候选集捕获概率质量、学生-教师不匹配度的位置级评分，在有限探测预算内分配探测位置；(2) 探索阶段：通过验证器评分的学生延续来评估教师提出的候选；(3) 利用阶段：基于下游结果生成闭合形式的KL正则化目标，倾向于结果更好的候选，同时锚定教师分布。

Result: 在多个学生模型和推理基准上的大量实验表明，SPOT在提升推理性能的同时能平衡解决方案质量和覆盖度。

Conclusion: SPOT通过将探测预算分配给信息量最高的位置，并利用下游结果校准蒸馏目标，有效解决了OPD中探测与蒸馏的耦合决策问题，实现了更好的推理表现。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) provides dense teacher supervision on student-generated trajectories, but standard reverse-KL training can assign insufficient probability to other plausible continuations. Teacher entropy alone does not reveal whether uncertainty is concentrated among a few plausible next tokens or dispersed over a long probability tail, nor whether the student already represents those candidates well. Moreover, local teacher probabilities may not predict downstream success. We introduce Sparse Probing and Outcome-calibrated Targets OPD (SPOT), which addresses two coupled decisions, where to probe and what to distill, through an acquisition--exploration--exploitation procedure. During acquisition, a position-level score combines normalized teacher entropy, the probability mass captured by a small top-$k$ candidate set, and student--teacher mismatch to allocate a limited probing budget. During exploration, SPOT evaluates teacher-proposed candidates through verifier-scored student continuations. During exploitation, these outcomes produce a closed-form, KL-regularized target that favors candidates with better downstream outcomes while remaining anchored to the teacher distribution. Extensive experiments across multiple student models and reasoning benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of SPOT in improving reasoning performance while balancing solution quality and coverage.

</details>


### [253] [Generative Optimization for Incentivized Advertising with Global Level Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04421)
*Gege Chen,Ning Luo,Hao Jiang,Da Li,Wenzheng Shu,Teng Sha,Yanxiang Zeng,Wenxin Tai,Fan Zhou,Xialong Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GOAL框架，将激励广告中的激励额度分配建模为条件序列生成问题，结合层次化因果状态编码器，并引入SCPO算法在不重新训练的情况下泛化至多种ROI约束，提升长期收益和用户留存率，降低ROI违规率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激励广告中的奖励分配面临连续性激励额度优化、严格全局约束、高频交互、延迟反馈以及非马尔可夫用户动态（如疲劳效应）等挑战，现有的提升建模和约束强化学习方法效果有限。

Method: 提出GOAL框架，将激励分配建模为条件序列生成问题，直接依据用户历史和系统全局压力生成激励额度；集成层次化因果状态编码器以捕获局部行为动态和长程依赖；引入安全约束策略优化（SCPO），学习单一生成策略以泛化至不同ROI约束而无需重训练。

Result: 在大规模真实数据和合成疲劳感知环境上的实验表明，GOAL在长期收益和用户留存方面优于强基线，同时显著降低ROI违规率。

Conclusion: GOAL框架有效解决了激励广告中约束感知的连续激励分配问题，在满足ROI约束的同时提升了用户长期参与度和收益表现。

Abstract: Incentivized advertising allocates monetary or virtual rewards to drive user engagement, where a key challenge is optimizing continuous incentive magnitudes under strict global constraints. This problem is complicated by high-frequency interactions, delayed feedback, and non-Markovian user dynamics such as fatigue, which limit the effectiveness of existing uplift modeling and constrained reinforcement learning approaches. To address these challenges, we propose GOAL, a constraint-aware generative framework that formulates incentive allocation as a conditional sequence generation problem. GOAL directly generates incentive magnitudes conditioned on user histories and system-level global pressure, and integrates a hierarchical causal state encoder to capture both local behavioral dynamics and long-range dependencies. To enable flexible constraint control, we introduce \textbf{S}afe \textbf{C}onstrained \textbf{P}olicy \textbf{O}ptimization (SCPO), which learns a single generative policy that generalizes across a spectrum of ROI constraints without retraining. Experiments on large-scale real-world data and a synthetic fatigue-aware environment show that GOAL improves long-term revenue and user retention while substantially reducing ROI violation rates compared to strong baselines.

</details>


### [254] [Robustness Emerges Early in Training Dynamics, but Is Not Preserved](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04442)
*Jiangang Yang,Wenhui Shi,Lu Hu,Jing Xing,Jian Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现深度神经网络在训练早期阶段，浅层会自发产生鲁棒表示和平坦的损失景观，但在标准收敛过程中这些特性会消失（即"鲁棒性消失"现象）。为此提出一个无需修改架构或引入可学习参数的框架，包含早期阶段稳定化（EPS）和不对称权重回退（AWR）两种参数无关策略，在多个基准和架构上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深层神经网络对自然损坏（如噪声、模糊等）的鲁棒性仍是根本性挑战。作者在研究中发现了一个"鲁棒性消失"现象：训练早期浅层就展现出鲁棒表示和平坦损失景观，但这些有益的先验特性在标准收敛过程中未被保留，导致最终模型鲁棒性下降。

Method: 提出一个对训练动态进行战略性干预的框架，旨在稳定经验发现的早期涌现鲁棒先验。具体包含两种无需可学习参数的策略：1）早期阶段稳定化（EPS）：在训练早期稳定浅层的鲁棒配置；2）不对称权重回退（AWR）：恢复浅层到先前鲁棒状态。两者均不修改模型架构或引入额外可学习参数。

Result: 在多个基准数据集和不同网络架构上进行了广泛实验，该框架在下游迁移、动态适应以及多种计算机视觉应用中均取得了显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过稳定训练早期涌现的鲁棒浅层先验，可以有效抵抗"鲁棒性消失"现象，在不增加模型复杂度的前提下显著提升模型的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Robustness to natural corruptions remains a fundamental challenge for deep neural networks. In this paper, we identify a robustness fading phenomenon where shallow layers spontaneously develop robust representations and flat loss landscapes in early training, yet these properties are not preserved during standard convergence. To address this, we propose a framework that performs strategic interventions on training dynamics to stabilize the empirically identified early-emergent robust priors. Our approach includes two parameter-free strategies: Early-Phase Stabilization~(EPS) and Asymmetric Weight Reversion~(AWR), which stabilize or recover robust shallow configurations without modifying the model architecture or introducing learnable parameters. Extensive experiments demonstrate the efficacy of our framework across various benchmarks and architectures, yielding significant gains in downstream transfer, dynamic adaptation, and diverse computer vision applications.

</details>


### [255] [Tropical Algebraic Geometry for Neuronal Representations: An Arakelov-Green Measure Based Descriptor for Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04460)
*Yuyang Zhang,Weihan Xu,Xuehai Zhou,Shucheng Cao,Qihuang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于热带代数几何的无训练几何先验方法，通过热带Abel-Jacobi变换和极化距离将3D神经元形态的树结构转换为循环度量图并嵌入热带Jacobian流形，绕过NP难的格上最近向量问题（CVP），采用Albanese环面万有覆盖上的连续松弛，利用图拉普拉斯广义逆的闭式解精确分解离散Arakelov-Green测度。该方法在BREC基准上超越1-WL表达力，并在ACT-4、JML-4、BIL-6等3D形态数据集上无需额外可训练参数即可无缝集成到标准架构中，提升分类精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有消息传递GNN受限于1-WL测试，无法捕捉空间邻近性诱导的环结构。3D神经元形态的定量分析需要同时捕捉图拓扑与空间几何，而当前方法在图论表达力上存在根本性瓶颈，亟需一种能超越1-WL限制且训练-free的几何先验。

Method: 提出一个结构变换管线：(1) 环空间增广与商空间构造，将空间树转换为循环度量图；(2) 将度量图嵌入热带Jacobian（Albanese环面）；(3) 放弃含量化误差的显式近似（如Babai舍入），在万有覆盖上采用连续松弛，证明离散Arakelov-Green测度（通过图拉普拉斯广义逆闭式计算）可精确分解为内在路径度量减去未量化的极化距离；(4) 导出两个描述符：特征向量提供节点级结构坐标，置换不变的谱提供图级签名。

Result: 在BREC基准上，特征向量表述展示了超越1-WL限制的表达力。在ACT-4、JML-4、BIL-6三个3D形态数据集上，谱特征无需额外可训练参数即可无缝集成到VAE、GNN、Tree-LSTM等标准架构中，优于显式格近似方法，且分类准确率超过现有空间模型。

Conclusion: 热带几何框架为树结构3D形态数据提供了理论上有保证且计算上可行的几何先验，其核心贡献在于用连续松弛规避NP难CVP问题，实现精确度量的闭式分解，使其成为即插即用的增强模块，可显著提升标准深度学习架构在空间图数据上的表达力与分类性能。

Abstract: The quantitative analysis of 3D neuronal morphologies requires capturing both graph topology and spatial geometry. Current message-passing Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) are bounded by the 1-Weisfeiler-Lehman (1-WL) test, limiting their ability to capture cycles induced by spatial proximities. To address this, we propose a training-free geometric prior based on tropical algebraic geometry. We apply the recently established tropical Abel-Jacobi transform and polarization distances to machine learning on tree-structured data. We introduce a structural transformation pipeline, comprising cycle space augmentation and quotient space construction, to convert spatial trees into cyclic metric graphs suitable for embedding into the Tropical Jacobian. Computing exact tropical polarization distances requires solving the NP-Hard Closest Vector Problem (CVP) on integer lattices. Instead of relying on explicit approximations with quantization errors (e.g., Babai's rounding), we adopt a continuous relaxation on the universal cover of the Albanese torus. We show that the discrete Arakelov-Green measure, computed in closed form via the graph Laplacian's generalized inverse, decomposes exactly into the intrinsic path metric minus the unquantized polarization distance on this cover, avoiding integer lattice searches. This metric yields two descriptors: eigenvectors provide node-level structural coordinates, and the permutation-invariant eigenvalue spectrum provides a graph-level signature. On the BREC benchmark, the eigenvector formulation demonstrates expressivity beyond the 1-WL limit. On 3D morphology datasets (ACT-4, JML-4, BIL-6), the spectrum seamlessly integrates into standard architectures (VAEs, GNNs, Tree-LSTMs) without additional trainable parameters, outperforming explicit lattice approximations and improving classification accuracy over existing spatial models.

</details>


### [256] [Beyond Linear Dynamics: Neural Bilinear Dynamical Models for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04471)
*Mengzhou Gao,Huangqian Yu,Pengfei Jiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出神经双线性动力学模型（NBDM），基于Koopman理论将非线性动力学升维到高维潜空间，通过双线性动力学模型刻画状态演化，并引入参数化误差补偿项以减小近似误差。该方法显式整合控制输入，在缺失控制输入时通过记忆增强控制器推断潜控制信号。在五个真实数据集上，NBDM在给定控制和缺失控制两种设定下均优于多个基线，尤其在多步和长时程预测中表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界时间序列常由非线性动力系统生成，现有显式建模系统动力学的方法依赖线性假设或Koopman线性化，难以充分捕捉复杂非线性行为，且在长时程预测中误差累积严重。

Method: NBDM利用Koopman理论将原始非线性动力学提升到高维潜空间，构建双线性潜动力学模型刻画状态演化；通过参数化误差补偿项缓解双线性表示的近似误差；显式将控制输入整合进动力学框架（有辅助变量时直接使用，否则用学习到的反馈信号）；针对控制输入缺失场景，设计记忆增强控制器，通过历史状态与控制信号的乘法交互推断潜控制信号。

Result: 在五个真实世界数据集上，NBDM在给定控制和缺失控制两种设置下均一致优于竞争基线，多步和长时程预测场景下优势尤为明显。

Conclusion: NBDM通过双线性潜动力学建模与误差补偿机制，有效提升了非线性动力系统时间序列的预测精度，尤其是在长时程预测中显著缓解了误差累积问题，为非线性动态系统建模提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Time series in real-world applications are often generated by nonlinear dynamical systems, making accurate forecasting challenging. Existing approaches that explicitly model system dynamics typically rely on linear assumptions or Koopman-based linearizations, which may inadequately capture complex nonlinear behaviors and lead to error accumulation in long-horizon prediction. To address this limitation, we propose the Neural Bilinear Dynamical Model (NBDM), which models nonlinear system dynamics through a bilinear latent dynamical formulation. Specifically, NBDM leverages Koopman theory to lift the original nonlinear dynamics into a higher-dimensional latent space, where a bilinear dynamical model is constructed to characterize state evolution. To mitigate the approximation error introduced by bilinear representations, we further incorporate a parameterized error compensation term. Within this formulation, control inputs are explicitly integrated into the dynamics, using auxiliary variables when available and learned feedback signals otherwise. To handle scenarios with missing control inputs, we design a memory-enhanced controller that infers latent controls through multiplicative interactions between historical states and control signals. Experiments on five real-world datasets demonstrate that NBDM consistently outperforms competitive baselines in both given-control and missing-control settings, particularly for multi-step and long-horizon forecasting.

</details>


### [257] [Local Violation Certification for Linear Predict-Then-Optimize Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04474)
*Ş. İlker Birbil,Wenhao Chi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个针对线性决策管线在输入不确定性下的局部违规认证框架，通过单次优化求解以闭式方式直接计算失败风险，并提供精确采样程序和闭式风险统计量以实现特征级归因，避免了传统重复随机测试在罕见失效场景下的计算瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动的决策管线（预测模型+下游优化）在高风险运营决策中越来越普遍，但认证其安全性、公平性和可靠性至关重要。传统场景生成方法依赖重复随机测试，当失效事件罕见时计算上不可行，且难以解释失效原因。标准采样方法在罕见违规场景下效率低下，亟需直接的结构化方法。

Method: 通过分析部署管线的固定决策边界，利用单个优化求解以闭式形式直接计算局部失败风险。具体包括：(1) 数学证明标准采样方法在罕见违规场景下低效；(2) 提出精确采样程序；(3) 推导闭式风险统计量，提供特征级归因（识别哪些输入特征对潜在不合规贡献最大）；(4) 无需重复随机试验或复杂采样算法。

Result: 在经济电力调度系统（受排放法规约束）上的实验表明，该方法能以传统计算方法一小部分的计算成本，提供精确、可审计的风险评估。

Conclusion: 该框架为线性决策管线提供了高效的局部违规认证方法，克服了传统采样方法在罕见失效下的计算瓶颈，同时提供了特征级归因能力，增强了决策的可审计性和可解释性。

Abstract: Data-driven decision pipelines combining predictive machine learning models with downstream optimization software are increasingly used to make high-stakes operational decisions. Certifying the safety, fairness, and reliability of these decisions is essential, yet traditional scenario generation methods rely on repeated random testing, which becomes computationally prohibitive when failure events are rare and offers little insight into why failures occur. We present a framework for local violation certification designed specifically for linear decision pipelines under input uncertainty. We mathematically demonstrate that standard sampling methods fail efficiently for rare violations, motivating a direct structural approach. By analyzing the fixed decision boundary of a deployed pipeline, we show that the local risk of failure can be calculated directly in closed form using a single optimization solve. Furthermore, we introduce an exact sampling procedure and closed-form risk statistics that provide feature-level attributions (identifying which input characteristics contribute most to potential non-compliance) without requiring repetitive random trials or complex sampling algorithms. We demonstrate our approach on an economic power dispatch system subject to emissions regulations, delivering precise, auditable risk assessments at a fraction of the traditional computational cost.

</details>


### [258] [Discretization and Statistical Consistency of Functional Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04531)
*Lennon J. Shikhman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了函数分布上的流匹配（functional flow matching）在有限系数/点值实现下的强L²收敛性，并给出了端到端Wasserstein界，适用于非嵌套σ-代数（散乱/自适应细化）场景，通过正则性空间、迹类高斯反例及Bernstein论证等完成了各技术环节的闭环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 函数空间上的流匹配是一种新范式，但其实际实现通常基于有限个系数或点值。当采用散乱采样或自适应细化时，相应的条件σ-代数不再嵌套，经典的鞅收敛理论无法证明传感器极限（sensor limit）的收敛性。本文旨在严格建立这一极限的数学模型，并给出端到端的误差界，以支撑学习算法的可证明性。

Method: 采用泛函分析和概率论工具：对于每个强一致的有限秩重建序列，证明有限条件速度目标的强L²收敛；针对正交投影给出量化界，并通过正则性空间将结果推广到点传感器情形。在学习流程中，将流直接耦合到总体叠加路径（population superposition path），无需假设总体有限维ODE的唯一性即可获得端到端Wasserstein界。具体验证包括：归一化求积神经算子（含全局Lipschitz激活的显式幅值递推）、非交换迹类高斯范例（对比投影限制与精确条件化的边界乘子）、空间正则性-求积证书闭合算子实现项，以及Bernstein论证给出固定模型维数与包络下的Õ(n^{-1})超风险项。

Result: 证明了有限条件速度目标在强一致有限秩重建下的强L²收敛；获得端到端Wasserstein界；非交换迹类高斯示例在投影限制下边界乘子为0，在精确条件化下为0.72；Bernstein论证给出Õ(n^{-1})超风险上界；在精确可实现的高斯缩放特例下得到了显式的端到端收敛速率。

Conclusion: 本文建立了函数流匹配在传感器极限下的严格收敛理论，即使σ-代数不嵌套也能保证收敛性与端到端误差界，为基于散乱/自适应细分的函数流匹配学习提供了理论保障。

Abstract: Functional flow matching is posed on distributions of functions but implemented from finitely many coefficients or point values. Under scattered or adaptive refinement, the resulting conditioning sigma-algebras need not be nested, so martingale convergence does not justify the sensor limit. We prove strong $L^2$ convergence of finite conditional velocity targets for every strongly consistent sequence of finite-rank reconstructions, with quantitative bounds for orthogonal projections and a point-sensor extension through a regularity space. For learned flows, coupling directly to a population superposition path yields an end-to-end Wasserstein bound without assuming uniqueness of the population finite-dimensional ODE. We verify sensor-independent constants for a normalized quadrature neural operator, including globally Lipschitz activations through an explicit magnitude recurrence. A noncommuting trace-class Gaussian example gives boundary multiplier $0$ under projected restriction and $0.72$ under exact conditioning. A spatial regularity--cubature certificate closes the operator-realization term, a Bernstein argument gives a $\widetilde{O}(n^{-1})$ excess-risk term for fixed model dimension and envelopes, and an exactly realizable clipped Gaussian scaling specialization yields an explicit end-to-end rate.

</details>


### [259] [Learning Compression Rules for Network Traffic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04545)
*Quentin Lampin,Éloi Sainte-Beuve,Louis-Adrien Dufrène,Guillaume Larue,Massih-Reza Amini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RECAP框架，通过无监督结构发现和受限规则选择两个阶段，自动学习紧凑的基于规则的头压缩器，用于结构化网络流量的压缩，并在SCHC（IETF标准）上验证其优于专家设计的规则集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络数据包的头字段在流内高度冗余，传统规则式压缩（如SCHC）依赖专家手工设计规则，成本高且难以适应不同网络场景。需要一种自动学习紧凑规则集的方法来替代人工设计。

Method: 将规则学习建模为两阶段问题：（1）无监督结构发现阶段，使用对小样本鲁棒的归一化熵比准则递归划分训练数据包；（2）受限选择阶段，利用动态规划在规则数量硬预算约束下挑选最大化期望压缩增益的规则子集。具体实例化为RECAP方法，应用于SCHC标准。

Result: 在四个真实物联网和5G核心网络数据集上，RECAP以少量学习到的规则超越了专家手工设计的规则集，消除了人工规则设计的需求。

Conclusion: RECAP能够自动学习紧凑且高效的规则式头压缩器，在受限网络环境中以少量规则达到甚至超过专家设计的性能，为规则式压缩的自动化设计提供了有效方案。

Abstract: We study the problem of learning compact rule-based compressors for structured network traffic. Each packet is a record of header fields that are highly redundant within a flow, and a compressor is a small set of rules matching such records and replacing predictable fields with short codes. We cast rule learning as a two-stage problem: (i) an unsupervised structure-discovery stage that recursively partitions training packets using a normalized entropy-ratio criterion robust to small samples, and (ii) a constrained selection stage that uses dynamic programming to pick the rule subset maximizing expected compression gain under a hard budget on the number of installable rules. We instantiate the framework on Static Context Header Compression (SCHC), the IETF standard for rule-based header compression in constrained networks, and evaluate it on four real-world Internet-of-Things and 5G core-network datasets. Our method, Robust Entropy Clustering for Adaptive comPression (RECAP), surpasses expert-engineered rule sets with a small number of learned rules and removes the need for manual rule design.

</details>


### [260] [A Model Merging Approach for Continual MLLM Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04548)
*Yuhang Wang,Linlin Zhang,Haoxuan Ji,Xianmin Ye,Zhenxing Niu,Haichang Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MCU（Merging for Continual Unlearning），一种面向多模态大语言模型（MLLM）持续遗忘场景的新方法。MCU通过动态合并多个一次性遗忘适配器为统一适配器，利用留一合并分析揭示遗忘适配器间存在强交叉任务依赖（既有促进跨任务遗忘迁移的正面效应，也有干扰遗忘效果并损害保留知识的负面效应），并通过将适配器投影到共享表示空间、保留主导方向、抑制过度集中坐标并重构交叉任务依赖来缓解干扰、增强迁移性。在ICU-Bench和MLLMU-Bench上，MCU在遗忘有效性、保留知识和通用多模态效用方面均表现优越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM遗忘方法主要面向一次性请求设计，在持续遗忘场景下反复应用一次性操作会导致累积效用退化、遗忘回弹和保留知识漂移等问题，缺乏有效的持续遗忘解决方案。

Method: MCU通过以下方式实现持续遗忘：1）动态将多个一次性遗忘适配器合并为统一适配器；2）通过留一合并分析揭示适配器间的交叉任务依赖；3）将适配器投影到共享表示空间；4）保留主导方向并抑制过度集中坐标；5）重新配置交叉任务依赖以在缓解干扰的同时增强跨任务遗忘迁移性。

Result: 在ICU-Bench和MLLMU-Bench两个基准上的实验表明，MCU在遗忘有效性上优于现有方法，同时能够保持保留知识和通用多模态效用。

Conclusion: MCU通过适配器合并策略有效解决了MLLM持续遗忘中的关键挑战，为多模态模型持续遗忘提供了可行方案，在遗忘效果与知识保留之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Multimodal large language model (MLLM) unlearning methods have been proposed to remove private, sensitive, or proprietary information from well-trained models. However, most existing MLLM unlearning methods are designed for one-shot requests and fail to adequately address continual scenarios, as repeatedly applying one-shot operations leads to cumulative utility degradation, unlearning rebound, and retention drift. We introduce Merging for Continual Unlearning (MCU), an approach that dynamically merges multiple one-shot unlearning adapters into a unified adapter upon receiving each new unlearning request.Through a leave-one-out merging analysis, we reveal that these unlearning adapters exhibit strong cross-task dependencies. Such dependencies have two contrasting effects: they can facilitate cross-task unlearning transferability, but they can also introduce severe interference that degrades unlearning effectiveness and compromises retained knowledge. To address this challenge, MCU projects the adapters into a shared representation space, preserves their dominant directions, suppresses over-concentrated coordinates, and reconfigures cross-task dependencies to mitigate interference while enhancing transferability. Experiments on ICU-Bench and MLLMU-Bench demonstrate that MCU achieves superior unlearning effectiveness while preserving both retained knowledge and general multimodal utility.

</details>


### [261] [Rethinking Reservoir Pruning: A Dynamical Perspective for Echo State Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04593)
*Sudip Laudari,Puspa Raj Adhikari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于动力学模式剪枝（DMP）的储层剪枝方法，通过轨迹平均Jacobian Gramian获得主导转移模式，根据神经元对主导模式的贡献进行排序并移除低影响单元，仅重训读出层，在混沌和真实时序基准上提升或保持预测精度并减少冗余储层组件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回声状态网络（ESN）的随机初始化储层常常过参数化且存在动态冗余；现有剪枝方法多依赖静态连接性或激活统计，可能忽略了塑造输入驱动状态转移的关键神经元。

Method: DMP方法：通过轨迹平均Jacobian Gramian获得主导转移模式，据此对神经元的动力学贡献进行排名，剪除低影响单元，仅重训读出层。

Result: 在混沌系统和真实世界时序基准上，DMP提升或保持了预测精度，同时减少了冗余的储层组件数量。

Conclusion: 动力学影响是储层精细化的重要判据，超越了单纯静态结构重要性的考量。

Abstract: Echo State Networks (ESNs) offer an efficient framework for temporal prediction, but their randomly initialized reservoirs are often over-parameterized and dynamically redundant. Existing pruning methods largely rely on static connectivity or activation statistics, which may overlook neurons that shape input-driven state transitions. We propose Dynamical Mode Pruning (DMP), a reservoir pruning method that ranks neurons by their contribution to dominant transition modes obtained from a trajectory-averaged Jacobian Gramian. DMP removes low-impact units and retrains only the readout. Experiments on chaotic and real-world time-series benchmarks show that DMP improves or preserves forecasting accuracy while reducing redundant reservoir components. Our results suggest that dynamical influence is a useful criterion for reservoir refinement beyond static structural importance alone.

</details>


### [262] [Why Ranking Anomaly Detection Algorithms Isn't as Reliable as You May Think](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04613)
*Simon Klüttermann,Jérôme Rutinowski,Frederik Polachowski,Alice Kirchheim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了异常检测算法基准测试中常见选择对算法排名稳定性的影响，使用7种代表性算法和690个数据集，引入排名不稳定性指标，发现算法排名高度不稳定，数据集选择和超参数配置是主要影响因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大量异常检测算法声称达到了最佳性能，但不同研究的基准测试设置不一致，缺乏可比性，这引发了对异常检测基准测试可复现性和可靠性的担忧。作者旨在系统性地研究基准测试选择对算法排名稳定性的影响。

Method: 使用7种代表性异常检测算法和来自OddBench基准套件的690个数据集，分析在数据集选择、评估指标、超参数配置和随机种子等不同基准设置下算法排名的变化。引入了一种排名不稳定性指标来量化算法排名在不同基准设置下的变异性。

Result: 异常检测中的算法排名高度不稳定，在多种情况下几乎每种有竞争力的算法都可以在某种基准配置下成为最佳方法。其中数据集选择和超参数配置对排名不确定性贡献最大，随机种子和评估指标的影响相对有限。可靠的基准测试需要比先前工作更庞大、更多样化的数据集集合。

Conclusion: 异常检测基准测试的可靠性面临严重挑战，算法排名对基准设置高度敏感。为了获得稳定可信的排名，需要更大规模、更多样化的数据集集合，并应关注数据集选择和超参数配置这两个主要影响因素。

Abstract: Anomaly detection is a safety-critical machine learning problem with applications ranging from fraud detection to network intrusion prevention and industrial monitoring. Despite the large number of proposed anomaly detection algorithms, many novel methods claim state-of-the-art performance. However, many authors do so under benchmark settings that are not aligned with one another. This lack of comparability raises concerns regarding the reproducibility and reliability of anomaly detection benchmarks.
  In this work, we study the impact of common benchmarking choices on the stability of algorithm rankings. Using seven representative anomaly detection algorithms and 690 datasets from the OddBench benchmark suite, we analyze how rankings change under varying dataset selections, evaluation metrics, hyperparameter configurations, and random seeds. To quantify this effect, we introduce a rank instability metric measuring the variability of algorithm rankings across benchmark settings.
  Our results show that algorithm rankings in anomaly detection are highly unstable. In many cases, almost every competitive algorithm can appear as the best-performing method under some benchmark configuration. Among the studied factors, dataset selection and hyperparameter choice contribute most strongly to ranking uncertainty, while random seeds and evaluation metrics have a comparatively limited impact. We also observe that reliable benchmarking requires substantially larger and more diverse dataset collections than the ones commonly used in prior work.

</details>


### [263] [An entropic explanation of insistence on sameness in autism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04616)
*Przemysław Śliwiński*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于信息论的框架，将自闭症中对同一性的坚持（insistence on sameness）解释为个体试图最小化惊奇与不确定性的通用行为模式。该框架将自闭症重新定义为认知功能局限于对环境可感知属性的辨别、记忆和预测的障碍，并通过熵度量公式 D_H(R, M) 建立了类比分析，得出坚持同一性是限制刺激于已知记忆范围这一行为的表现。框架还提供了量化工具、治疗指南以及无需人类受试者的类图灵测试验证方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 试图为自闭症中"坚持同一性"这一核心行为特征提供一个统一的理论解释框架，将其置于信息论的一般行为模式下理解，从而超越纯粹的描述性观察，并为治疗提供形式化基础。

Method: 采用信息论框架，定义自闭症个体的认知限制为仅能进行辨别、记忆和预测环境中的可感知属性。引入双向条件熵度量 D_H(R, M) = H(R|M) + H(M|R) 作为惊奇与不确定性的量化指标，通过逻辑推断分析该度量的两种最小化途径（学习更多关于R的知识或限制R为已知的M），并论证自闭症个体倾向于后者。同时提出类图灵测试的验证方法和治疗作为优化算法的形式化设计思路。

Result: 框架成功将诸如惊奇、不确定性、感官过载与剥夺、焦虑、舒适区、失望、迷失、迂腐、刻板等概念量化；导出了一系列学习疗法和日常护理指南，可将其定义为优化算法并实现为机器人护理程序；提出了无需涉及自闭症个体的类图灵测试验证方案。

Conclusion: 该框架若得到积极验证，将为改善自闭症个体日常生活自理能力的治疗提供形式化基础和设计准则，但作者也承认其有效性仍需通过所提出的验证途径加以确认。

Abstract: An information theory-based framework is proposed in attempt to explain insistence on sameness in autism as an instance of a general behavior pattern in which an individual tries to reduce surprise and uncertainty. It offers a new definition of autism as an impairment in which cognitive functions are restricted to discrimination, memorization and prediction of tangible properties of the environment. An analogy between insistence on sameness and constrained minimization of the entropy metric is observed and examined for a set of assumptions that describe cognitive limitations of a person with autism. The metric is given by the formula $D_H(R, M) = H(R|M) + H(M|R)$, where $R$ represents sequences of random stimuli, $M$ is a memory that stores and retrieves them, and where $H(.|.)$ denotes their conditional entropies interpreted as surprise and uncertainty, respectively. It is first inferred that to minimize the metric an individual can learn about $R$ (and store that knowledge in $M$) or can restrict $R$ to the already known $M$. Then, it is concluded that insistence on sameness is a manifestation of the latter. Moreover, it is shown that the proposed framework: (1) Helps to quantify the concepts of surprise, uncertainty, sensory overload and deprivation, anxiety, comfort zone, disappointment, disorientation, pedantry, rigidness, observance or aberrant precision. (2) Leads to a list of guidelines for learning therapies and daily care routines, and allows them to be defined as optimization algorithms and implemented as programs for robotic live-in caregivers. (3) Can be validated with the help of a Turing test-like approach that requires no experiments involving individuals with autism. The framework-if positively validated-will provide formal foundations and design guidelines for therapies aimed at improving self-reliance of individuals with autism in basic activities of daily living.

</details>


### [264] [Informational Frustration in Neural Manifolds: Shannon Bottlenecks and the Limits of Learnability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30512)
*Srinivasa Rao P.,Vangmayi P Reddy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统-框架，将信息论、拓扑学和统计力学联系起来，解释深度网络为何能泛化。核心概念是"熵可学习性地平线"（ELH）定律，以及"香农-拓扑瓶颈定理"，用于刻画深度学习的硬性极限。文中还提出了"熵梯度下降"（EGD）算法来动态管理权重熵，并重新定位"grokking"现象为"熵释放"过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典理论框架（如VC维、Rademacher复杂度）预测现代过参数化深度网络会严重过拟合，这与现实中模型出色的泛化能力形成巨大理论鸿沟。作者希望弥合这一理论与实践之间的差距，为深度学习的泛化能力提供新的理论解释。

Method: 作者构建了一个统一框架，融合信息论（香农熵）、拓扑学（决策边界的拓扑熵）和统计力学（冯诺依曼熵、相变理论）。核心方法包括：(1) 提出熵可学习性地平线（ELH）定律，比较数据流形的香农熵与决策边界的拓扑熵以及权重空间的冯诺依曼熵；(2) 证明香农-拓扑瓶颈定理，说明几何复杂度超过信息地平线时系统发生熵相变；(3) 将grokking解释为熵释放过程；(4) 提出熵梯度下降（EGD）算法来动态管理权重熵。

Result: 该框架证明了当一个目标边界的几何复杂度超过信息地平线时，系统会经历突发的熵相变，进入"信息挫折"状态——一种类玻璃态的刚性记忆阶段，此时泛化在热力学意义上不可能实现。同时，grokking现象被重新解释为熵释放过程，权重突然重组以解锁信息瓶颈。提出了EGD算法以实际应用该理论。

Conclusion: 本文重新定义了熵在深度学习中的角色：熵不仅是追踪不确定性的工具，更是决定机器能否学习的根本物理货币。该工作为过参数化深度网络的泛化能力提供了新的理论视角，弥合了经典学习理论与现代深度学习实践之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Why overparameterised deep networks generalise so remarkably well remains one of the most stubborn open questions in machine learning theory. Classical frameworks like VC dimension and Rademacher complexity predict catastrophic overfitting in modern models, leaving a massive theoretical gap between theory and reality. In this paper, we bridge this divide by introducing a unified framework that links information theory, topology, and statistical mechanics to map the hard limits of deep learning. Central to our approach is the Entropic Learnability Horizon (ELH): a fundamental law stating that a network can only truly learn a target function if the Shannon entropy of the data manifold outpaces the topological entropy of the function's decision boundary, balanced by the von Neumann entropy of the network's weight space. We establish the Shannon-Topological Bottleneck Theorem, proving that when a target boundary's geometric complexity exceeds this informational horizon, the system undergoes a sudden entropic phase transition. It falls into a state of Informational Frustration - a glassy, rigid memorization phase where generalization becomes thermodynamically impossible. Using this lens, we show that the enigmatic phenomenon of "grokking" is actually an Entropic Release, where weights abruptly reorganise to unlock the bottleneck. Finally, we translate this theory into practice with Entropic Gradient Descent (EGD), an optimization algorithm that dynamically manages weight entropy to keep learning on track. Ultimately, this work repositions entropy not just as a tool for tracking uncertainty but as the fundamental physical currency that dictates whether a machine can learn.

</details>


### [265] [Active Learning Guided Design Space Refinement for Scalable Multi-Objective Bayesian Optimization in Materials Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04651)
*Alexandros Ntagiantas,Panagiotis Tsilimidos,George Giannakopoulos,Christoforos Rekatsinas,Panagiotis Krokidas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种主动学习引导的自适应搜索空间细化框架，结合多目标贝叶斯优化，通过将候选空间缩减约一半（同时保留99%以上原始超体积）来加速材料优化，在COF的CH4/N2分离和压力容器设计两个任务中验证了该方法能改善早期收敛性和累积Pareto前沿发现效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统贝叶斯优化在大规模离散设计空间中效率低下，经常会先评估大量低价值区域，难以快速到达信息丰富的区域，从而限制了在有限评估预算下的材料发现效率。

Method: 提出一个主动学习（AL）引导的自适应搜索空间细化框架，与多目标贝叶斯优化（MOBO）相结合，通过基于AL的候选空间采样与缩减，在保留Pareto相关区域的同时缩小搜索空间，从而提升优化效率。

Result: 在COF材料CH4/N2分离和压力容器设计（材料方向应力分量与厚度目标）两个案例中，AL引导的细化将候选空间缩减约一半，同时保留99%以上的原始超体积；缩减后的策略改善了BO的早期收敛性和累积Pareto前沿发现。

Conclusion: 该框架展示了一种高效的面向约束自主材料发现场景的大规模材料优化方法，能够在有限评估预算下实现更快、更有效的多目标优化。

Abstract: Advanced materials discovery increasingly relies on machine learning and Bayesian optimization to explore large discrete design spaces under limited evaluation budgets. However, conventional Bayesian optimization (BO) can become inefficient as candidate spaces grow, often evaluating low-value regions before reaching informative areas. We propose an active-learning (AL)-guided adaptive search-space refinement framework combined with multi-objective BO to accelerate materials optimization while preserving Pareto-relevant regions. We evaluate the approach on CH4/N2 separation in covalent-organic frameworks and pressure-vessel design with material-direction stress components and thickness objectives. Results show that the AL-guided refinement reduces the candidate space by approximately half while preserving more than 99 percent of the original hypervolume. The reduced-space strategy improves early convergence and cumulative Pareto-front discovery from the BO, demonstrating efficient large-scale materials optimization across constrained autonomous materials discovery settings.

</details>


### [266] [Statistical Mechanics of Learning on Product Wasserstein Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01434)
*Srinivasa Rao P Vangmayi P Reddy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将深度神经网络和变分量子电路的权重/参数分布约束视为定义学习内在几何的度量结构，而非单纯限制容量。通过在经典Wasserstein流形（每层一个）和量子Wasserstein流形（电路参数）的乘积上进行梯度流学习，并设计了两种算法（Hierarchical DisCo-SGD和Quantum DisCo），实验表明该方法相比无约束和纯范数约束的基线在泛化、训练稳定性和缓解贫瘠高原问题上均有改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统统计力学将权重分布约束视为缩小解空间、降低模型容量的限制。作者希望反转这一视角：与其把预设的权重分布当作约束，不如将其视为定义学习自然展开的内在几何。这种视角转换能为经典和量子学习系统提供统一的几何框架，并为融入先验分布信息（生物学、谱或硬件相关）开辟路径。

Method: 将深度神经网络和变分量子电路分别建模为Wasserstein流形乘积上的梯度流：每个经典层对应一个经典Wasserstein空间，电路参数对应一个量子Wasserstein空间（阶数为1）。提出分层平均场描述用于深度网络，并将框架推广到量子设置。以此为基础，设计了两种沿流形乘积近似测地线运动的实用算法：Hierarchical DisCo-SGD（经典深度网络）和Quantum DisCo（变分量子电路）。

Result: 在teacher-student问题、标准图像分类任务以及小型变分量子分类器上的实验表明：尊重分布几何的方法相比无约束和仅基于范数的基线，在泛化性能、训练稳定性方面有提升，并减少了量子电路训练中贫瘠高原（barren plateaus）的严重程度。

Conclusion: 该工作首次将结构约束重新定义为几何先验，表明分布约束带来的"容量减少"实际上是约束流形自身的度量结构。这一框架为将生物、谱或硬件来源的分布信息融入经典与量子学习系统提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Normally the statistical mechanics of learning treats constraints on weight distributions as restrictions that shrink the space of possible solutions. Therefore, it reduces model capacity. In this paper we would like to take a contrary approach, which, however, is based on the earlier work on distribution-constrained perceptrons. Rather than treating a prescribed weight distribution as a mere restriction, we propose that it defines the intrinsic geometry upon which learning naturally unfolds. We formulate both deep neural networks and variational quantum circuits as gradient flows on a product of Wasserstein manifolds -- one classical Wasserstein space for each layer and one quantum Wasserstein space for the circuit parameters. Within this geometry, the capacity reduction, which was previously associated with distributional constraints, appears as the metric structure of the constraint manifold itself. We develop a hierarchical mean-field description for deep networks, extend the framework to the quantum setting using the quantum Wasserstein distance of order 1, and introduce two such practical algorithms, Hierarchical DisCo-SGD and Quantum DisCo, that follow approximate geodesics on the manifold of the product itself. Experiments on teacher-student problems, standard image classification tasks, and small variational quantum classifiers show that respecting these distributional geometries improves generalization, stabilizes training, and reduces the severity of barren plateaus compared with unconstrained and purely norm-based baselines. This approach firstly reframes structural constraints as geometric priors and suggests a route for incorporating biological, spectral, or hardware-derived distributional information into both learning systems, viz., classical and quantum learning.

</details>


### [267] [Differentiating Through Dual Prices: End-to-End Policy Learning Under Capacity Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04669)
*Mohammadsaeed Haghi,Mahdi Salmani,Nima Kelidari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在稀缺资源（如住房援助、医院干预）按序到达且即时决策的场景下，如何从观测数据中学习分配策略。作者提出端到端训练方法，通过区分离线策略估计值对偶价格进行优化，相较传统的"决策盲目"（先拟合模型再定价）流水线，端到端方法在六个数据集上取得更优的部署调整价值，尤其在资源紧张时能更好地满足容量约束并减少排队延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法将学习过程分为两步：先独立拟合每个臂的结果模型，再基于拟合模型为资源定价并分配。这种"决策盲目"的流水线忽略了定价与模型拟合之间的耦合关系，导致在资源稀缺时学习出的策略可能违反容量约束或产生过长排队延迟。作者希望将模型训练与定价决策端到端联合优化，以提升部署场景下的实际价值。

Method: 作者提出端到端训练框架：将离线策略估计值对偶价格直接纳入损失函数进行反向传播。提出两种公式化方法：(1) 精确非凸公式；(2) 凸松弛，其最优解总是满足期望容量约束，且次优性上界为平滑温度线性项加臂数量的对数项。在资源按容量速率补充的排队模拟中评估所有方法。

Result: 在六个数据集的排队模拟中，两种端到端变体在所有延迟成本（包括零）下均占据部署调整价值指数的前两名。当容量受限时，决策盲目基线频繁违反容量约束并导致更长的排队延迟。在最大数据集（七万患者医院队列）上，端到端训练实现了显著更高的策略价值，且优势在容量匹配的神经基线面前依然保持。而灵活的决策盲目回归在可测量真实结果时仍是更优的纯预测器。

Conclusion: 端到端训练最适合资源真正稀缺且可行性（容量约束满足、排队延迟控制）至关重要的场景；而决策盲目回归在纯预测准确性方面仍有优势。研究结果为选择学习框架提供了依据：当可行性是核心关切时，端到端方法是更优选择。

Abstract: Many social services assign scarce resources, such as housing assistance or hospital interventions, to people who arrive one at a time: each arrival must receive a decision immediately, and the long-run usage of every resource must stay within its capacity. We study how to learn such an assignment policy from logged observational data. The standard pipeline is decision-blind: fit one outcome model per arm by regression, price each capacitated resource from the fitted models, and assign each arrival the arm whose predicted outcome minus price is largest. We instead train the outcome models end-to-end, differentiating an off-policy estimate of the deployed policy's value through the dual prices themselves. We study two formulations: an exact nonconvex one, and a convex relaxation whose optimum always satisfies the capacity constraints in expectation and which is suboptimal by at most a term linear in the smoothing temperature and logarithmic in the number of arms. Every method is evaluated in a queueing simulation with resources replenished at their capacity rates. Across six datasets, the two end-to-end variants take the top slots on a deployment-adjusted value index at every delay cost, including zero; when capacities are binding, decision-blind baselines frequently violate them and incur much longer queueing delays. On the largest dataset, a hospital cohort of seventy thousand patients, end-to-end training also achieves significantly higher policy value, a margin that survives a capacity-matched neural baseline. Flexible decision-blind regression remains the stronger pure predictor where ground truth is measurable; end-to-end training is best suited to settings where resources are genuinely scarce and feasibility matters.

</details>


### [268] [Diverse and Plausible Algorithmic Recourse via Tractable Recourse Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04677)
*Anagha Sabu,Hrithik Suresh,Narayanan C. Krishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出可处理的追索分布（Tractable Recourse Distributions），一个概率框架，将给定事实实例的可行替代方案空间表示为有利结果上的概率分布。该分布可通过指数倾斜概率电路获得精确表示，无需重训模型即可得到闭式解，采样时能同时保证多样性、合理性和可行性，并通过倾斜参数显式控制邻近性和稀疏性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有算法追索方法将问题建模为优化问题，仅构造一个或一小部分反事实，而非建模底层可行解空间，实际应用中往往在多样性、合理性或可行性之间做出牺牲，难以满足不同个体对多条可行路径的需求。

Method: 提出概率框架，将追索问题转化为在有利结果上的概率分布建模。对于基于邻近性和特征变化次数的常用代价函数，证明该分布可通过指数倾斜（exponential tilting）得到概率电路的精确表示；每个个体的分布以闭式形式可用而无需重训模型。从分布中采样自然产生多样且合理的追索方案，倾斜参数提供对邻近性和稀疏性的显式控制。

Result: 在标准算法追索基准数据集上的实验表明，该框架能同时实现多样性、合理性和可行性，并保留足够的目标反事实概率质量使拒绝采样具有实用性；MNIST上的可视化研究展示倾斜强度如何权衡邻近性与有效性。

Conclusion: 通过将追索空间建模为可处理的概率分布，而非离散优化目标，该框架克服了现有方法在多样性与可行性之间的权衡，提供了一种既具理论保障又实践可行的追索生成方案。

Abstract: Algorithmic recourse seeks to help individuals reverse unfavorable automated decisions by recommending actionable changes that achieve a desired outcome. As an individual usually has several distinct routes to a favorable decision, and different people can act on different ones, a recourse system should offer multiple realistic alternatives rather than one. Existing approaches formulate recourse as an optimization problem that constructs one or a small set of counterfactuals rather than modeling the underlying space of feasible solutions, and in practice each sacrifices diversity, plausibility, or feasibility to secure the others. We propose Tractable Recourse Distributions, a probabilistic framework that represents the space of feasible alternatives for a given factual instance as a probability distribution over favorable outcomes. For commonly used cost functions based on proximity and the number of feature changes, we show that this distribution admits an exact representation as a probabilistic circuit, obtained by exponentially tilting the circuit; each individual's distribution is therefore available in closed form, without retraining the model. Sampling from these distributions naturally produces diverse and plausible recourses, while the tilting parameters provide explicit control over their proximity and sparsity. Experiments on standard algorithmic recourse benchmark datasets demonstrate that the proposed framework attains diversity, plausibility, and feasibility simultaneously, while retaining sufficient probability mass over feasible counterfactuals for rejection sampling to be practical. A visual study on MNIST illustrates how the tilt strength trades proximity against validity.

</details>


### [269] [The Sample Complexity of Distributionally Robust PAC Learning under Cressie--Read Divergences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04686)
*Elad Aigner-Horev,Daniel Rosenberg,Roi Weiss*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了Cressie--Read散度约束下的分布鲁棒PAC学习，建立了0-1损失在可实现和不可知场景下的样本复杂度界，紧密到常数和对数因子，并揭示了鲁棒性引起的ε依赖指数变化规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布鲁棒优化是近年来机器学习的重要方向，但针对0-1损失、以Cressie--Read散度族（推广了先前仅研究的χ²散度情形）为扰动态约束的PAC学习样本复杂度尚未被系统研究，且已有边界在ρ→0极限下的插值行为不正确。

Method: 基于鲁棒0-1风险到普通分类误差的标量约化，作者分析分类误差统计估计与其被鲁棒性放大之间的尺度敏感相互作用，从而解释不可知情形下速率的转变。对VC维度为d的假设类，分别推导现实可学习和不可知学习的样本复杂度上界，并通过经验风险最小化实现。

Result: 可实现情形下样本复杂度阶为max{1/ε, ρ^{1/(k-1)}/ε^{k*}}·(d+log δ^{-1})，不可知情形下为max{1/ε², ρ^{1/(k-1)}/ε^{k*∨2}}·(d+log δ^{-1})，其中k*=k/(k-1)。固定ρ>0时，可实现情形ε依赖从ε^{-1}变为ε^{-k*}；不可知情形下，1<k<2时从ε^{-2}变为ε^{-k*}，k≥2时保持经典指数2但具有非平凡的ρ依赖。

Conclusion: 本文将先前χ²散度特例推广到所有Cressie--Read阶k>1，闭合了上下界间隙，并在ρ→0时正确恢复到标准PAC学习速率。揭示了统计估计与鲁棒放大之间的尺度敏感交互是速率转变的根本原因。

Abstract: We study distributionally robust PAC learning for the $0$--$1$-loss, where adversarial perturbations of the data distribution are constrained by a Cressie--Read divergence of order $k>1$ and radius $ρ\geq 0$. For hypothesis classes with VC dimension $d$, we establish realizable and agnostic sample-complexity bounds tight up to constant and logarithmic factors, respectively; ordinary empirical risk minimization attains both rates up to logarithmic factors. For target accuracy $\varepsilon\in(0,1)$ and confidence $δ\in(0,1)$, their respective orders are \[ \max\!\left\{\frac{1}{\varepsilon}, \frac{ρ^{\frac 1{k-1}}}{\varepsilon^{k_\star}} \right\}\cdot(d+\log δ^{-1}) \qquad\text{and}\qquad \max\!\left\{\frac{1}{\varepsilon^2}, \frac{ρ^{\frac1{k-1}}}{\varepsilon^{k_\star\vee 2}} \right\}\cdot(d+\log δ^{-1}), \] where $k_\star={k}/{(k-1)}$. For every fixed $ρ>0$, robustness changes the realizable $\varepsilon$-dependence from $\varepsilon^{-1}$ to $\varepsilon^{-k_\star}$ as $\varepsilon\downarrow0$. In the agnostic case, for $1<k<2$, robustness changes the $\varepsilon$-dependence from $\varepsilon^{-2}$ to $\varepsilon^{-k_\star}$, whereas for $k\geq2$ the exponent remains the classical $2$, with nontrivial $ρ$-dependence.
  Building on the known scalar reduction of robust $0$--$1$ risk to ordinary classification error, our analysis reveals a scale-sensitive interaction between the statistical estimation of classification error and its amplification by robustness, sharply explaining the transition in the agnostic rate. We extend the previously studied $χ^2$-divergence case to every Cressie--Read order $k>1$, close its upper--lower gaps, and recover standard PAC learning rates as $ρ\to0$, unlike previous bounds that fail to interpolate correctly in this limit.

</details>


### [270] [Personalized Federated Sparse Adaptation of Time-Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04695)
*Priyanka Nihalchandani,Naman Srivastava,Varun Ojha,Pandarasamy Arjunan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种个性化联邦稀疏自适应框架，在预训练时间序列基础模型(TSFM)之后引入异构时间混合专家(MoE)适配器，通过序列级路由将168小时上下文窗口映射到针对不同时间特征的专家子集，在50栋建筑和三种TSFM骨干上验证了个性化联邦学习优于全局联邦和纯本地训练方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑能耗预测中，仪表数据具有隐私性、分布性和高度非IID特性，TSFM联邦自适应十分必要。但单一参数共享策略难以兼顾所有预训练TSFM或建筑客户端：完全共享适配器会抑制建筑特有的时间行为，而完全本地自适应则丢弃跨建筑迁移信息，需要一种既能保护隐私又能应对异构性的自适应方案。

Method: 提出个性化联邦稀疏自适应框架，核心是在预训练TSFM表示之后放置异构时间混合专家(MoE)适配器。采用序列级路由器将每个168小时上下文窗口映射到top-k专家子集，这些专家分别专精于周期性、长程交互、局部变化、趋势残差结构和多分辨率行为。对比了全局FL、本地训练和个性化FL三种变体，并考察了全局共享或客户端私有专家库两种设置。

Result: 在50栋建筑和三种TSFM骨干上的实验表明：个性化联邦学习一致优于全局FL-MoE和本地MoE；最佳稀疏自适应策略因骨干网络和评估指标而异；路由行为揭示了客户端级专家专业化、专家集中以及跨骨干的近均匀路由现象。

Conclusion: 联邦TSFM自适应应同时考虑客户端感知和骨干感知。动态路由的MoE适配器能够兼顾跨建筑迁移与建筑特异性，而适配策略的选择需依据具体TSFM骨干和目标任务指标进行定制。

Abstract: Federated adaptation of time-series foundation models (TSFMs) is attractive for building energy forecasting because meter data are private, distributed, and highly non-IID. However, a single parameter-sharing strategy is unlikely to serve all pretrained TSFMs or building clients: fully shared adapters can suppress building-specific temporal behavior, while fully local adaptation discards cross-building transfer. We propose a personalized federated sparse adaptation framework with a heterogeneous temporal mixture-of-experts (MoE) adapter placed after the pretrained TSFM representation. A sequence-level router maps each 168-hour context window to a top-$k$ subset of experts specialized for periodicity, long-range interactions, local variation, trend-residual structure, and multi-resolution behavior. We compare global FL, local training, and personalized FL variants with globally shared or client-private expert banks. Across 50 buildings and three TSFM backbones, personalization consistently outperforms Global FL-MoE and Local MoE, while the best sparse-adaptation strategy varies by backbone and metric. Routing behavior further reveals client-level expert specialization, expert concentration, and near-uniform routing across backbones, showing that federated TSFM adaptation should be both client-aware and backbone-aware.

</details>


### [271] [Design Choices That Matter: A Functional ANOVA Analysis for Remote Sensing Multi-Label Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04702)
*Maryam Gholami Shiri,Eva Tuba,Sašo Džeroski,Tome Eftimov,Ana Nikolikj*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究采用功能方差分析（fANOVA）系统量化深度学习多标签遥感图像分类中各设计选择（网络架构、微调策略、学习策略、初始化）及其交互对性能变异的贡献，超越简单的基准排名。通过对48和20个DL模型在7个数据集上的分析，构建数据集元表征并揭示数据集按设计选择敏感性自然分组，且与数据集固有属性（尺度、空间分辨率、标签空间复杂度）紧密相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的深度学习模型在遥感图像多标签分类中的基准排名无法跨数据集泛化，缺乏对设计选择如何影响性能的系统性理解。作者希望超越排名，深入量化各设计维度对性能变异的具体贡献，从而提供更具可迁移性的洞见。

Method: 采用功能方差分析（fANOVA）作为核心分析框架，对48个和20个DL模型进行两组实证分析，跨越网络架构、微调策略、学习策略和初始化等设计维度。在七个MLC RSI数据集上应用fANOVA，构建反映设计选择敏感性的数据集元表征，并借助层次聚类分析数据集的自然分组模式。

Result: 结果显示：在大型数据集中，微调策略和网络架构是主导因素；在数据受限场景下，初始化起决定性作用；在中间规模场景，架构与学习策略的交互主导性能。层次聚类揭示数据集按对设计决策的响应方式自然分组，且与数据集固有属性（尺度、空间分辨率、标签空间复杂度）强相关。

Conclusion: fANOVA框架提供了超越传统基准排名的系统性视角，揭示了设计选择对MLC RSI性能的贡献取决于数据集规模及其固有属性。这一发现为研究者根据数据集特性选择合适的设计配置提供了可操作的指导。

Abstract: Benchmarking deep learning (DL) models for multi-label classification (MLC) of remote sensing images (RSI) typically yields rankings that do not generalize beyond the evaluated datasets. In this work, we move beyond rankings by employing functional analysis of variance (fANOVA) to systematically quantify the contributions of individual design choices and their interactions to performance variability. We conduct two empirical analyses covering 48 and 20 DL models, respectively, spanning design choices such as network architecture, fine-tuning strategy, learning strategy, and initialization. By applying fANOVA across seven MLC RSI datasets, we construct dataset meta-representations that capture design-choice sensitivity profiles. Hierarchical clustering of these meta-representations reveals that datasets naturally group according to how they respond to design decisions, with patterns strongly linked to intrinsic dataset properties such as scale, spatial resolution, and label space complexity. Our findings show that for large-scale datasets, fine-tuning strategy and architecture are dominant factors, while in data-limited regimes, initialization becomes decisive. For intermediate regimes, the interaction between architecture and learning strategy governs performance.

</details>


### [272] [Benchmarking Deep Learning Models for Dense Event Classification of Offshore Wind Infrastructure in Sentinel-1 Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04706)
*Thorsten Hoeser,Felix Bachofer,Claudia Kuenzer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对基于Sentinel-1 SAR数据的海上风电基础设施时间序列密集分类，系统比较了十种深度学习模型训练变体（LSTM、Transformer和全连接模型，结合单时相、单向与双向上下文感知，各有/无自监督预训练）。监督BiLSTM表现最佳，将AUC从规则基线的0.7853提升至0.8509，完美匹配率从0.3508提升至0.5063；与基线标签结合形成最小化标签转移的集成后进一步提升一致性。基于改进标签在全球尺度上隔离单台涡轮机的部署阶段，进行区域与子区域分析（2016-2025），报告了中国（84天）、欧盟（242天）和英国（258天）的中位部署时长，并识别出补贴等法律规管和環境條件等部署驱动因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海上风电基础设施的生命周期监测，尤其是在部署阶段，对利益相关方在部署活动日益增加的背景下做出知情决策至关重要。ESA的Sentinel-1 SAR任务产生海量数据档案，可支持全球海上风电基础设施的监测，但将高容量档案转化为信息需要在全球尺度上从密集时间序列中自动提取单一事件标签的算法，现有规则法分类精度不足，亟需更先进的深度学习方法。

Method: 研究提出了十种深度学习模型训练变体的结构化比较，涵盖LSTM、Transformer和全连接模型三种架构，每种架构分别配置单调时相、单向和双向上下文感知，且各有无自监督预训练的版本。模型用于Sentinel-1海上风电基础设施时间序列的密集分类。此外，将表现最佳的BiLSTM预测与现有规则基线标签通过标签转移最小化集成策略相结合，进一步优化分类结果，并基于改进后的标签在全球尺度上隔离各涡轮机的部署阶段进行区域和子区域分析。

Result: 监督BiLSTM在所有变体中表现最佳，将目标AUC从规则基线的0.7853提升至0.8509，完美匹配率从0.3508提升至0.5063。BiLSTM预测与基线标签的标签转移最小化集成进一步改善与测试数据的一致性。区域分析显示中位部署时长为中国84天、欧盟242天、英国258天。分析揭示了补贴等法律规管政策和環境條件等部署驱动因素在多个空间尺度上清晰显现。

Conclusion: 深度学习方法，特别是带双向上下文感知的监督BiLSTM模型，能显著超越基于规则的事件分类基线，实现对Sentinel-1海上风电基础设施时间序列更准确的自动事件标签提取。通过集成策略结合深度学习预测与规则基线可进一步提升性能，从而支持全球尺度上部署阶段的监测和驱动因素分析。

Abstract: Monitoring of offshore wind energy infrastructure life cycles, especially during the deployment phase, is an important contribution for stakeholders to make informed decisions in a phase of increasing deployment activities. ESA's Sentinel-1 Synthetic Aperture Radar (SAR) mission produces large data archives that enable the global monitoring of offshore wind infrastructure. Turning these high-volume archives into information requires algorithms that automatically extract single event labels from dense time series at a global scale. In this study, we present a structured comparison of ten deep learning model-training variants for the dense classification of Sentinel-1 based offshore wind infrastructure time series, aiming to advance rule-based event classification of this task. We trained LSTM, Transformer, and fully connected model variants with monotemporal, unidirectional, and bidirectional context awareness, each with and without self-supervised pretraining. Among these, the supervised BiLSTM performs best, raising the target AUC score from 0.7853 for the rule-based baseline to 0.8509, and the perfect match rate from 0.3508 to 0.5063. Combining the BiLSTM predictions with the existing baseline labels in a label-transition-minimising ensemble further improves agreement with the test data. Using these improved labels, we isolate the deployment phase of individual turbines at a global scale and conduct a regional and subregional analysis covering 2016-01-01 to 2025-03-31, reporting median deployment durations of 84 d (China), 242 d (EU), and 258 d (UK). Deployment-related drivers, including legal regulations such as subsidies, and environmental conditions, emerge clearly from the analysed results across multiple spatial scales.

</details>


### [273] [A 6G Integrated Sensing and Communication Framework for Railway Intrusion Detection and Collision Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04710)
*Ajeet Kumar Yadav,Sankaran Balasubramaniam,Aritra Chatterjee,Vinod Aduru,Yogesh Simmhan,Pandarasamy Arjunan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对ISAC铁路入侵检测用例，利用3D渲染铁路环境与Sionna仿真器生成22,695个CSI矩阵，构建了3D CNN与BiLSTM结合的深度学习模型，在合成数据上实现了99.57%的入侵检测准确率以及0.4240的联合MAE，证明了基于CSI的ISAC感知在铁路入侵检测中的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ISAC作为下一代无线网络的关键范式，能够利用CSI实现物理层感知。3GPP Release 19识别了32个ISAC用例，其中铁路入侵检测（包括野生动物进入轨道）具有重要的安全价值。该工作旨在利用CSI和机器学习实现可靠的铁路入侵检测，解决传统感知手段的局限性。

Method: 使用3D渲染的铁路环境和Sionna无线仿真器生成22,695个CSI矩阵及对应标注数据；构建了3D CNN与BiLSTM的混合深度学习模型，用于检测轨道危险区内的入侵者，并实时估计其相对列车的位置、速度及碰撞时间。

Result: 在合成CSI数据上，模型在平衡测试集上达到99.57%的入侵检测准确率；位置、速度和碰撞时间预测的联合MAE为0.4240。代码已在GitHub开源。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，基于CSI的ISAC感知结合机器学习能够为铁路入侵检测提供可靠的解决方案，展示了该技术在下一代无线网络中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Integrated Sensing and Communication (ISAC) combines sensing and communication to efficiently utilize wireless resources and is emerging as a key paradigm for next-generation wireless networks. By leveraging the wide bandwidth, high frequencies, and massive antenna arrays of 5G-Advanced and 6G systems, ISAC enables physical-layer sensing using Channel State Information (CSI). The 3rd Generation Partnership Project (3GPP) Release 19 identifies 32 potential ISAC use cases, with particular emphasis on detecting and tracking moving objects. In this work, we address the Sensing for Railway Intrusion Detection use case, where intruders, including wildlife, entering a railway track can pose serious collision risks. We generated 22,695 CSI matrices with corresponding ground truth using a 3D-rendered railway environment and the Sionna radio simulator. We developed a machine learning model combining a three-dimensional Convolutional Neural Network (3D CNN) and Bidirectional Long Short-Term Memory (BiLSTM) network to detect intruders in the track danger zone and estimate their real-time position relative to the train, velocity, and time to collision. On synthetic CSI data, the model achieves 99.57% intruder-detection accuracy on a balanced test set and a combined Mean Absolute Error (MAE) of 0.4240 for position, velocity, and time-to-collision prediction. These results demonstrate the potential of CSI-based ISAC sensing with machine learning for reliable railway intrusion detection. The complete codebase for CSI generation, preprocessing, and model development is publicly available at https://github.com/EdgeIntelligenceLab/6g-isac-railway-intrusion-detection.

</details>


### [274] [Attention, Anomalies! Handling Attention Layers in Unsupervised Federated Outlier Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04753)
*Mihailo Ilić,Miloš Savić,Vladimir Kurbalija,Mirjana Ivanović,Giancarlo Fortino,Dušan Jakovetić*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对联邦学习（FL）场景下记忆增强自编码器（Memory Augmented Autoencoders, MemAE）的聚合问题，提出了新颖的引导式聚合方法，在非独立同分布（non-IID）数据集上验证了其对异常检测任务的鲁棒性，即使在资源受限环境下也能提升浅层自编码器的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 注意力机制是当今最强大模型的核心组件，其在记忆增强自编码器（MemAE）中表现出色，尤其在集中式学习的无监督异常检测任务中。然而，联邦学习（FL）场景下缺乏针对MemAE模型的专门聚合技术，这阻碍了其在分布式、资源受限环境中的应用。

Method: 本文首先深入分析记忆增强自编码器的架构特性，然后提出新颖的引导式（guided）联邦聚合方法，针对MemAE的注意力机制和记忆模块设计专门的模型聚合策略，以应对联邦学习中数据集不平衡和非IID分布带来的挑战。

Result: 在非IID数据集上的实验表明，所提出的新颖聚合方案在处理大量边缘节点和不均衡数据集时具有更强的鲁棒性，尤其在无监督异常检测场景中表现优异。该方法显著提升了浅层自编码器的性能，使其能够在资源受限环境中实际应用。

Conclusion: 本文填补了联邦学习中MemAE模型缺乏专门聚合策略的空白，通过引导式聚合方法使得注意力增强的异常检测模型在分布式环境下也能保持良好性能，为资源受限的联邦学习异常检测部署提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Attention layers are the backbone of today's most powerful and impactful models. Models with multi-million and billion parameters rely on contextual knowledge provided by attention layers. However, their use goes well beyond just being the core component of large language models. One particularly interesting application is in Memory Augmented Autoencoders (MemAE), specifically for unsupervised representation learning in outlier detection tasks. It was shown that attention helps these models be more effective in centralized learning scenarios. Our work aims to address the lack of specialized aggregation techniques in Federated Learning (FL) when it comes to MemAE models. In this paper we analyze the intricacies of the architecture behind Memory Augmented Autoencoders, and propose novel, guided approaches to effectively aggregate these models in federated scenarios. We demonstrate our approach on non-IID datasets and show that these novel aggregation schemes are more robust when dealing with numerous edge nodes in environments with unbalanced datasets, specifically for unsupervised anomaly detection scenarios. This approach improves the performance of even very shallow autoencoders, allowing them to be used in resource constrained environments.

</details>


### [275] [IMFACT: Counterfactual Explanations for Time Series via Intrinsic Mode Function Substitution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04777)
*Udo Schlegel,Julian Rakuschek,Thomas Seidl,Andreas Holzinger,Tobias Schreck,Javier Del Ser*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出IMFACT框架，在经验模态分解空间中生成反事实解释，通过逐步替换IMF分量直至分类器翻转，在UCR基准上优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 振荡信号（如振动）的类别判别信息集中于特定频段，直接在原始特征空间中扰动会破坏其时序结构并产生物理上不合理的反事实结果。

Method: 采用经验模态分解将输入信号分解为内蕴模态函数（IMFs），选择性地将IMF替换为最近异类邻居（NUN）的对应IMF，迭代直至分类器翻转到目标类别。提出了六种IMF选择策略以及多NUN循环扩展。

Result: 在两个UCR基准数据集（FaultDetectionA、FruitFlies）上，基于方差的策略配合三个NUN在可靠性和合理性指标上优于两种基线方法；在接近度指标上，三个NUN循环策略在两个数据集上均取得最佳表现。

Conclusion: IMFACT通过在分解空间而非原始特征空间进行反事实扰动，能够在保持物理合理性的同时生成高质量的时间序列分类反事实解释。

Abstract: Oscillatory signals, such as vibration, carry class-discriminative information in specific frequency bands; perturbing them in raw feature space for counterfactual analysis easily destroys their temporal structure and produces physically implausible results. In this work, we introduce IMFACT (IMF-based counterfACTuals), a model-agnostic framework for generating plausible counterfactual explanations for time series classifiers that operates in the decomposition space of Empirical Mode Decomposition. An input signal is split into Intrinsic Mode Functions (IMFs), and selected IMFs are progressively substituted with those of a Nearest Unlike Neighbour (NUN) until the classifier flips to the target class. We evaluate six IMF-selection strategies and a multi-NUN cycling extension on two UCR benchmarks (FaultDetectionA, FruitFlies). The variance-based strategy with three NUNs outperforms two prominent baseline techniques on reliability and plausibility metrics, while cycling across three NUNs yields the best proximity across both datasets.

</details>


### [276] [Variational Bounds for Perceptron Learning from Structured Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04882)
*Francesco Camilli,Pierluigi Contucci,Federica Gerace,Emanuele Mingione*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对有限温度连续自旋感知机（在Gaussian mixture数据上训练）的变分方法。通过将插值方法与对数凹性和集中估计相结合，推导出极限淬火压力的上下极小极大变分界限。两个界限仅在两个变分参数的优化顺序上不同，当两者可交换时，界限匹配并识别出模型的解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该论文的核心动机是为有限温度下的连续自旋感知机模型提供一个统一的变分分析框架。该模型允许广泛的凹效用函数和对数凹可分离先验测度，此前缺乏系统的理论分析工具来刻画其淬火压力、基态能量、训练损失和泛化误差等关键量。

Method: 作者将插值方法（interpolation method）与对数凹性（log-concavity）以及集中不等式（concentration estimates）相结合。具体而言：1）推导淬火压力的上下极小极大变分界限；2）通过变分势的凹-凸结构控制所有剩余极值；3）当两个变分参数的优化可交换时，界限匹配得到精确解；4）同一变分势同时给出定点方程作为驻点条件，并提供计算基态能量、训练损失和泛化误差的统一路径。

Result: 得到的上下界限仅在两个变分参数的优化顺序上存在差异，而其余极值均受控于变分势的凹-凸结构。当两个优化交换时，上下界匹配并唯一确定模型的解。该变分势同时产生定点方程作为驻点条件，为基态能量、训练损失和泛化误差的计算提供了统一方法。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一个统一的变分框架来分析有限温度连续自旋感知机模型，其上下界的结构对称性简洁而优美。该方法不仅解决了当前模型的理论刻画问题，还为更广泛的神经损失模型（如Gaussian mixture下的感知机）的统计力学分析提供了通用工具。

Abstract: We introduce a variational approach to a finite-temperature continuous-spin perceptron trained on a Gaussian mixture. The model allows for a broad class of concave utilities and log-concave separable prior measures on the spins. By combining the interpolation method with log-concavity and concentration estimates, we derive lower and upper minimax variational bounds for the limiting quenched pressure. Remarkably, the two bounds differ only in the order of optimization of two variational parameters, while all remaining extrema are controlled by the concave--convex structure of the variational potential. Whenever the two optimizations commute, the two bounds match and identify the solution of the model. The same potential yields the fixed-point equations as stationarity conditions and provides a unified route to the computation of the ground-state energy, training loss, and generalization error.

</details>


### [277] [Continual-Learning Physics-Informed Neural Networks for Parameterized Partial Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04778)
*Xujia Chen,Xinyue Hu,Letian Chen,Yi Liu,Wenhui Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CL-PINN（持续学习物理信息神经网络），通过将不同参数下的PDE实例视为连续相关任务进行顺序学习，结合贝叶斯优化主动参数选择、任务级动态损失加权、稀疏物理约束回放及可选参数子网络，在无观测数据条件下高效求解参数化PDE，实现跨参数的准确与平衡泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ParamPINN存在三个关键问题：训练效率低、不同参数间精度不均衡、对有限采样参数任务过拟合导致无法泛化到未采样参数。这些问题限制了参数化PDE求解器的实用性与可扩展性。

Method: CL-PINN将不同参数值的PDE实例视为相关任务并按顺序学习，核心组件包括：基于贝叶斯优化的主动参数选择策略、任务级动态损失权重调整、稀疏物理约束回放机制，以及可选的参数子网络。该方法在有限活动任务容量下优化任务分配与知识保留，无需任何观测数据。

Result: 在五个基准（包括一个连续函数和四个参数化PDE）上的多种子评估表明：贝叶斯选择相比网格贪心搜索显著减少目标函数查询次数；稀疏回放有效缓解对早期任务的遗忘；在预设的计算资源协议下，CL-PINN比固定采样和网格贪心基线提供更高且更均衡的求解精度。

Conclusion: CL-PINN为在有限计算资源下学习跨物理参数泛化的PDE解提供了一条实用路径，并有望支持大规模工程参数研究中可复用的物理信息代理模型。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) incorporate governing equations into neural-network training and can approximate PDE solutions without requiring large observational datasets. Parameterized PINNs (ParamPINNs) further take physical parameters as inputs, allowing a single model to represent a family of PDE solutions over a parameter domain. Existing ParamPINNs, however, still face inefficient training, uneven accuracy across parameters, and overfitting to a limited set of sampled parameter tasks, which can impair generalization to unsampled parameters. To address these issues, we propose a continual-learning physics-informed neural network (CL-PINN), which treats PDE instances at different parameter values as related tasks and learns them sequentially. CL-PINN combines Bayesian-optimization-based active parameter selection, task-wise dynamic loss weighting, sparse physics-constrained replay, and an optional parameter subnetwork to improve task allocation and knowledge retention under bounded active-task capacity. It requires no observational data and is designed to solve parameterized PDEs over relatively broad parameter domains under limited computational resources. Multi-seed evaluations on five benchmarks, including one continuous function and four parameterized PDEs, show that Bayesian selection substantially reduces objective-loss queries relative to grid-greedy search, while sparse replay mitigates forgetting of earlier tasks. Under the prescribed within-case resource protocols, CL-PINN generally provides higher and more balanced solution accuracy than fixed-sampling and grid-greedy baselines. CL-PINN offers a practical route toward learning PDE solutions that generalize across physical parameters and has the potential to support reusable physics-informed surrogates for large-scale engineering parameter studies.

</details>


### [278] [Stochastic Emulation using Generalized Stratified Sampling for Performance-Based Risk Optimization of Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05006)
*Isabela D. Rodrigues,Seymour M. J. Spence,Henrique M. Kroetz,André T. Beck*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将广义分层抽样（GSS）与随机多项式混沌展开（SPCE）相结合的新框架（GSS-SPCE），用于结构在随机荷载下的基于性能风险优化（PBRO）。该框架通过将输入空间按灾害强度分层，并在每层独立训练SPCE仿真器，改善了极端响应（尾部）的表示精度，再通过全概率定理重组各层的条件超越概率来评估概率约束。在一栋两层钢建筑的屈曲约束支撑截面优化设计中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 元模型（metamodel）对减少嵌套可靠度分析和优化循环的计算负担至关重要。SPCE虽不需重复非线性分析，但在准确表示结构响应分布尾部（极端响应）方面存在局限，而尾部行为对评估概率性能约束至关重要。

Method: 提出GSS-SPCE框架：1）GSS方案根据灾害强度将输入空间划分为多个层（strata）；2）在每层内独立训练SPCE仿真器；3）利用全概率定理将各层估计的条件超越概率重组，以评估概率约束。应用于两层钢建筑中屈曲约束支撑截面积的优化设计，目标为最小化初始造价并满足规定的概率性能约束。

Result: 结果表明，所提出的框架能够准确估计结构响应分布（包括尾部区域），同时大幅减少PBRO所需的非线性模型评估次数。

Conclusion: GSS-SPCE框架有效克服了SPCE在尾部响应表示上的不足，在保障精度前提下显著提升PBRO的计算效率，为结构在随机荷载下的概率约束优化提供了一种高效且准确的替代方案。

Abstract: Metamodels are instrumental in reducing the computational burden associated with nested reliability analyses and optimization loops in Performance-Based Risk Optimization (PBRO) of structures under stochastic loads. In this context, stochastic emulators are particularly useful because they approximate response distributions while accounting for the intrinsic stochasticity of the simulator. Among these methods, Stochastic Polynomial Chaos Expansion (SPCE) is especially attractive because it does not require replications of nonlinear analyses at fixed input conditions. However, SPCE may present limitations in accurately representing extreme responses in the tails of structural response distributions. To address this limitation, this study proposes a framework that combines Generalized Stratified Sampling (GSS) with SPCE. The GSS scheme partitions the input space into strata according to the intensity of the hazard, improving the representation of extreme responses, while independent SPCE emulators are trained within each stratum. The conditional exceedance probabilities estimated in each stratum are then recombined using the total probability theorem to evaluate the probabilistic constraints. The proposed GSS-SPCE framework is applied to the optimal design of buckling-restrained brace cross-sectional areas in a two-story steel building. The objective is to minimize the initial construction cost while satisfying prescribed probabilistic performance constraints. Results show that the proposed framework accurately estimates structural response distributions, including their tail regions, while substantially reducing the number of nonlinear model evaluations required for PBRO.

</details>


### [279] [Agentic Reinforcement Learning with Observation-Calibrated Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04788)
*Yi Yang,Cong Qin,Xiaodan Liu,Chishui Chen,Qing Dong,Yan Zhang,Cao Liu,Zhao Yang,Lu Pan,Jiaye Lin,Yi Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Observation-Calibrated Self-Distillation (OCSD)，通过对两个仅在未来观测上有差异的结构匹配重放视图（完整与观测消融）进行对比，计算观测残差来消除重放脚手架引起的分数偏移，从而获得纯净的基于观测信息的token级稠密奖励，用于改进基于LLM智能体的强化学习训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型智能体通过稀疏轨迹级奖励进行强化学习训练，难以准确衡量每个token的更新强度。On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD) 通过基于特权重放视图重新打分生成token以获得稠密token级监督，但其生成的分数同时包含重放视图中的特权信息和重放脚手架本身引起的分数偏移，两者混淆难以归因。特别是当未来环境观测作为特权信息时，重建扩展脚手架会进一步扰动token分数，使问题更加严重。

Method: OCSD通过对比两个结构匹配的重放视图——完整视图（Full）和观测消融视图（Observation-Ablated），二者仅在是否包含实际未来观测上有差异。通过取两者分数的差异得到观测残差，该残差能够抵消重放脚手架共享的分数变化，从而单独归因于未来观测信息。OCSD将这一残差应用于高不确定度步骤的token级GRPO更新调制，同时保留轨迹级更新方向。

Result: 在ALFWorld、WebShop和Search-QA三个基准上，跨三种Qwen3模型规模进行实验，OCSD一致性地优于强基线方法。诊断分析进一步证实校准后的残差与局部环境反馈更吻合。

Conclusion: OCSD通过对比式残差校准有效解耦了特权观测信息与重放脚手架效应，提供更准确、可归因的稠密token级监督信号，显著提升LLM智能体在多种任务和环境中的强化学习训练效果。

Abstract: Large language model agents are commonly trained through reinforcement learning with sparse trajectory-level rewards, which offer limited guidance on how strongly individual tokens should be updated. On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD) addresses this by re-scoring generated tokens under a privileged replay view to obtain dense, token-level supervision. However, we identify a confounding issue: the resulting support may reflect both the privileged information contained in the replay view and score shifts induced by the replay scaffold, making it difficult to attribute the support specifically to that information. This issue is especially pronounced when future environment observations serve as privileged information, since replaying them requires reconstructing an extended scaffold that itself perturbs token scores. To resolve this confounding, we propose Observation-Calibrated Self-Distillation (OCSD), which contrasts two structurally matched replay views, Full and Observation-Ablated, differing only in whether the actual future observation is present, to derive an observation residual that discounts score changes shared by the replay scaffold. OCSD then applies this residual to modulate token-level GRPO updates at high-uncertainty steps, while preserving the trajectory-level update direction. Experiments on ALFWorld, WebShop, and Search-QA across three Qwen3 model scales show that OCSD consistently outperforms strong baselines. Diagnostic analyses further confirm that the calibrated residual aligns better with local environment feedback. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/yiy1x/OCSD.

</details>


### [280] [Canonical Joint Energy-Based Model on CIFAR-10: failure modes and practical indistinguishability of Predictor-Corrector and SGLD samplers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05025)
*Dmytro Knopov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统复现了经典联合能量模型（JEM）在WideResNet-28-10上的训练，并检验了理论上更优的Predictor-Corrector（PC）采样器是否在无退火噪声调度下保留其优势。结果显示PC与SGLD在所有协议上均无方法级差异（AUROC差异<0.007，FID差异<0.5），同时记录了两个失败模式：训练后期灾难性发散和依赖运行的SVHN OOD判别动态。PC在固定噪声下预测步退化为恒等映射，因此其理论保证无法迁移到经典JEM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典JEM训练依赖SGLD采样器，而理论上更优的PC采样器此前未在经典模型上进行过系统性的复制测试。作者旨在检验PC在无退火噪声调度下是否仍能保持其理论优势，并探究JEM复现中潜在的不稳定因素。

Method: 在无归一化层的WideResNet-28-10上独立运行两次经典JEM训练。设计三种协议：(1) PC在整个约130个训练epoch中替代SGLD；(2) 冷启动生成（FID评估）；(3) 精炼式多OOD检测（AUROC评估）。使用分层种子-图像bootstrap对宏平均AUROC差异构建95%置信区间，并对两方法各两次运行进行种子级等价性检验。

Result: 重建达到92.88%测试准确率和44.46的buffer-FID（经典为92.9%和38.40）。记录了两种失败模式：通过经典outlier-buffer机制导致的训练后期灾难性发散（两次SGLD运行和两次PC运行均出现相同特征），以及依赖运行的SVHN OOD判别动态。所有协议上PC对SGLD均无方法级优势：推理时AUROC绝对差异在所有十个检查点-OOD对中保持在0.007以下，FID差异低于0.5。训练协议上bootstrap置信区间包含零，但种子级等价性检验无法确立正式等价性。

Conclusion: PC与SGLD在经典JEM上实际不可区分，这与理论预期一致：在固定噪声下，PC的预测步在构造上退化为恒等映射（degenerates by construction），因此其理论保证不能转移到经典JEM。数据同时与等价性和小的方向性效应两种解释相容。

Abstract: Joint Energy-Based Models (JEM) unify classification and generation within a single network and support out-of-distribution (OOD) detection. Canonical JEM training relies on stochastic gradient Langevin dynamics (SGLD); a theoretically motivated alternative, the Predictor-Corrector (PC) sampler, has not previously undergone a systematic replication test on the canonical model. We reproduce canonical JEM on WideResNet-28-10 without normalisation layers on two independent runs and test whether PC retains its theoretical advantage without an annealed noise schedule, across three protocols: PC replacing SGLD throughout the roughly 130 training epochs; cold-start generation (FID); and refinement-style multi-OOD detection (AUROC). The reconstruction reaches 92.88% test accuracy and buffer-FID 44.46 (canonical: 92.9% and 38.40). We document two failure modes: catastrophic late-training divergence via the canonical outlier-buffer mechanism (both SGLD runs and, with the same signature, both PC runs), and run-dependent SVHN OOD-discrimination dynamics. No method-level advantage of PC over SGLD is observed on any protocol: at inference the absolute AUROC difference stays below 0.007 across all ten checkpoint-OOD pairs and the FID difference below 0.5; on the training protocol a hierarchical seed-by-image bootstrap gives a 95% confidence interval on the macro-averaged AUROC difference that contains zero, while a seed-level equivalence test with two runs per method cannot establish formal equivalence. The data are consistent both with equivalence and with a small directional effect. This practical indistinguishability is theoretically expected: under fixed noise the PC predictor step degenerates by construction, so its guarantees do not transfer to canonical JEM.

</details>


### [281] [Above-ground Biomass Estimation with Geospatial Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04792)
*Ghjulia Sialellia,Linus Scheibenreif,Jan Dirk Wegner,Konrad Schindler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究系统评估了地理空间基础模型（GFMs）在全球尺度地上生物量（AGB）估算这一回归任务中的表现，使用AGBD基准数据集对比了11种GFM和两种预计算嵌入产品，发现以冻结编码器方式使用的GFM明显逊色于有监督SOTA模型，而预计算嵌入产品（尤其是AlphaEarth Foundations）则表现出色，训练于AEF嵌入的SOTA模型取得了最佳综合成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卫星图像精确估算地上生物量（AGB）对大规模碳储量监测至关重要，但全球尺度的回归任务仍具挑战性。地理空间基础模型（GFMs）在分类和分割任务上表现优异，但其在定量回归任务（如生物量估算）中的实用性尚未得到充分探索，大多数现有基准侧重于分类和分割，缺乏针对回归任务的系统评估。

Method: 使用AGBD数据集（一个覆盖多样生物群落和地理区域的机器学习基准），区分两类GFM交付方式：一类是以权重形式分发的模型，作为冻结编码器在PANGAEA基准框架中评估；另一类是预计算嵌入产品（AlphaEarth Foundations和TESSERA）。共比较11种PANGAEA上的GFM及两种嵌入产品，与全监督SOTA模型对比，评估其地理和时间泛化能力以及与ESA CCI生物量产品的一致性。

Result: 以冻结编码器运行的GFMs显著逊色于有监督SOTA模型；而预计算嵌入产品则表现高效。基于AEF嵌入训练的MLP即可超越在有AGBD特征上训练的监督SOTA模型；使用AEF嵌入（可选添加精选原始特征）训练的同一SOTA模型取得最佳整体结果，且在空间和时间上具有更好的泛化性能。

Conclusion: GFMs在AGB估算中的有效性高度依赖于其分发方式：预计算嵌入产品比以冻结编码器方式使用的GFM模型权重更具实用性。AEF嵌入产品为全球尺度的生物量估算提供了有竞争力的替代方案，有望在碳储量大规模监测中发挥重要作用。

Abstract: Accurate estimation of Above-Ground Biomass (AGB) from satellite imagery is essential for the large-scale monitoring of carbon stocks, yet it remains a challenging regression task at global scale. Geospatial Foundation Models (GFMs) have recently emerged as a promising machine learning paradigm to derive general-purpose representations from Earth observation data, but their utility for quantitative regression tasks like biomass estimation remains largely unexplored, as most benchmarks emphasize classification and segmentation. Here, we present a comprehensive benchmark of GFMs for global-scale AGB estimation using the AGBD dataset, a machine learning-ready benchmark spanning diverse biomes and geographies. We distinguish two ways in which GFMs reach practitioners: (i) models distributed as weights to be run by the user, which we evaluate as frozen encoders within the PANGAEA benchmarking framework; and (ii) models distributed as ready-to-use, pre-computed embedding products, for which we evaluate AlphaEarth Foundations (AEF) and TESSERA. We compare 11 GFMs available on PANGAEA and both embedding products against a fully supervised state-of-the-art (SOTA) model, assess their geographical and temporal generalization abilities, as well as agreement with the ESA CCI biomass product on independent reference data. Our results show that GFMs run as frozen encoders substantially underperform with respect to the supervised SOTA model, whereas pre-computed embedding products prove highly effective. An MLP trained on AEF embeddings outperforms the supervised SOTA model trained on AGBD features, and the same SOTA model trained on AEF embeddings (optionally augmented with selected raw features) achieves the best overall result, while also generalizing better across space and time.

</details>


### [282] [SSTQ:Privacy-Preserving Vector Quantization via Subsampled Stochastic TurboQuant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05127)
*Adel Javanmard,David P. Woodruff,Vahab Mirrokni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 SSTQ（子采样随机 TurboQuant）框架，结合超完全等范数紧框架、坐标子采样和隐私感知一维量化，在分布式优化中实现局部差分隐私的同时，仅需 ⌈log₂N⌉ + b 比特的通信开销，并达到最优均方误差缩放。包含 Flat Randomized Response 和 Metric-Aware Laplace 两个变体，在联邦学习任务（CIFAR-10、Fashion-MNIST）上展示了优越的效果与通信效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分布式优化中同时实现局部差分隐私和低通信成本仍然具有挑战性。现有的向量量化方法（如 vqSGD）依赖高维几何构造，但存在不利的维度相关方差，需要一种兼顾隐私保护与通信效率的新框架。

Method: SSTQ 框架结合三大要素：(1) 超完全等范数紧框架用于高维向量分解；(2) 坐标子采样降低计算和通信开销；(3) 隐私感知的一维量化实现差分隐私。框架包含两个变体：Flat Randomized Response 版本和 Metric-Aware Laplace 版本，后者在高码字位宽场景下表现更优。此外，论文还推导了一个隐私感知的码本目标函数，将码本相关的 MSE 缩放从 O(4^b) 降至 O(2^b)。

Result: SSTQ 达到了最优的均方误差缩放，同时仅需 ⌈log₂N⌉ + b 比特/客户端的通信量（其中 N = Θ(d) 为框架大小）。在 CIFAR-10 和 Fashion-MNIST 上的联邦学习实验中，SSTQ 相比既有基线方法在效用和通信效率方面均展现出优势。

Conclusion: SSTQ 成功地在局部差分隐私、通信效率和量化精度之间取得了良好的平衡，通过理论推导和实验验证，为分布式优化中的隐私保护通信方案提供了新的有效框架。

Abstract: Achieving local differential privacy in distributed optimization while maintaining low communication cost remains challenging. Existing vector quantization methods, such as vqSGD, use high-dimensional geometric constructions but incur unfavorable dimension-dependent variance. In this work, we propose Subsampled Stochastic TurboQuant (SSTQ), a framework that combines overcomplete equal-norm tight frames, coordinate subsampling, and privacy-aware one-dimensional quantization. SSTQ includes two variants: a Flat Randomized Response version and a Metric-Aware Laplace version, the latter being better suited to higher codebook bit-width regimes. We show that SSTQ achieves optimal mean squared error scaling while using only $\lceil \log_2 N \rceil + b$ bits per client, where $N = Θ(d)$ is the frame size. We also derive a surrogate privacy-aware codebook objective that reduces the codebook-dependent MSE scaling from $O(4^b)$ to $O(2^b)$. Finally, we empirically evaluate SSTQ against established baselines on federated learning tasks using CIFAR-10 and Fashion-MNIST, demonstrating favorable utility and communication efficiency.

</details>


### [283] [MGSB: Manifold Gated Signature Branch Pressure-Domain Baseline Architecture for Two-Phase Pipeline Flows Under Distributional Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04805)
*Issah Suleiman,Sormeh Serpoosh,Nadine Elkholy,Hicham Ferroudji,Mohammad Azizur Rahman,Matthew Hamilton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出流型感知的多相管道泄漏检测架构MGSB（Manifold Gated Signature Bias），通过流型条件特征融合、TT-RoughPath编码器和Mean-Teacher一致性正则化，在leave-one-group-out评估下实现检测F1=0.930，OOD F1=0.783，显著优于CNN-LSTM和全连接基线，证明了显式流型感知建模是提升工业多相管道泄漏检测鲁棒性的有效路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有泄漏检测模型在部署到与训练分布不同的流型（如从气泡流切换到段塞流）时性能严重退化，而现有的评估方法通常只在同分布条件下测试，掩盖了流型切换导致的失败。作者旨在解决这一分布偏移下的鲁棒性问题。

Method: 提出MGSB架构，包含三个核心组件：(1) 流型条件特征融合（regime-conditioned feature fusion），实现流型感知的特征处理；(2) TT-RoughPath（Tensor-Train RoughPath）编码器用于高效序列特征提取；(3) Mean-Teacher一致性正则化增强训练稳定性。评估采用leave-one-group-out策略以模拟真实分布偏移场景。

Result: MGSB在leave-one-group-out评估下达到检测F1为0.930，OOD F1为0.783，在严重特征损坏下显著优于CNN-LSTM和全连接基线。消融实验表明架构本身（而非训练流程）是OOD鲁棒性的主要贡献因素；Mahalanobis距离分析确认了留出条件确实属于分布外样本。

Conclusion: 显式的流型感知建模是提升工业多相管道泄漏检测鲁棒性的实用途径，MGSB为传感器无关的跨流型泄漏检测提供了可行方案，弥补了现有模型在分布偏移下性能退化的关键短板。

Abstract: Leak detection models for multiphase pipelines often degrade when deployed under flow regimes that differ from training. Existing evaluations typically assess performance under in-distribution operating conditions, masking failures caused by regime transitions such as bubble-to-slug flow. We propose the Manifold Gated Signature Bias (MGSB), a regime-aware architecture combining regime-conditioned feature fusion, a TT-RoughPath encoder, and Mean-Teacher consistency regularization to improve robustness under distribution shift. Under leave-one-group-out evaluation, MGSB achieves a detection F1 of 0.930 and an OOD F1 of 0.783, substantially outperforming CNN-LSTM and fully connected baselines under severe feature corruption. Ablations show the proposed architecture, not the training procedure, is the primary contributor to OOD robustness, while Mahalanobis-distance analysis confirms the held-out conditions are genuinely out-of-distribution. These results show that explicit regime-aware modelling is a practical path toward robust, sensor-agnostic leak detection in industrial multiphase pipelines.

</details>


### [284] [Robust Control under Stationary Ambiguity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04832)
*Konrad J. Mueller,Amira Akkari,Ben Wood,Lukas Gonon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"平稳模糊性"（stationary ambiguity）原则来训练策略：在模拟器中让潜在参数的不确定性随时间保持稳定而非衰减，从而使策略在真实系统中面对潜在因素漂移时保持持续鲁棒性。作者在期权对冲问题上验证了该方法，表明其在实际市场数据上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在仿真中优化的控制策略在实际系统中可能表现不佳，因为从有限数据估计的模拟器参数存在不确定性，而传统方法未在仿真中表示这种不确定性。虽然随机采样参数可以在仿真中引入模糊性，但策略会随着观测逐步推断出真实参数值，导致鲁棒性随时间衰减。然而在真实系统中（如金融市场中的波动率体制），潜在因素会持续漂移，因此策略需要保持持续鲁棒性。

Method: 提出训练策略于具有"平稳模糊性"的模拟器中：要求模拟器在潜在状态上诱导出一个平稳的滤波器过程，即模糊性随系统状态变化但不随时间系统性衰减。具体构造了满足该条件的模拟器，并在一系列对冲问题上训练策略，与传统的参数随机化方法进行对比。

Result: 在对冲问题的实验中，在平稳模糊性下训练的策略对潜在因素保持了持续的鲁棒性，在真实市场数据上表现出强劲性能，优于传统方法。

Conclusion: 平稳模糊性可以作为一种建模原则，指导模拟器的多种设计决策（如模型选择、参数随机化方式、模拟器与策略的初始化），适用于其他由外生随机过程驱动的、具有漂移潜在结构的序贯控制问题。

Abstract: Control policies optimized in simulation can perform poorly in the real system when the parameters $x$ of the simulator are estimated from limited data but the resulting parameter uncertainty is not represented inside the simulation. A common way to incorporate such ambiguity is to simulate each trajectory of the system under a randomly drawn value for $x$. Since the policy cannot observe the drawn value, it must initially choose controls that perform well across many possible parameter values. However, if the policy progressively observes the system, it can often gradually infer the value of $x$, so that ambiguity vanishes. Over time, the policy then specializes to its estimate of $x$ and loses its robustness. This is undesirable in many real systems, where latent factors are expected to shift. In financial markets, for example, a policy hedging a derivative payoff should remain robust to changes in the volatility regime.
